# OUTLINE_frontier — 第七部分「前沿进展」：Ch22 `lamet`、Ch23 `yzliu`、Ch24 `front`（spec v2 草案）

**Status.** Draft of the frontier part of `FINAL_OUTLINE_v2.md`, written on 2026-10-10 by the spec-v2 frontier drafter. It is merged by the spec-v2 synthesizer with `OUTLINE_core`, `OUTLINE_appMNOPQ` and `ASSUMPTIONS` into `FINAL_OUTLINE_v2.md`. Everything marked **[PENDING-VERIFY]** waits for the verification workflow (`verify/`, claim IDs of `verify/CLAIMS.md`); the marker and the planning phrase 「本书新结果（待文献核查）」 are never printed (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.12).

**Inputs read in full.** `CONTEXT.md`; `REQUIREMENTS.md`; `frontier/lamet/LAMET.md` (all 15 sections); `frontier/yzliu/YZLIU.md` (all 8 sections); `frontier/others/OTHERS.md` (§0–§14, App. A); `spec/FINAL_OUTLINE.md` (§0, Ch3, Ch4, Ch7, Ch13, Ch15–Ch17, Ch19–Ch21, App B, D, J, K, L, registries); `spec_v2/WRITER_SPEC_v2.md` (all); `spec_v2/VERIFIED_FORMULAS_v2.md` (header, groups LAM, YZL, FRO, pending register); `spec_v2/FIGURES_INDEX_v2.md` (§4.2–§4.4); `spec_v2/REFS_NOTES_v2.md` (§0, §1, §3.2–§3.4); `refdoc/REFDOC_ANALYSIS.md` §5 (S1–S12, S43–S45, S57–S63); `verify/CLAIMS.md` (index; E01–E16, E34–E36, N01–N11, N27–N29, A03–A10); `research/c_tmd/DERIVATION.md` §2.2, §3.4–§3.5, §8; `research/critic/REVIEW.md` §3.2 (C5, C6, C9, C10, C12), O11, O12, §7 (M2, M15–M22); `frontier/others/OTHERS.md` again in full for Ch24; the first-stage verification records `verify/claims/batch_E01_s1.md`, `batch_E07_s1.md`, `batch_E13_s1.md` (E01–E18; single confirmations — every claim stays [PENDING-VERIFY], their precision notes are applied in the entries and listed in §F6.4).

**Order of authority** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 preamble): binding lead-author decisions > WRITER_SPEC_v2 > this outline (as part of FINAL_OUTLINE_v2) > VERIFIED_FORMULAS_v2 > frontier reports. Where this outline deviates from a *default* of WRITER_SPEC_v2, the deviation is listed in §F0.2 with its reason; the synthesizer patches WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 5 / §12.2 and the "Used in" lines of VF-LAM-15/-16 accordingly (§F5).

**Citation keys.** All keys below are merged keys of `spec_v2/refs_merged_v2.bib` (checked by script against the bib and `key_aliases_v2.tsv`: 0 missing). Frontier texkeys that map to readable v1 keys are written in the readable form (e.g. `IzubuchiEtAl2018` = Izubuchi:2018srq, `JiEtAl2021` = the LaMET RMP Ji:2020ect, `Ji2013` = Ji:2013dva, `MaQiu2018` = Ma:2017pxb, `Radyushkin2017` = Radyushkin:2017cyf, `XiongEtAl2014` = Xiong:2013bka, `EbertStewartZhao2019` = Ebert:2018gzl, `KugoOjima1979` = Kugo:1979gm, `Sterman1987` = Sterman:1986aj, `BecherNeubertPecjak2007` = Becher:2006mr, `CollinsRogersSato2022` = Collins:2021vke, `CandidoForteHekhorn2020` = Candido:2020yat, `Ma2020` = Ma:2019hjq, `Gaunt2014` = Gaunt:2014ska, `RothsteinStewart2016` = Rothstein:2016bsq). Reading depth: *(read)* = located in the full text by the frontier agent; *(abstract)* = abstract only → the writer introduces the claim with 「据该文摘要」 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 2). Preprints (arXiv only on 2026-10-10) are marked † here and 「（预印本）」 in the book.

## F0. Global decisions for Part VII

### F0.1 Scope, budgets, format

| # | key | file | 中文标题（binding） | pp | format |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 22 | `lamet` | `ch22_lamet.tex` | LaMET 与格点因子化：季向东等人的因子化证明 | 18 | frontier |
| 23 | `yzliu` | `ch23_yzliu.tex` | Yizhuang Liu 的严格因子化研究 | 15 | frontier |
| 24 | `front` | `ch24_front.tex` | 其他前沿严格结果 | 18 | frontier |

Frontier format (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §11, §6.11): ≈55 % statements with hypotheses and proof architecture, ≈20 % explicit results (one-/two-loop kernels, checks, worked examples), ≈25 % status, evidence and open problems. Every chapter opens with the box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (§6.6) and ends with a status table and an open-problem list. No correspondence table is required (§6.7 lists only core chapters).

### F0.2 Ownership decisions (binding for this outline)

1. **Ch22 `lamet`** owns: the LaMET principle; lattice moments and the Euclidean Compton amplitude (v1 `prop:lattice-moments`); quasi-/pseudo-distributions and lattice cross sections (v1 `def:lattice-quasi`); support and analyticity; renormalization of straight space-like Wilson-line operators (v1 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`); renormalization schemes incl. the hybrid scheme; one- and two-loop kernels; the matching theorems with the hypotheses (H1)–(H5) of the LaMET report (now `prop:lattice-renormalizability` and `asm:lamet-ope`, `asm:lamet-uniform`, `asm:lamet-twist4`, `asm:lamet-large-z`) and the three proof routes (OPE route of Izubuchi–Ji–Jin–Stewart–Zhao; Ma–Qiu collinear-subtraction route; good lattice cross sections); v1 `thm:lattice-lamet`; target-mass and dynamical power corrections; the linear renormalon of the Wilson-line self-energy and leading-renormalon resummation; the Fourier step incl. the exponential-decay theorem (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2, VF-LAM-18); LaMET vs short-distance factorization (SDF); quasi-GPD/DA; **the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, the off-light-cone and reduced soft functions (definitions), the lattice Collins–Soper kernel (v1 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`) and TMD ratios**; v1 `rem:lattice-critiques`, `ex:lattice-quasi-tree`; the status board.
   - **Deviation from the WRITER_SPEC_v2 default** (§6.11 item 5 and §12.2 put "quasi-TMD factorization and the CS kernel from ratios" in Ch23). Reason: `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` is a moved v1 `lattice` label, and the binding lead-author decision says that Ch22 absorbs the v1 `lattice` content with unchanged labels; its proof uses the quasi-TMD factorization theorem, and the no-forward-edge rule (§4.5 item 4) forbids `\uses{thm:<Ch23 label>}` in Ch22. The frontier task statement also lists "LaMET for DAs/GPDs/TMDs (Ji–Liu–Liu; Ebert–Stewart–Zhao; Vladimirov–Schäfer), lattice Collins–Soper kernel" under Ch22. Consequences: VF-LAM-15 and VF-LAM-16 are "Used in: Ch22" (`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:lattice-cs-kernel`); `fig:yzliu-leading-region` stays in Ch23 because it illustrates Liu's Collins-style proof route (`prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`).
2. **Ch23 `yzliu`** owns: Euclidean soft functions as heavy-quark (HQET) form factors; the partial-exponentiation lemma; pinch poles and ultrasoft modes; analyticity in the complex hyperbolic angle and the universality classes; the linearity (factorization) of rapidity divergences; the intrinsic soft function $S_I$ (on-light-cone definition, scheme independence, anomalous dimension, two-loop structure); the 2024 time-ordering theorem; Liu's Collins-style power-counting proof route of the quasi-TMD theorem; the hard kernel (RGE, heavy–light decomposition, two loops); LFWF amplitudes; the meson form factor and the extraction of $S_I$; DY in terms of quasi-TMDs; Sivers ratios; the space-like jet function and its continuation; **the threshold factorization of the quasi-PDF kernel** (owner per WRITER_SPEC_v2 §12.2 and VF-LAM-14); the anomalous-dimension algebra; the linear renormalon of the heavy–light kernel; GPDs near $x=\pm\xi$; the exactly solvable 2D models; the extensions of the exponential-decay theorem.
3. **Ch22 does not own threshold factorization.** It owns only the one-loop endpoint structure of the matching kernel (`prop:lamet-oneloop`(e)) and the domain-of-validity remark (`rem:lamet-domain`), with forward pointers in running text to `thm:yzliu-threshold` (never in `\uses`).
4. **Ch24 `front`** owns all OTHERS.md items (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 5). It never re-states a core statement. Where a core statement already covers part of a topic, Ch24 states the frontier refinement under a new `front` label and cites the core label; the core statements keep their labels and owners. Overlaps handled this way: `thm:ir-landau-momentum`, `rem:ir-landau-status`, `prop:ir-coleman-norton`, `prop:ir-regions-minkowski`, `lem:appD-gordan` (Landau/regions); `thm:power-ope-convergence` (massive $\varphi^4_4$ OPE convergence stays in Ch21); `prop:scet-glauber-operators` (RS Glauber operators stay in Ch15); `prop:break-jet-veto`, `prop:break-fss`, `prop:break-spacelike`, `prop:break-mass-singularity`, `thm:break-double-dy` (Ch18); `prop:jets-sll`, `thm:jets-ngl-factorization`, `thm:jets-bms` (Ch16); `thm:smallx-bk`, `thm:smallx-bfkl` (Ch17); `conj:heavy-nrqcd-production`, `prop:heavy-quarkonium-fragmentation` (Ch20); `conj:pdf-positivity` (Ch8); `prop:appJ-local-operators`, `rem:appJ-nonlocal`, `prop:appL-renormalized` (App J, App L); `prop:power-nlp-endpoint` (Ch21); `prop:thr-nlp` (Ch13); `prop:jets-ngl-beyond` (Ch16); `prop:dy-abelian-model` (Ch12, the eikonal spectator phase); `thm:heavy-nrqcd-decay` (Ch20). Requested core-side pointers: §F5.
5. **Exponential decay.** Ch22 owns `thm:lamet-exp-decay` (the theorem of Ji–Liu–Su under axioms) and `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` (the QCD asymptotic forms); Ch23 owns `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions` (all-order spectral representation and exponential decay in $\varphi^3/\varphi^4$, [P]); the extension of `thm:lamet-exp-decay` to one-particle intermediate states is described in the text of that entry, with the status of `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, and is not graded separately.

### F0.4 Tags, qualifiers and assumptions in Part VII

1. **Mapping of frontier grade notations** (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.3 item 4): "[R] in PT, diagram by diagram" → [R, R_PT]; "[R]$_{1L}$/[R]$_{2L}$" → [R, R_PT] for the statement restricted to that order (first sentence states the order), the all-order statement keeps its own tag; "[R] (cond.)" → [R, R_cond]; "[R] under Wightman-type axioms" → [R, R_cond] with `asm:intro-wightman` + the frontier assumption it needs; "[R] (model)" → [P] with the model in the first sentence (the model-internal claim may get a full proof); "[R]†" → this outline decides (Ch24: [A] unless the proof is printed in full; status paragraph 「（证明仅见于预印本）」); sub-grades A±: dropped; "open" → [C].
2. **What becomes an `asm:`.** Only hypotheses that act as *formal premises* of a statement (R_cond premises, or hypotheses that several statements share) become register entries. The weakest *step* of an [A] argument (e.g. Liu's explicitly assumed IR-control step in the rapidity-slicing argument) is named in the 「地位与缺口」 paragraph, not turned into an assumption — otherwise the grade-propagation rule (§4.6) would turn every [A] argument with a sketched step into [C].
3. **Frontier register entries (14).** Ch22 (9): `asm:lamet-aux-renorm` [A], `asm:lamet-ope` [A], `asm:lamet-uniform` [C], `asm:lamet-twist4` [C], `asm:lamet-large-z` [C], `asm:lamet-gap` [C], `asm:lamet-staple` [A], `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` [A], `asm:lamet-order-limits` [A]. Ch23 (3): `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair` [C], `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` [A], `asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform` [P]. Ch24 (2): `asm:front-mor-complete` [C], `asm:front-scet-modes` [A]. Full register entries with all fields: §F1. All are *technical* assumptions in the sense of WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.4 item 7: they enter the grade propagation and fix the content grade of every R_cond statement that lists them (e.g. the explicitly non-perturbative `thm:lamet-exp-decay` and `cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor` have content grade [C]); only the six global `asm:intro-*` entries are exempt.
4. **Inner item names** are chapter-unique: (AX1)–(AX3) aux field; (OP1)–(OP2) OPE; (UI) uniformity; (TW1)–(TW2) twist 4; (LZ1)–(LZ2) large $z$; (GP1)–(GP3) gap; (ST1)–(ST3) staple; (SR) soft rapidity; (OL1)–(OL2) order of limits; (HP1)–(HP3) heavy pair; (WP1)–(WP2) Wightman PT; (UL) uniform light-cone limit; (MR) regions; (SM1)–(SM2) SCET modes. Local hypothesis lists inside statements: (QT1)–(QT7) (Liu's quasi-TMD power counting), (PS1)–(PS3) (positivity), (J1)–(J4) (Jantzen), (ED-a)–(ED-c) are not used (the decay theorem uses `asm:lamet-gap`). The frontier reports' own numbering (Thm 4.3, Prop 10.4, (H1)–(H6), (T1)–(T5), (Q1)–(Q7), (S1)–(S3)) is never printed.
5. **Non-perturbative status line.** Hadron-level statements end their hypotheses with 「非微扰地位：\cref{asm:…}（MA…）。」 using the global entries `asm:intro-wightman`, `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-bridge`; statements about partonic states, operators or models say 「（本命题只涉及部分子态。）」 or name the model.

### F0.5 How each entry is organised

As in v1 §0.7, plus the v2 fields: **type, canonical label, Chinese title, tag (+ qualifier; for R_cond the premise sentence)**; **statement** in formulas (our conventions; VF IDs in `%` comments); **hypotheses / non-perturbative line**; **`\uses{…}`** (only statement labels, only backward edges; v2-new core labels are those of REFDOC_ANALYSIS §5 and WRITER_SPEC_v2); **proof plan** (full / sketch, numbered steps, literature locations with reading depth); **status and gap** (for [A]/[P]/[C]/[V]: weakest step, `asm:` labels, error type where power counting is involved, refereed or preprint); **[PENDING-VERIFY]** with claim ID; **budget** in pages. "(数学，无标签)" marks pure mathematics.

# 第七部分 前沿进展 (Part VII — Frontier progress)

The master file issues `\part{前沿进展}` before Ch22 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §1.1 note 4). Part VII opens with a half-page part preface written by the Ch22 writer (not a statement; no labels): what "frontier" means in this book (results after ≈2010 whose status is still moving), the reading-depth and preprint policy, and the map of the three chapters: Ch22 (LaMET: Euclidean access to light-cone objects, Ji and collaborators), Ch23 (Yizhuang Liu: soft functions, analyticity, threshold, exactly solvable tests), Ch24 (other rigorous progress: Landau theory, OPE, regions, Glauber, NLP, positivity, BRST, small $x$, DPS, jets, quarkonium, QED/EW).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
## Chapter 22 — LaMET 与格点因子化：季向东等人的因子化证明 / LaMET and lattice factorization: the factorization proofs of Ji and collaborators

`\label{ch:lamet}` · `ch22_lamet.tex` · **18 pp** · frontier format · absorbs v1 Ch21 `lattice` (all v1 `lattice` labels, section labels, `fig:lattice-matching` and the existing figure `fig:quasi-pdf` keep their names)

**Purpose.** Lattice QCD computes Euclidean, equal-time matrix elements; parton distributions are light-cone objects. Large-momentum effective theory (大动量有效理论，LaMET) and short-distance factorization (短距离因子化，SDF) connect the two through factorization theorems of their own. This chapter states these theorems with all hypotheses explicit and separates three levels sharply: (i) explicit fixed-order results ([R, R_PT] at the stated order); (ii) all-order perturbative matching for partonic states ([A], with the implication from the hypotheses written as [R, R_cond]); (iii) the hadron-level statement, whose non-perturbative content is isolated in numbered assumptions ([C]). It closes with the TMD side that the lattice uses today: the quasi-TMD factorization theorem and the Collins–Soper kernel from momentum ratios. Lattice results are evidence and supply matrix elements; they never test cross-section factorization (critic C12).

**Depends on.** Ch3 (`def:prelim-wilson`, `prop:prelim-wilson-rules`, `def:prelim-plus`, `def:prelim-mellin`); Ch2 (`def:prelim-qcd`, `def:prelim-brst`, `def:prelim-msbar`, `thm:prelim-composite-renorm`); Ch7 (`def:dis-twist`, `prop:dis-dispersion`, `prop:dis-dispersion-np`, `thm:dis-ope` [A], `thm:dis-moments` [R, R_OPE], `thm:dis-moments-np`, `prop:dis-tmc`); Ch8 (`def:pdf-quark`, `def:pdf-lightray`, `def:pdf-polarized`, `thm:pdf-support-pt`, `thm:pdf-moments-local`, `thm:pdf-dglap`, `prop:pdf-lo-kernels`, `prop:pdf-partonic-dr`); Ch14 (`def:tmd-soft`, `def:tmd-collins`, `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, `prop:tmd-evolution`, `prop:tmd-evolution-oneloop`); Ch19 (`def:excl-gpd`, `def:excl-da`); Ch21 (`def:power-borel`, `prop:power-renormalon-examples`, `prop:power-rearrangement`); App B (`prop:appB-webs`); global `asm:intro-wightman`, `asm:intro-composite`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `asm:intro-growth`, `asm:intro-bridge`. Forward pointers (text only, never `\uses`): Ch23 (`thm:yzliu-threshold`, `thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft`, `prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear`, `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`, `conj:yzliu-gpd-xi`, `prop:yzliu-linear-renormalon`, `thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`), Ch24 (`prop:front-rapidity-renorm`).

**Box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」 (content for the writer).**
- 证明了：`prop:lamet-support`、`cor:lamet-entire`（[R, R_PT]）；`prop:lamet-oneloop`、`prop:lamet-tails`、`prop:lamet-twoloop`、`prop:lamet-hybrid-kernel`（[R, R_PT]，限于所述阶）；`thm:lamet-coordinate`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`、`asm:lamet-ope`）、`thm:lamet-parton-momentum`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `thm:lamet-coordinate`）；`prop:lamet-tmc`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `asm:lamet-ope`）；`thm:lamet-hadronic`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `asm:lamet-uniform`、`asm:lamet-twist4`、`asm:lamet-large-z`）；`thm:lamet-exp-decay`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `asm:intro-wightman`、`asm:lamet-gap`）；`prop:lamet-sdf`（[R, R_cond]）；`lem:lamet-fourier`、`lem:lamet-strip`、`prop:lamet-truncation`、`prop:lamet-slepian`（数学）；`prop:lattice-moments`（[R, R_OPE]）。
- 给出全阶论证：`prop:lattice-renormalizability`（[A]；瓶颈 `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`）；`thm:lattice-lamet`、`prop:lamet-cfp`、`thm:lamet-lcs`、`prop:lamet-gluon-singlet`、`thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`（[A]；瓶颈 `asm:lamet-ope`）；`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`、`prop:lattice-cs-kernel`、`prop:lamet-tmd-ratios`（[A]；瓶颈 `asm:lamet-order-limits`）；`cor:lamet-principle-pt`。
- 没有证明：LaMET 原理的非微扰形式（`conj:lamet-principle`，[C]）；强子层次匹配所需的 `asm:lamet-uniform`、`asm:lamet-twist4`、`asm:lamet-large-z`（[C]）；QCD 中 Wilson 线关联函数的指数衰减（`conj:lamet-decay-qcd`，[C]）；比值与 RI/MOM 方案在大 $|z|$ 处的动量空间匹配（`rem:lamet-ratio-large-z`，[C]）；GPD 在 $x=\pm\xi$ 的情形（第 23 章）。

### Canonical statements

#### §22.2 `sec:lamet-principle`

* **猜想 `conj:lamet-principle` — LaMET 原理（非微扰形式）** [C].
  - *Statement.* Let $\mathcal O_E$ be a renormalized equal-time (Euclidean) operator whose formal infinite-boost limit is a light-front operator $\mathcal O_{LF}$ (the "universality class" $[\mathcal O_{LF}]$; e.g. $\Gamma=\gamma^0$ and $\gamma^z$ quasi-PDF operators belong to the class of $\gamma^+$). For hadron states with $P^z\gg\Lambda_{\rm QCD},M$:
    $$\langle P|\mathcal O_E|P\rangle(P^z,\mu)=\big[\mathcal Z(P^z/\mu,\alpha_s(\mu))\otimes\langle P|\mathcal O_{LF}|P\rangle(\mu)\big]+\sum_{k\ge1}\Big(\frac{\Lambda}{P^z}\Big)^{2k}\times(\text{matrix elements of higher-twist equal-time operators}),$$
    where $\mathcal Z$ has a perturbative expansion free of infrared sensitivity, the power expansion is asymptotic and organized by the hard scales of the partons ($|x|P^z$, $|1-x|P^z$), and linear terms $\Lambda/P^z$ are absent when the Wilson-line self-energy and the matching coefficients are defined with one renormalon prescription (`prop:lamet-lrr`, later in this chapter).
  - `\uses{def:pdf-quark, def:pdf-lightray}`.
  - *Status and gap.* No non-perturbative proof exists for any member of any universality class; it would require a non-perturbative OPE/factorization in QCD (`conj:ee-np-ope`, `asm:intro-np-ope`, `rem:power-np-status`). The perturbative content is `cor:lamet-principle-pt` ([A], end of chapter); the precise non-perturbative content of the collinear case is the set `asm:lamet-uniform`, `asm:lamet-twist4`, `asm:lamet-large-z` (`thm:lamet-hadronic`). Evidence (never proof): renormalon analyses (`prop:lamet-bvz`), exactly solvable 2D models (Ch23 `thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`, pointer), lattice data. Ji's slogan "the two orders of limits differ only in perturbation theory" (`Ji:2024oka`, read) is a heuristic. Literature: `Ji2013`, `Ji:2014gla`, `JiEtAl2021` §II–III, `Ji:2020byp`, `Ji:2024oka` (all read). Over-claim to avoid: 「LaMET 原理已被非微扰地证明」 (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.9).
  - *Budget.* 0.3 pp.
* **注 `rem:lamet-formulations` — LaMET 原理的几种表述及其可证明的内容** (no tag).
  - (a) `Ji2013` (read): from $\langle P|O^{\mu_1\cdots\mu_n}|P\rangle=2a_n(P^{\mu_1}\cdots P^{\mu_n}-{\rm tr})$, $a_n=\int dx\,x^{n-1}q(x)$, all indices $=z$: $\langle P|\bar\psi\gamma^ziD^z\cdots iD^z\psi|P\rangle=2a_n(P^z)^n[1+O(\Lambda^2/P_z^2,M^2/P_z^2)]$ — this moment relation is [R, R_def] (Lorentz covariance); the "inversion" to the non-local operator is heuristic: (i) the sum over $n$ does not commute with $P^z\to\infty$ (the moments of the $\overline{\rm MS}$ quasi-PDF do not exist, `prop:lamet-tails`), (ii) UV renormalization does not commute with $P^z\to\infty$ (Ji's one-loop transverse-cutoff result has a linear divergence for $\mu\to\infty$ first and the DGLAP logarithm for $P^z\to\infty$ first).
  - (b) `Ji:2014gla` (read): EFT form $F(P^z/\Lambda)=Z(P^z/\Lambda,\Lambda/\mu)f(\mu)+O(1/P_z^2)$, $\gamma=\partial\ln Z/\partial\ln P^z$; universality classes (`Hatta:2013gta` for $\Delta G$, abstract); quasi-LFWFs; smallest resolvable fraction $x_{\min}\sim\Lambda/P^z$. The paper asserts, without proof, that "the factorization in terms of Feynman diagrams can be proved order by order".
  - (c) `JiEtAl2021` §II–III (read): quasi-PDF as the momentum density $N_P(k^z)$; momentum RGE, at one loop $P^z\frac{d\tilde q}{dP^z}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi\big[\big(\frac{1+y^2}{1-y}\big)_+-\frac32\delta(1-y)\big]\otimes f$ (follows from the $\overline{\rm MS}$ kernel of `prop:lamet-oneloop` with $d\ell_p/d\ln P^z=-2$); the matching statement "true to all orders in perturbation theory" and its inverse.
  - (d) `Ji:2020byp`, `Ji:2024oka` (read): Euclidean EFT "in the spirit of Weinberg"; leading large-velocity Lagrangian $\mathcal L^{(0)}_{q,v}=\bar\psi_v[iv\cdot D+\frac{i\bar v\cdot D}{2\gamma^2}+iD_\perp\frac1{2i\bar v\cdot D}iD_\perp]\slashed{\bar v}\psi_v$ (write it in our sign convention, §F0.3 row 1); the finite boost as a non-perturbative regulator of rapidity divergences; twist-4 equal-time correlators with power divergences tied to IR renormalons of the leading coefficient; Symanzik analogy; breakdown at $x\sim\Lambda/P^z$; analyticity of the quasi-PDF at $y=0,1$ at finite $P^z$ (made precise by `lem:lamet-strip`).
  - *Budget.* 0.6 pp. Figure: none.

#### §22.3 `sec:lattice-moments` (v1)

* **命题 `prop:lattice-moments` — 格点可计算的矩** [R, R_OPE] (v1 label and tag; v2 additions from refdoc S43).
  - *Statement.* (a) (R_def content) By `thm:pdf-moments-local` the PDF moments $A^{(n)}_i(\mu)$ are forward matrix elements of local twist-2 operators and hence Euclidean-accessible. (b) The Euclidean forward Compton amplitude $T^{\mu\nu}(\omega,Q^2)$ at $|\omega|<1$ is lattice-computable (heavy-quark-mediated Compton tensor `DetmoldLin2006`, Feynman–Hellmann method `ChambersEtAl2017`, both abstract); its Taylor coefficients in $\omega$ are the structure-function moments (`prop:dis-dispersion`; for hadrons `prop:dis-dispersion-np`), and their OPE decomposition is $\sum_iC^{(n)}_iA^{(n)}_i$ (`thm:dis-moments`; hadrons `thm:dis-moments-np`). (c) Lattice moments therefore test `thm:dis-moments-np`, including the caveat for low moments $n<n_0$ (`asm:intro-growth`); the twist-2 basis is normalized non-perturbatively on the lattice (`prop:power-rearrangement`(a)).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：`asm:intro-wightman`、`asm:intro-composite`、`asm:intro-np-ope`（MA0、MA4）。
  - `\uses{thm:pdf-moments-local, prop:dis-dispersion, prop:dis-dispersion-np, thm:dis-moments, thm:dis-moments-np, prop:power-rearrangement}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Full (0.3 pp): combination of the cited statements. Status sentence: lattice renormalization and the continuum limit are numerical; power-divergent mixing of higher local moments limits $n$ (`rem:lattice-critiques`).
  - *Budget.* 0.5 pp.

#### §22.4 `sec:lattice-quasi` (v1)

* **定义 `def:lattice-quasi` — 准分布、赝分布与格点截面** (no tag; v1 label, content upgraded to VF-LAM-0/1).
  - Space-like separation $z^\mu=z\hat e_z^\mu$, hadron $P=(P^0,0,0,P^z)$, Ioffe time $\lambda=P\cdot z=-P^zz$, $-z^2=z_3^2>0$ (§F0.3 row 2).
  - Quasi-PDF ($\Gamma\in\{\gamma^0,\gamma^z\}$; $\gamma^0\equiv\gamma^t$):
    $$\tilde q_\Gamma(x,P^z;\mu)=N_\Gamma\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\frac{dz}{4\pi}\,e^{ixP^zz}\,\frac{\langle P|\bar\psi(z\hat e_z)\Gamma W[z\hat e_z,0]\psi(0)|P\rangle}{Z_X(z)},\qquad N_{\gamma^z}=1,\ N_{\gamma^0}=\frac{P^z}{P^0},$$
    with bare fields and $Z_X(z)$ the renormalization factor of a scheme $X$ (examples in `def:lamet-schemes`, `prop:lattice-renormalizability`; forward pointers in text). `% VF-LAM-1`
  - Lorentz decomposition $\langle P|\bar\psi(z)\gamma^\alpha W[z,0]\psi(0)|P\rangle=2P^\alpha\mathcal M(\lambda,-z^2)+2z^\alpha\mathcal N(\lambda,-z^2)$; $\tilde h_\Gamma(\lambda,z^2)\equiv\langle P|O_\Gamma(z)|P\rangle/(2P^\Gamma)$ ($P^{\gamma^0}=P^0$, $P^{\gamma^z}=P^z$); $\Gamma=\gamma^0$ isolates $\mathcal M$, $\gamma^z$ is contaminated by $\mathcal N$. Then $\tilde q(x,P^z)=\int\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-ix\lambda}\tilde h(\lambda,-\lambda^2/P_z^2)$ ($z$ and $\lambda$ vary together).
  - Pseudo-PDF $\mathcal P(x,-z^2)=\int\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-ix\lambda}\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)$ (transform at fixed $z^2$); reduced ITD $\mathfrak M(\lambda,z^2)=\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)/\tilde h(0,z^2)$; light-cone ITD $I(\lambda,\mu)=\frac{\langle P|\bar\psi(w)\gamma^+W[w,0]\psi(0)|P\rangle}{2P^+}=\int_{-1}^1dx\,e^{ix\lambda}f(x,\mu)$, $\lambda=P^+w^-$.
  - Lattice cross sections (Ma–Qiu, `MaQiu2018` read): $\sigma_n(\omega,\xi^2,P^2)=\langle P|T\{\mathcal O_n(\xi)\}|P\rangle$, $\omega=P\cdot\xi$ (our $\lambda$), $\xi^0=0$; "good" if (i) computable with Euclidean time, (ii) renormalizable, (iii) with the same factorizable collinear divergences as PDFs; examples: $\xi^{d_1+d_2-2}Z_1Z_2\,j_1(\xi)j_2(0)$ and the Wilson-line bilinear $\bar\psi(\xi)\slashed\xi W\psi(0)$.
  - Normalization: $\int dx\,\tilde q=1$ per valence quark if the scheme preserves the local limit (ratio, hybrid; not $\overline{\rm MS}$, `prop:lamet-tails`). Table (in the text, not a label): quasi-ITD ($\lambda,z^2$) → quasi-PDF ($z\to xP^z$ at fixed $P^z$; support $\mathbb R$) and pseudo-PDF ($\lambda\to x$ at fixed $z^2$; support $[-1,1]$, `prop:lamet-support`).
  - `\uses{def:prelim-wilson, def:pdf-quark, def:pdf-lightray}`.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-support` — 准关联函数的支撑性质（逐图）** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$ qualifier.）For every Feynman diagram, including counterterm diagrams, contributing to $\langle p|\bar\psi(z)\Gamma W[z,0]\psi(0)|p\rangle$ with partonic external states (and to the analogous multiparton correlators), the Fourier transform in $\lambda=p\cdot z$ at fixed $z^2$ is supported in $[-1,1]$; equivalently $\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)=\int_{-1}^1dx\,e^{ix\lambda}\mathcal P(x,-z^2)$ diagram by diagram. `% VF-LAM-1`
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.4 pp): α-(Schwinger) representation of each diagram; the $(p\cdot z)$ dependence enters as $\exp[-i(p\cdot z)\sum_k\alpha_kc_k/\sum\cdots]$ with a coefficient that is a convex combination bounded by $1$ in modulus. Original: `Radyushkin:1983wh` (recalled by the frontier agent — do not attribute details to it); statement and use read in `Radyushkin2017`, `Radyushkin:2018cvn`, `IzubuchiEtAl2018`. Analogue: `thm:pdf-support-pt`.
* **推论 `cor:lamet-entire` — Ioffe 时间分布是指数型整函数** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* Diagram by diagram, $\lambda\mapsto\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)$ extends to an entire function of exponential type $\le1$; for the OPE coefficient function this is the precise form of the statement "$\sigma_n$ is analytic in $\omega$ with the only singularity at $\omega=\infty$" (`MaQiu2018`, read).
  - `\uses{prop:lamet-support}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Full (Paley–Wiener–Schwartz theorem for distributions of compact support; 「文献待补：Hörmander, *The Analysis of Linear Partial Differential Operators I*, Thm. 7.3.1」 — not in the bib).
* **例 `ex:lattice-quasi-tree` — 自由夸克的准 PDF：树图与单圈非物理区** (v1 label; content extended).
  - (a) Tree level: $\langle p|\bar\psi(z)\gamma^0\psi(0)|p\rangle=2p^0e^{-ip^zz}$ gives $\tilde q^{(0)}=\delta(1-x)$ (check the $N_{\gamma^0}$ factor and spin average).
  - (b) One loop, $\xi=x/y\notin[0,1]$ (all graphs finite in $d=4$): on-shell massless quark $p=(p^z,0,0,p^z)$, $\Gamma=\gamma^0$, Feynman gauge, our rules (vertex $ig\gamma^\mu t^a$, link vertex from $ig\int_0^1ds\,z^\mu A_\mu(sz)$). Feynman parameters with $k=l+(1-y)p$ and $yk^2+(1-y)(p-k)^2=(k-(1-y)p)^2$; 3D Euclidean integrals at fixed $l^z=(\xi-1+y)p^z$: $\int\frac{d^3l_E}{(2\pi)^3}(l_E^2+\ell^2)^{-2}=\frac1{8\pi|\ell|}$, $\int\frac{d^3l_E}{(2\pi)^3}(l_E^2+\ell^2)^{-3}=\frac1{32\pi|\ell|^3}$. Vertex $a\int_0^1dy\frac{1-y}{|\xi-y|}$; sails $\frac a{1-\xi}\int_0^1dy\frac{\xi+y}{|\xi-y|}$; Wilson-line self-energy $-\frac a{|1-\xi|}$ (no linear term in DR); sum
    $$\xi>1:\ \tilde q^{(1)}=a\Big[\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\ln\frac{\xi}{\xi-1}+1\Big],\qquad \xi<0:\ \tilde q^{(1)}=a\Big[-\frac{1+\xi^2}{1-\xi}\ln\frac{-\xi}{1-\xi}-1\Big].$$ `% VF-LAM-7`
  - Lesson: support on all of $\mathbb R$ at finite $P^z$; matching is a convolution over $y\in\mathbb R$; the historical transverse-cutoff result (`XiongEtAl2014`) has a linear divergence $\propto\Lambda_{\rm UV}/((1-y)^2P^z)$ (as reported in `JiEtAl2021`, read).
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, prop:prelim-wilson-rules}`. Verification: LAMET s1 D1/D2 (exact at 9 points; agrees with `IzubuchiEtAl2018` App. B). Claim N05 (frontier derivation; a re-derivation, **not** a novelty claim). Budget 0.5 pp.
* **Figures.** `fig:quasi-pdf` (existing, tikz3; owner Ch22; caption conventions to be checked against §F0.3 row 2), `fig:lamet-ioffe` (N5 of FIGURES_INDEX_v2; its proposed section `sec:lamet-objects` is this section `sec:lattice-quasi`).

#### §22.5 `sec:lamet-renorm`

* **假设 `asm:lamet-aux-renorm` — 含类空辅助静态色源的 QCD 的可重整性** [A]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (AX1) Extend QCD by a colour-triplet spinless Grassmann field $Q$ with $\mathcal L_Q=\bar Q\,i\hat n\cdot D\,Q$ (+ $\delta m\,\bar QQ$ for cut-off regulators), $\hat n=\hat e_z$; in a background field $S_Q(x,y)=\theta(x^z-y^z)\delta(x^0-y^0)\delta^2(\vec x_\perp-\vec y_\perp)W[x,y]$, so that $O_\Gamma(z)=\langle\bar\psi(z)\Gamma Q(z)\,\bar Q(0)\psi(0)\rangle_Q$ is a product of the local composite operators $\bar J=\bar\psi Q$, $J=\bar Q\psi$ of the extended theory.
  - (AX2) The extended theory is renormalizable with the counterterms $Z_Q$, $Z_1^{QQg}$ (tied to $Z_g$ by Slavnov–Taylor identities) and $\delta m$ only, BRST-invariantly.
  - (AX3) The $Q$-loop determinant $\det(i\hat n\cdot D)$ is a constant in the regularization used (DR).
  - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **命题 `prop:lattice-renormalizability` — Wilson 线夸克双线性算符的乘法可重整性** [A] (v1 label and tag).
  - *Statement.* (a) Let $O^B_\Gamma(z)=\bar\psi_0(z\hat n)\Gamma W_0[z\hat n,0]\psi_0(0)$, $\hat n$ space-like, $z\ne0$, flavour non-singlet or singlet, any $\Gamma$. With a gauge-invariant regulator there exist constants $Z_j$ (independent of $z$, $\Gamma$ and the external states) and, for cut-off regulators, a linearly divergent $\delta m$, such that $O^R_\Gamma(z,\mu)=Z_j^{-2}e^{-\delta m|z|}O^B_\Gamma(z)$ has finite Green functions with elementary fields at every order; $Z_j=Z_\psi^{1/2}Z_Q^{1/2}Z_V$ is the renormalization of the heavy–light current $\bar Q\psi$; no mixing with gluon operators or other flavours. (b) Lattice, chirally non-symmetric fermions: $O^R_\Gamma(z)=Z_\phi^2e^{-m|z|}O_{\Gamma'}(z)$, $\Gamma'=\Gamma+r_{\rm mix}{\rm sgn}(z)\{\slashed n,\Gamma\}+r^2_{\rm mix}\slashed n\Gamma\slashed n$ (three parameters: $m$ linear, $Z_\phi$ logarithmic, $r_{\rm mix}$ finite, $O(g^2)$); no mixing for $\Gamma=\gamma^0$ at $O(a^0)$ because $\{\gamma^z,\gamma^0\}=0$. (c) Gluon operators $O_g(z)=g_\perp^{\mu\nu}F^{n_1}{}_\mu(z/2)W^{ab}F^{n_2}{}_\nu(-z/2)$, $n_i\in\{\hat e_z,\hat e_t\}$: for $z\ne0$ and transverse indices the relevant combinations renormalize multiplicatively (four unpolarized, three helicity correlators); gluon and singlet-quark quasi-PDF operators do not mix under renormalization (mixing appears only in the matching). (d)（单圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）In DR: $\delta m=0$, $Z_j=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{4\pi}\frac3{2\varepsilon}$, $Z_O=Z_j^2=1+\frac{\alpha_sC_F}{4\pi}\frac3\varepsilon$, $\frac{d\ln O^R}{d\ln\mu^2}=\frac{3\alpha_sC_F}{4\pi}+O(\alpha_s^2)$ $(=\frac32a)$, the coefficient of $\ln\mu^2$ in $C_0=1+a(\frac32L_z+\frac52)$ (`prop:lamet-oneloop`, forward pointer in text). Gluon one loop (TR, `JiEtAl2021` read): $Z_{J^{n_ti}}=1+\frac{\alpha_s}{4\pi}(\frac76C_A-\frac43n_fT_F)\frac1\varepsilon$, $Z_{J^{n_z\nu}}=1+\frac{\alpha_s}{4\pi}(\frac16C_A-\frac43n_fT_F)\frac1\varepsilon$. `% VF-LAM-2`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及算符与部分子 Green 函数。）
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:prelim-wilson, def:prelim-msbar, thm:prelim-composite-renorm}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.6 pp). Route A (auxiliary field; `Ji:2017oey`, `Green:2017xeu`, read): (1) `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`; (2) the heavy–light currents renormalize multiplicatively by the local-operator argument of `thm:prelim-composite-renorm` (no lower-dimensional operator with the same quantum numbers; for gluons Dorn's mixing of $F^{\mu\nu}_aQ^a$ with $J_2^{\mu\nu}=(n^\nu F^{\mu n}-n^\mu F^{\nu n})Q/n^2$ and an EOM operator, harmless for $z\ne0$ and transverse indices; `Dorn:1986dt`); (3) integrate out $Q$. On the lattice $Q$ is a static-quark-like field with action $\frac1{1+am_0}\bar\zeta(\nabla_n+m_0)\zeta$, propagator $\theta(\xi)e^{-m\xi}W$, $m=a^{-1}\ln(1+am_0)$; the mass term is not forbidden by any symmetry (GJS). Route B (diagrammatic; `Ishikawa:2017faj`, read): coordinate-space power counting of link insertions ($\Delta\omega_3$, $\Delta\omega_4$); gauge-link-irreducible diagrams have no overall UV divergence from 3D sub-integrations; UV divergences are local and come from (i) the link self-energy (linear part exponentiates: $1+c\int dr_1+c^2\int\!\!\int+\dots=Pe^{cr}$), (ii) gluon–link vertices (coupling renormalization, `Dotsenko:1979wb`), (iii) quark–link endpoints (constant counterterm). Gluons: `Li:2018tpe`, `Zhang:2018diq` (abstracts). Explicit checks: one loop (many groups), two loops `Ji:2015jwa`, three-loop anomalous dimension `Braun:2020ymy` (read), heavy–light current to three loops `Chetyrkin:2003vi`; lattice mixing `Constantinou:2017sej`, `Chen:2017mie`, improvement `Green:2020xco`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: (i) the space-like extension of the HQET/BRST renormalizability proof is asserted (`asm:lamet-aux-renorm`); (ii) the diagrammatic counting is at the level of superficial degrees and "bookkeeping forests", not a BPHZ/Zimmermann proof (error type E-S); (iii) $z=0$ is a local current with different renormalization (contact terms) — all statements are for $z\ne0$. Note for the writer (claim E05, [PENDING-VERIFY]): the "no lower-dimensional mixing" statement of `Ji:2017oey` is correct; same-dimension lattice mixing is item (b) — state it without calling it an error unless App Q lists it; verify s1: say "at $O(a^0)$", and for $\gamma^t$ the $r^2_{\rm mix}$ term only rescales the operator.
  - *Budget.* 0.8 pp. Figure `fig:lamet-lattice-geometries` (N8; panel (a) here; panels (b), (c) re-used in `sec:lattice-tmd`).
* **命题 `prop:lamet-ad-spacelike` — 类空与类时 Wilson 线算符的反常维度相同** [A].
  - *Statement.* Green functions of the non-local quark operator with space-like and with time-like Wilson lines are related by an explicit substitution rule (analytic continuation in $v^2$); the renormalization constants and anomalous dimensions coincide to all orders; $\gamma_O=2\gamma_{hl}$ ($\gamma_{hl}$: heavy–light current), independent of $z$. (三圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$) three-loop $\gamma_O$ in `Braun:2020ymy` App. A (TR; BCK normalization $a_s=\alpha_s/(4\pi)$, $\mu^2d/d\mu^2$).
  - `\uses{prop:lattice-renormalizability}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (analytic continuation; `Braun:2020ymy`, read). Status: [A] (continuation argument; explicit three-loop agreement). Pointer: the analytic-continuation technology of Ch23.
  - *Budget.* 0.15 pp.
* **定义 `def:lamet-schemes` — 准关联函数的重整化方案** (no tag).
  - A scheme $X$: $\tilde h^X=\lim_{a\to0}\tilde h(z,a)/Z_X(z,a)$, convertible to $\overline{\rm MS}$ by $\tilde h^X=Z'_X(z^2\mu_R^2,\mu_R/\mu)\tilde h^{\overline{\rm MS}}$ (`IzubuchiEtAl2018`, read). Schemes (table `tab:lamet-schemes`): Wilson-line mass subtraction ($e^{-\delta m|z|}$ + log $Z$; $z$-independent conversion; `Chen:2016fxx`, `Ishikawa:2016znu`†, `Green:2017xeu`); RI/MOM ($Z^{-1}\tilde h_{\rm quark}(z,p)|_{p^2=-\mu_R^2,p^z=p^z_R}=$ tree; $z$-dependent conversion with $\ln z^2\mu_R^2$; gauge and projector choices; `Constantinou:2017sej`, `Stewart:2017tvs`, `Alexandrou:2017huk`, `Chen:2017mzz`, `LatticeParton:2018gjr`); ratio/reduced ITD ($\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)/\tilde h(0,z^2)$ or a vacuum matrix element; conversion $1/C_0(-z^2\mu^2)$; `Radyushkin2017`, `Orginos:2017kos`, `Braun:2018brg`); hybrid (ratio for $|z|\le z_S$; $e^{-\delta m|z|}Z_{\rm hyb}$ for $|z|>z_S$, $Z_{\rm hyb}=e^{\delta mz_S}/Z_X(z_S)$ by continuity; conversion $1/Z^{\overline{\rm MS}}_X(\min(|z|,z_S))$, perturbative for all $z$; `Ji:2020brr`); self-renormalization (fit $\ln Z(a)$ to $m_{-1}(a)/a+m_0$ + discretization + the $\overline{\rm MS}$ short-distance shape; `LatticePartonLPC:2021gpi`, abstract); Coulomb-gauge bilinears without link (no linear divergence, $z$-independent $Z$; `Gao:2023lny`, `Zhao:2023ptv`, abstracts). Hybrid conversion: $\tilde h_{\rm hyb}=\tilde h_{\overline{\rm MS}}/C_0(z_S)=\tilde h_{\rm ratio}C_0(z)/C_0(z_S)$ for $|z|>z_S$. `% VF-LAM-3`
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, prop:lattice-renormalizability, def:prelim-msbar}`.
* **引理 `lem:lamet-lorentzian` — 残余线性项 $e^{-m_0|z|}$ 的 Lorentz 抹平** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* If $\tilde h_1(\lambda)=e^{-m_0|\lambda|/P^z}\tilde h_2(\lambda)$, then $\tilde q_1(y)-\tilde q_2(y)=\frac1\pi\int dy'\frac{\delta}{\delta^2+(y-y')^2}[\tilde q_2(y')-\tilde q_2(y)]$, $\delta=m_0/P^z$; if $\tilde q_2$ is bounded with bounded second derivative, $\sup_y|\tilde q_1-\tilde q_2|\le\frac\delta\pi(\|\tilde q_2''\|_\infty+4\|\tilde q_2\|_\infty)$: a **linear** correction $O(m_0/P^z)$.
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (Fourier transform of $e^{-\delta|\lambda|}$ is the Lorentzian; split $|u|<1$, $|u|\ge1$). The identity is `Ji:2020brr` (read); the explicit bound is writer-derived (report the check).
* **命题 `prop:lamet-lrr` — Wilson 线自能的线性重整子与领头重整子重求和** [P].
  - *Statement.* (Large-$\beta_0$ / bubble-chain statement, first sentence.) (a) On the lattice $\delta m=m_{-1}(a)/a+m_0$; $m_{-1}=O(\alpha_s)$ is universal, $m_0=O(\Lambda)$ depends on the condition used to fix $\delta m$ (static potential, large-$z$ decay, auxiliary-field propagator, RI/MOM). (b) The perturbative series of the linearly divergent self-energy has the pole-mass renormalon at $u=\frac12$ (`def:power-borel`); truncation at $n\sim1/\alpha_s$ leaves an $O(\Lambda)$ ambiguity of $\delta m$. (c) A residual $e^{-m_0|z|}$ smears $\tilde q$ by a Lorentzian of width $m_0/P^z$ (`lem:lamet-lorentzian`): a linear power correction $O(\Lambda/(xP^z))$. (d) In $\overline{\rm MS}$ the Wilson coefficients $C_k(\alpha_s,z^2\mu^2)$ of the quasi-ITD carry the corresponding renormalon at $u=\frac12$, with an ambiguity linear in $|z|$ and independent of spin; leading-renormalon resummation (LRR) — the same principal-value prescription in $C_k$ and in $m_0$, with $m_0$ fixed from the $P^z=0$ matrix element at short $z$ — removes the $O(\Lambda/P^z)$ corrections; normalized (ratio) schemes have no $u=\frac12$ term.
  - `\uses{lem:lamet-lorentzian, def:lamet-schemes, def:power-borel, prop:power-renormalon-examples, prop:lattice-renormalizability}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: (a) `Ji:2020brr` (read); (b) pole-mass renormalon (`Beneke:1994sw`, `Bigi:1994em`); (c) `lem:lamet-lorentzian`; (d) `Braun:2018brg` (read: UV renormalon $w=\frac12$ of the coefficient function = Wilson-line linear divergence), `Zhang:2023bxs` (read: LRR, pole-mass normalization cross-checked with lattice perturbation theory to 20 loops), `Holligan:2023rex` (abstract).
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: rigorous only as a statement about the bubble chain; the prescription is a scheme choice; the universality of $m_0(\tau)$ across matrix elements is open (it depends on the external states, `Zhang:2023bxs`); the ensemble-specific numbers ($m_0\approx0.16$ GeV; "factor 3–5" improvement) are not printed as general facts.
  - *Budget.* 0.4 pp.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-euclid-minkowski` — 欧氏格点矩阵元与 Minkowski 矩阵元的等同** [A].
  - *Statement.* Equal-time matrix elements computed in Euclidean lattice QCD coincide with the Minkowski (LSZ) matrix elements; the identification is unique to all orders in PT (`Briceno:2017cpo`, abstract: 「据该文摘要」). Remark: finite-volume effects of spatially non-local operators are exponentially small but grow with $|z|$ (`Briceno:2018lfj`, abstract).
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] at abstract-level reading; the writer states the claim as the authors' and gives no proof beyond the idea (equal-time operators: no Euclidean continuation of the operator needed).
  - *Budget.* 0.1 pp.

#### §22.6 `sec:lamet-oneloop`

* **命题 `prop:lamet-oneloop` — 准 PDF 匹配的单圈结果** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$ qualifier.）At one loop, non-singlet, $a=\alpha_sC_F/(2\pi)$, $\overline{\rm MS}$ operator renormalization (the one-loop counterterm is computed here; it is the one-loop content of `prop:lattice-renormalizability`(d), a pointer, so the [A] registry tag of that composite statement does not enter), partonic states with DR as IR regulator:
    (a) coordinate kernel ($\Gamma=\gamma^0$: $\sigma=0$; $\gamma^z$: $\sigma=1$)
    $$\mathcal C(\alpha,L_z)=\Big[1+a\Big(\frac32L_z+\frac32\Big)\Big]\delta(1-\alpha)+a\Big\{\Big[\frac{1+\alpha^2}{1-\alpha}\Big]_+(-L_z-1)-\Big[\frac{4\ln(1-\alpha)}{1-\alpha}\Big]_++2(1+\sigma)(1-\alpha)\Big\}\theta(\alpha)\theta(1-\alpha),$$
    with moments $C_n=1+a\big[\big(\frac{3+2n}{2+3n+n^2}+2H_n\big)L_z+\frac{5+2n}{2+3n+n^2}+2(1-H_n)H_n-2H^{(2)}_n\big]$ ($+\frac{2a}{2+3n+n^2}$ for $\gamma^z$), $C_0=1+a(\frac32L_z+\frac52)$, and RG $\frac{d\mathcal C}{d\ln\mu^2}=a[\frac32\delta(1-\alpha)-B(\alpha)]$, $B=[\frac{1+\alpha^2}{1-\alpha}]_+$ (consistent with DGLAP and with $\frac{d\ln O^R}{d\ln\mu^2}=\frac32a$); `% VF-LAM-4`
    (b) ratio kernel $\mathcal C^{\rm ratio}=\mathcal C-\delta(1-\alpha)C_0$ = Radyushkin's reduced-ITD relation (his $\nu=-\lambda$); `% VF-LAM-5`
    (c) the unphysical-region quasi-PDF of `ex:lattice-quasi-tree`(b);
    (d) momentum-space kernels $C^{\overline{\rm MS}}(\xi)$ and $C^{\rm ratio}(\xi)$ of VF-LAM-8 (three-domain plus distributions $[g]^D_{+(1)}$; $\gamma^z$ variants; δ functions at $\xi=\pm\infty$ dropped for $f(y)\sim y^{-1+a'}$, $a'>0$), which are the distributional Fourier transforms of (a), (b) at fixed parton momentum; `% VF-LAM-8`
    (e) endpoint structure (input of the threshold factorization, Ch23 `thm:yzliu-threshold`, pointer): for $0<\xi<1$, $C^{\overline{\rm MS}}\supset\frac{2a}{1-\xi}\big(L_\zeta+\ln(1-\xi)-1\big)$; for $\xi>1$, $C^{\overline{\rm MS}}\supset\frac{2a\ln(\xi-1)}{\xi-1}$; together $a(L_\zeta-1)\big[\mathcal P\frac1{1-\xi}+\mathcal P\frac1{|1-\xi|}\big]+a\,\mathcal P\frac{\ln(1-\xi)^2}{|1-\xi|}$, $L_\zeta=\ln(4p_z^2/\mu^2)$;
    (f) momentum RGE: $P^z\frac{d\tilde q}{dP^z}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi\big[\big(\frac{1+y^2}{1-y}\big)_+-\frac32\delta(1-y)\big]\otimes f+O(\alpha_s^2)$.
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, def:lamet-schemes, def:prelim-msbar, prop:pdf-partonic-dr, prop:pdf-lo-kernels, def:prelim-plus}`.
  - *Proof plan.* (c), (e), (f): full (algebra from (c) and VF-LAM-8; 0.3 pp). (a), (b), (d): sketch with the one-loop integrals — the momentum-space kernel is the one-loop quasi-PDF of a quark minus $f^{(1)}_{q/q}$ (pure pole in DR, `prop:pdf-partonic-dr`); literature `IzubuchiEtAl2018` eqs. (quasi-matching), (quasi-matching-alt) and App. B (read), `Radyushkin:2018cvn` (read), `Zhang:2018ggy`. Cross-checks to cite in the report: LAMET s1 A/B/C (moments exact for $n\le10$, ratio = Radyushkin, RG), s2 (pairing with Gaussian test functions equals the Fourier transform of the coordinate kernels to $10^{-9}$–$10^{-12}$; claim N27, a verification, not a novelty).
  - *Budget.* 0.9 pp (formula-heavy). Figure `fig:lamet-oneloop` (N6).
* **命题 `prop:lamet-tails` — 单圈准 PDF 的尾部、矩与卷积定义域** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$ qualifier.）At one loop: $C^{\overline{\rm MS}}(\xi)\to-\frac{3a}{2|\xi|}$, $C^{\rm ratio}(\xi)\to\mp\frac{4a}{3\xi^2}$ ($\xi\to\pm\infty$). Hence (i) the $\overline{\rm MS}$ quasi-PDF behaves as $-\frac{3a}{2|x|}\int f$ at large $|x|$ — no zeroth moment (a reflection of $\ln z^2$ at $z\to0$); (ii) in the ratio scheme $\int dx\,\tilde q=1$ exactly (all orders, by construction) but $\tilde q\to-\frac{4a\langle y\rangle}{3x|x|}$, so the first moment diverges logarithmically and the higher ones by power counting; (iii) the $\overline{\rm MS}$ convolution $\int\frac{dy}{|y|}C(\frac xy)f(y)$ converges iff $\int_0|f(y)|dy<\infty$, the ratio/hybrid convolutions need only $\int_0|yf(y)|dy<\infty$; valence distributions satisfy both, Regge-like singlet sea/gluon distributions $y^{-1-\delta}$ ($\delta\ge0$) make the $\overline{\rm MS}$ momentum-space formula ill-defined. `% VF-LAM-9`
  - `\uses{prop:lamet-oneloop}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Full (asymptotics of VF-LAM-8; dominated convergence near $y=0$; 0.3 pp). Checks LAMET s1 E, s5 L2.
  - *Pending.* Candidate 「本书新结果」 (planning marker only; claim N09) **[PENDING-VERIFY]**; until the patch, print as 「本书给出的推导」.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-twoloop` — 非单态准 PDF 匹配核的两圈结果** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$ qualifier; two loops.）The NNLO non-singlet coordinate-space coefficient functions and the corresponding quasi- and pseudo-PDF kernels have been computed independently by two groups (`Li:2020xml`, `Chen:2020ody`; flavour non-diagonal pieces `Chen:2020arf`; master integrals `Chen:2020iqi`; abstracts), "establishing the non-trivial factorization at this order explicitly" (CWZ abstract); they pass the RG check and the threshold check of Ch23 (pointer).
  - `\uses{prop:lamet-oneloop}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Cite; formulas not reproduced. Status sentence: no complete N³LO kernel found (INSPIRE searches, Oct 2026; literature-absence claim A06 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**); the three-loop anomalous dimension is known (`Braun:2020ymy`); NNLO with resummation is used in lattice analyses (`Gao:2021dbh`, `Su:2022fiu`).
  - *Budget.* 0.15 pp.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-polarized` — 极化、横度与扭度 3 准分布的匹配** [P].
  - *Statement.* Helicity ($\gamma^z\gamma_5$) and transversity ($\gamma^0\gamma^\perp\gamma_5$) quasi-PDFs match onto their light-cone counterparts at one loop (cut-off `XiongEtAl2014`; RI/MOM `Lin:2018pvv`, `Liu:2018hxv`†; abstracts). Twist 3: $g_T$ (`Bhattacharya:2020xlt`), $e$ and $h_L$ with essential zero-mode contributions $\propto\delta(x)$ (`Bhattacharya:2020jfj`); twist-2/twist-3 separation for axial-vector (`Braun:2021aon`) and chiral-odd (`Braun:2021gvv`) quasi/pseudo-distributions via Jaffe–Ji-type relations (abstracts).
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, def:pdf-polarized, prop:lattice-renormalizability}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: one-loop matching only; the all-order twist-3 matching including zero modes is open; all sources at abstract level (「据该文摘要」); $\gamma_5$ scheme per `def:prelim-gamma5`.
  - *Budget.* 0.15 pp.

#### §22.7 `sec:lattice-matching` (v1)

* **假设 `asm:lamet-ope` — 类空 Wilson 线算符的短距离 OPE（重整化微扰论）** [A]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (OP1) As $z^2\to0$ at fixed $\lambda$, in renormalized PT,
    $$O^R_{\gamma^\alpha}(z,\mu)=\sum_{n\ge0}\Big[C_n(-z^2\mu^2)\frac{(-i)^n}{n!}z_{\mu_1}\cdots z_{\mu_n}O_1^{\alpha\mu_1\cdots\mu_n}(\mu)+C'_n(-z^2\mu^2)\frac{(-i)^n}{n!}z_{\mu_1}\cdots z_{\mu_n}O_2^{\alpha\mu_1\cdots\mu_n}(\mu)\Big]+(\text{twist}\ge4),$$
    $O_{1,2}$ the traceless symmetric twist-2 quark/gluon operators, $C_n=1+O(\alpha_s)$, $C'_n=O(\alpha_s)$ ($=0$ for non-singlet); with our ITD phase the forward matrix element reads $\sum_nC_n\frac{(i\lambda)^n}{n!}a_{n+1}$ (the sign of $(-i)^n$ depends on the derivative convention: convert, §F0.3 rows 1–2).
  - (OP2) For massless on-shell partonic states there is no other scale: order by order the leading-twist expression holds for all $z$.
  - `\uses{prop:lattice-renormalizability, asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:dis-twist}`.
* **定理 `thm:lamet-coordinate` — 坐标空间因子化（赝分布形式）** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* 「（$R_{\mathrm{cond}}$：在所列编号假设之下证明完整；结论的地位不高于其中最弱的假设。）」「前提：\cref{asm:lamet-ope}（及 \cref{prop:lattice-renormalizability}）。」
  - *Statement.* For partonic states, to all orders in $\alpha_s$ at leading twist,
    $$\tilde h(\lambda,z^2;\mu_R)=\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,\mathcal C\Big(\alpha,-z^2\mu^2,\frac{\mu_R}\mu\Big)I(\alpha\lambda,\mu),\qquad \mathcal C(\alpha,-z^2\mu^2)\equiv\int\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-i\alpha\lambda}\sum_nC_n(-z^2\mu^2)\frac{(i\lambda)^n}{n!},\quad{\rm supp}\,\mathcal C\subset[-1,1];$$
    equivalently $\mathcal P(x,-z^2)=\int_{-1}^1\frac{dy}{|y|}\mathcal C(\frac xy,-z^2\mu^2)f(y,\mu)$ and $\int_{-1}^1dx\,x^n\mathcal P(x,-z^2)=C_n(-z^2\mu^2)a_{n+1}(\mu)$, $a_{n+1}=\int dy\,y^nf(y,\mu)$. One- and two-loop $\mathcal C$: `prop:lamet-oneloop`, `prop:lamet-twoloop`. `% VF-LAM-6`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本定理只涉及部分子态；强子情形见 `thm:lamet-hadronic`，指针。）
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-ope, prop:lattice-renormalizability, prop:lamet-support, cor:lamet-entire, def:lattice-quasi, def:pdf-lightray, thm:pdf-moments-local}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (implication; 0.5 pp), following `IzubuchiEtAl2018` §§II–III (read): (1) multiplicative renormalization (`prop:lattice-renormalizability`); (2) insert (OP1) and take forward partonic matrix elements $\langle p|O^{\mu_0\cdots\mu_n}|p\rangle=2a_{n+1}(p^{\mu_0}\cdots p^{\mu_n}-{\rm tr})$, traces $\propto p^2=0$; (3) resum the tower: $\tilde h=\sum_nC_n\frac{(i\lambda)^n}{n!}a_{n+1}=\int d\alpha\,\mathcal C(\alpha)I(\alpha\lambda)$, where $\mathcal C$ is the Fourier transform of the generating series ("the pseudo-PDF of a target with all $a_{n+1}=1$"); (4) at each order $\mathcal C$ is a finite combination of diagram Fourier transforms (support $[-1,1]$, `prop:lamet-support`) and of convolutions with $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDF counterterms (support $[0,1]$), so ${\rm supp}\,\mathcal C\subset[-1,1]$ and the series manipulations are legitimate (`cor:lamet-entire`); (5) moments at $\lambda=0$ (`thm:pdf-moments-local`).
  - *Status.* Content grade [A] (from `asm:lamet-ope` and `prop:lattice-renormalizability`). SDF uses this theorem for $|z|\lesssim0.2$–$0.3$ fm (`sec:lamet-sdf`).
* **定理 `thm:lamet-parton-momentum` — 动量空间形式与部分子动量** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* R_cond qualifier; 「前提：\cref{asm:lamet-ope}（及 \cref{thm:lamet-coordinate}、\cref{prop:lattice-renormalizability}）。」
  - *Statement.* For $f$ with $\int_{-1}^1|f(y)|dy<\infty$ (for ratio/hybrid kernels $\int|yf(y)|dy<\infty$ suffices, `prop:lamet-tails`):
    $$\tilde q(x,P^z;\mu_R)=\int_{-1}^1\frac{dy}{|y|}C\Big(\frac xy,\frac{\mu_R}\mu,\frac\mu{|y|P^z}\Big)f(y,\mu),\qquad C\Big(\xi,\frac\mu{p^z}\Big)=\int\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-i\xi\lambda}\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,e^{i\alpha\lambda}\,\mathcal C\Big(\alpha,\frac{\lambda^2\mu^2}{p_z^2}\Big);$$
    the argument is the **parton** momentum $p^z=|y|P^z$; the quasi- and pseudo-PDF are two Fourier representations of one function: $\tilde q(x,\frac\mu{P^z})=\int\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-ix\lambda}\int_{-1}^1dy\,e^{iy\lambda}\mathcal P(y,\frac{\mu^2\lambda^2}{P_z^2})$. `% VF-LAM-6`
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, asm:lamet-ope, prop:lattice-renormalizability, def:lattice-quasi, prop:lamet-tails}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.2 pp): insert `thm:lamet-coordinate` into `def:lattice-quasi` at $z^2=-\lambda^2/P_z^2$ and substitute $\lambda'=y\lambda$ ($d\lambda=d\lambda'/|y|$, $\lambda^2/P_z^2=\lambda'^2/(yP^z)^2$, $e^{-ix\lambda}=e^{-i(x/y)\lambda'}$). The distributional identity is checked at one loop (LAMET s2).
  - *Status.* Content grade [A].
* **注 `rem:lamet-early-form` — 以强子动量为变量的早期匹配形式不成立** [V].
  - *Statement.* The early form $\tilde q(x,\mu,P^z)=\int_x^1\frac{dy}y\,Z\big(\frac xy,\frac\mu{P^z}\big)q(y,\mu)$ (`Ji2013`) is wrong at $O(\alpha_s)$: by `thm:lamet-parton-momentum` the kernel depends on $\mu/(|y|P^z)$; replacing $|y|P^z$ by $P^z$ changes $\ell_p=\ln\frac{\mu^2}{4y^2P_z^2}$ by $\ln y^2$, i.e. by a term $\propto a\,(P\otimes\ln|y|f)$ that does not vanish.
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-parton-momentum, prop:lamet-oneloop}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V]; evidence: explicit one-loop calculation (`IzubuchiEtAl2018`, read). Claim E06 **[PENDING-VERIFY]** — this is the authors' own later correction, cited as such (attribution), not an App Q item unless verify decides otherwise. Verify s1 (`batch_E01_s1.md` §7): the exact $O(\alpha_s)$ difference is $\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi\ln|y|\,\frac{1+\xi^2}{(1-\xi)_+}\otimes f=\frac{\alpha_s}\pi\ln|y|\,[P_{qq}-\frac32C_F\delta(1-\xi)]\otimes f$ ($P_{qq}$ normalized as in Ch8); the early integration range $\int_x^1$ is also too small (support $[-1,1]$); the point is explicit in `IzubuchiEtAl2018` and, for quasi-GPDs, already in `Ji:2015qla`.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-cfp` — 光锥规范下的共线减除路线（Ma–Qiu）** [A].
  - *Statement.* In light-cone gauge $A^+=0$ the quasi-PDF of an on-shell massless quark admits the 2PI ladder decomposition $\tilde f=C_0\sum_iK^i$, with the operator and its renormalization entirely in the top kernel $C_0$; with the collinear projector $\hat{\mathcal P}W\equiv\int\frac{d^4k}{(2\pi)^4}\delta\big(x-\frac{k\cdot n}{p\cdot n}\big)\frac{{\rm Tr}[\gamma\cdot n\,W\,\gamma\cdot p]}{4p\cdot n}+{\rm UVCT}$ (the light-cone PDF cut vertex),
    $$\tilde f^{\rm ren}=\Big[C_{\rm ren}\frac1{1-(1-\hat{\mathcal P})K}\Big]\Big[\frac1{1-\hat{\mathcal P}K}\Big]=f_{q/q}\otimes C+O\big((\tilde xP^z)^{-2}\big),$$
    the second factor being the light-cone PDF of the quark and the first collinear (IR) safe; this gives the momentum-space form of `thm:lamet-parton-momentum` independently of `asm:lamet-ope`.
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子态。）
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, prop:lattice-renormalizability, def:pdf-quark, thm:pdf-dglap}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.4 pp): `Ma:2014jla` (read), `JiEtAl2021` §III.B (read): (1) cut-vertex representation with vertex $\frac{\gamma^z}{2P^z}\delta(\tilde x-k^z/P^z)$ (resp. $\gamma^0$); soft divergences cancel between real and virtual contributions; (2) ladder decomposition in $A^+=0$ (`EllisEtAl1979`, `CurciFurmanskiPetronzio1980`); (3) iterate the projector; (4) Feynman-gauge version: in the collinear region $\gamma^z\to\gamma^+/\sqrt2$; $A^+$ gluons attached to the space-like link carry $1/P^z$, compensated by the boost factor, and are removed in $A^+=0$; the regions $R_i$ are subtracted recursively "in a way similar to the BPHZ relation".
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (CFP/Collins-style, cf. Ch9; error type of the power-counting steps E-S). Sketched points: light-cone-gauge spurious poles $1/(n\cdot k)$ in the presence of the space-like link; the claim that $C_0$ has no collinear divergence; the cut-vertex representation of an equal-time operator. **No Glauber or final-state issue** arises: single hadron, no measured final state, fields at space-like separation — the obstruction of Ch12 cannot occur (state this explicitly; for quasi-TMDs see `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`). The RMP adds "while the proof is only for perturbative free quark states, the factorization formulas are widely believed to be true nonperturbatively".
* **定理 `thm:lamet-lcs` — “好的格点截面”的因子化（Ma–Qiu）** [A].
  - *Statement.* For a good lattice cross section,
    $$\sigma_n(\omega,\xi^2,P^2)=\sum_a\int_{-1}^1\frac{dx}x\,f_a(x,\mu)\,K^a_n(x\omega,\xi^2,x^2P^2,\mu)+O(\xi^2\Lambda^2),\qquad K^a_n=\sum_J\frac2{S_a}W^{(J,a)}_n\Sigma_J(x\omega,x^2P^2\xi^2),\quad \Sigma_J=\sum_i\binom{J-i}i\omega^{J-2i}\Big(-\frac{P^2\xi^2}4\Big)^i,$$
    with exact target-mass sums $\Sigma_J$; for partonic states the coefficient functions are entire in $\omega$ (`cor:lamet-entire`), so the statement holds for all $\omega$ order by order. LO examples (TR, `MaQiu2018`): $K^{q(0)}_q=2x\omega e^{ix\omega}$; scalar current $K^{q(0)}_S=\frac i{\pi^2}x\omega(e^{ix\omega}-e^{-ix\omega})$, in momentum space $-2i\frac{x^2\tilde\omega^2}{1-x^2\tilde\omega^2}$ with cuts for $\tilde\omega^2>1$ ("good" only for $\tilde\omega^2<1$).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* Partonic states; the hadronic extension from $|\omega|\ll1$ to finite $\omega$ is `asm:lamet-uniform`(UI-a) (status paragraph).
  - `\uses{thm:dis-ope, thm:pdf-moments-local, cor:lamet-entire, def:lattice-quasi}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: OPE of two local currents for $|\omega|\ll1$ (`thm:dis-ope`); moments via `thm:pdf-moments-local`; analyticity in $\omega$ (`MaQiu2018`: "if we fix $\xi$ at short distance while we increase $\omega$ … we cannot introduce any new perturbative divergence").
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: the route needs only the OPE of local currents of ordinary QCD — not `asm:lamet-ope`; for partonic states it could be upgraded to [R, R_OPE] along the lines of `thm:dis-moments` (decision for the lead author, §F5 item 7). Related programmes (evidence, abstracts): heavy-quark Compton tensor `DetmoldLin2006`; position-space current correlators for DAs at NNLO + twist 4 `Braun:2007wv`; Compton amplitude via Feynman–Hellmann `ChambersEtAl2017`; Euclidean hadronic tensor `Liu:1993cv`; LaMET with current–current correlators `Zhang:2026lle`† (read: abstract and setup) — "simple renormalization properties and [no] linear power divergences", at the price of four-point functions.
* **定理 `thm:lattice-lamet` — LaMET 匹配定理（部分子态、领头幂次、全阶）** [A] (v1 label and tag; v1 title "LaMET/短距离因子化" refined; the v1 power-correction remark moves to `thm:lamet-hadronic`).
  - *Statement.* For partonic states, at leading power and to all orders, $\tilde q_\Gamma(x,P^z;\mu_R)=\int_{-1}^1\frac{dy}{|y|}C_\Gamma\big(\frac xy,\frac{\mu_R}\mu,\frac\mu{|y|P^z}\big)f(y,\mu)$ with an infrared-safe perturbative kernel $C_\Gamma$, established by two independent routes: the OPE route (`thm:lamet-parton-momentum`, content [A]) and the collinear-subtraction route (`prop:lamet-cfp`).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本定理只涉及部分子态；强子层次见 `thm:lamet-hadronic`，内容等级 [C]。）
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-parton-momentum, prop:lamet-cfp, prop:lattice-renormalizability, prop:lamet-tails}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: either route. Literature: `IzubuchiEtAl2018`, `Ma:2014jla`, `MaQiu2018`, `JiEtAl2021`, review `Constantinou:2020hdm`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]; bottlenecks: `asm:lamet-ope` (route A), the light-cone-gauge sketch (route B). Over-claim to avoid: 「准 PDF 匹配定理对强子严格成立」.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-gluon-singlet` — 单态夸克与胶子准 PDF：带混合的匹配** [A].
  - *Statement.* $q_i(x)=\int\frac{dy}{|y|}\big[\sum_j\tilde C_{q_iq_j}\tilde q_j+\tilde C_{qg}\tilde g\big]$, $g(x)=\int\frac{dy}{|y|}\big[\sum_j\tilde C_{gq}\tilde q_j+\tilde C_{gg}\tilde g\big]$ (inverse-matching form, `JiEtAl2021`, read) + power corrections; the operators do not mix under renormalization (`prop:lattice-renormalizability`(c)), mixing arises only in the matching; for Regge-like singlet distributions only ratio/hybrid-type schemes give convergent momentum-space convolutions (`prop:lamet-tails`).
  - `\uses{thm:lattice-lamet, prop:lattice-renormalizability, prop:lamet-tails, prop:pdf-lo-kernels}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]; one-loop kernels: RI/MOM with mixing `Wang:2019tgg`, pseudo-gluon `Balitsky:2019krf`, unified forward/non-forward singlet `Yao:2022vtp` (abstracts) — no independent check in our material, so no [R] item. Title-only items (`Good:2025daz`, `NieMiera:2025mwj`†) are not used for content.
  - *Budget* (both propositions above with this one): 0.5 pp.
* **Table `tab:lamet-dependency` — 匹配定理的依赖结构（Lean 式）.** Rows = the canonical labels of §22.5–§22.7 and `thm:lamet-hadronic`, with columns: content; direct premises (`\uses`); grade of the implication; grade of the statement; weakest premise. It reproduces the frontier report's node table in our labels (N1 = `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`; N3 = `prop:lattice-renormalizability`; N4 = `asm:lamet-ope`; N5 = `prop:lamet-support`; N6 = `thm:lamet-coordinate`; N7 = `thm:lamet-parton-momentum`; N8 = `prop:lamet-cfp`; N9 = `thm:lattice-lamet`; N10 = `prop:lamet-tmc`; N11 = `lem:lamet-fourier`; N12 = `thm:lamet-hadronic`; N13 = `prop:lamet-scheme-validity`). Claim N11 (a presentation, not a theorem; no novelty claim). Remark beneath the table (text, from LAMET §4.5): what a complete proof of `asm:lamet-ope` would require — Zimmermann's forest formula with the anisotropic power counting of the propagator $\theta(x^z)\delta(x^0)\delta^2(x_\perp)$, and care with $\det(in\cdot D)$ and $\delta(0)$-type terms; no counter-indication is known.
* **Figures.** `fig:lattice-matching` (v1 new; FIGURES_INDEX_v2 #28 — update its footer to 「微扰匹配：全阶论证 [A]；强子层次：依赖编号假设 [C]」); optional new `fig:lamet-dependency` (proposal §F4: the graph of `tab:lamet-dependency`, nodes coloured by tag, generated with the App P script restricted to Ch22 or drawn by hand).
* *Budget of §22.7.* 2.6 pp.

#### §22.8 `sec:lamet-schemes`

* **命题 `prop:lamet-hybrid-kernel` — 混合方案的单圈匹配核** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\rm PT}$; one loop.）For $\Gamma=\gamma^0$ and $Z_X$ the zero-momentum matrix element: $\mathcal C_{\rm hyb}(\alpha,z)=\mathcal C_{\rm ratio}(\alpha,z)+\frac{3a}2\delta(1-\alpha)\ln\frac{z^2}{z_S^2}\theta(|z|-z_S)$, and its exact Fourier transform at fixed parton momentum is
    $$C_{\rm hyb}(\xi)=C_{\rm ratio}(\xi)+\frac{3a}2\Big[-\frac1{|1-\xi|}+\frac{2\,{\rm Si}((1-\xi)\lambda_S)}{\pi(1-\xi)}\Big]^{[-\infty,\infty]}_{+(1)},\qquad\lambda_S=z_Sp^z,\qquad\int d\xi\,C_{\rm hyb}=1.$$ `% VF-LAM-3, VF-LAM-10`
  - `\uses{prop:lamet-oneloop, def:lamet-schemes}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.3 pp): for $u=\xi-1\ne0$, $\int\frac{d\zeta}{2\pi}e^{iu\zeta}\ln\frac{\zeta^2}{\lambda_S^2}\theta(|\zeta|-\lambda_S)=\frac2\pi\int_{\lambda_S}^\infty d\zeta\cos(u\zeta)\ln\frac\zeta{\lambda_S}=-\frac1{|u|}+\frac{2{\rm Si}(u\lambda_S)}{\pi u}$ (integration by parts); the bracket decays like $-\frac{2\cos(u\lambda_S)}{\pi\lambda_Su^2}$, so only $u=0$ needs a prescription; the $\delta$ coefficient is fixed by $\int du=0$ because the coordinate-space correction vanishes at $\zeta=0$ (vector-current conservation). Checks LAMET s2 (24 checks, $10^{-12}$).
  - *Pending.* The printed forms of `Ji:2020brr` (eq. hybridm2) and `JiEtAl2021` differ by $3a\ln(\lambda_Se^{\gamma_E})\delta(1-\xi)$ (claim E01, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**, App Q candidate); the book prints the correct form silently with `% VF-LAM-10; E01`; the later form of `Su:2022fiu` (journal eq. (A.14), with $\lambda_S=z_S|y|P^z$) is correct and may be cited. Verify s1 (`verify/claims/batch_E01_s1.md` §2) finds the first published correction in Chou–Chen, PRD 106 (2022) 014507, eq. (35) (key not in `refs_merged_v2.bib`: 「文献待补」): E01 is an erratum with a prior published correction, never a new result.
* **例 `ex:lamet-hybrid-normalization` — 混合核的归一化** (no tag). Derive the $\delta$ coefficient from $\int du=0$ and show $\int du$ of the bracket vanishes; use $\int_0^V\frac{{\rm Si}(v)}v\,dv=\frac\pi2(\ln V+\gamma_E)+o(1)$. (The comparison with the printed prescription is App Q material, after verification.) `\uses{prop:lamet-hybrid-kernel}`. 0.15 pp.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-scheme-validity` — 动量空间匹配在何种重整化方案中成立** [A].
  - *Statement.* Let $X$ be a scheme with conversion $\tilde h^X=Z'_X(z^2\mu_R^2,\mu_R/\mu)\tilde h^{\overline{\rm MS}}$. If $Z'_X(z)$ is perturbative for **all** $z$ that contribute to the Fourier transform, then the momentum-space matching of `thm:lattice-lamet` holds in scheme $X$ with $C^X=\bar Z'_X\circledast C$, where at fixed parton momentum $\tilde{\mathcal C}^X(\lambda)=Z'_X(\lambda^2\mu_R^2/p_z^2)\tilde{\mathcal C}(\lambda)$ and $\circledast$ is the ordinary convolution in $\xi$ on $\mathbb R$ ($\bar Z'_X$ = Fourier transform of $Z'_X$). The hybrid scheme satisfies the condition by construction (`prop:lamet-hybrid-kernel`).
  - `\uses{thm:lattice-lamet, def:lamet-schemes, prop:lamet-hybrid-kernel}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (inherits `thm:lattice-lamet`). Source: `JiEtAl2021` (read), `Ji:2020brr`.
* **注 `rem:lamet-ratio-large-z` — 比值与 RI/MOM 方案在大 $|z|$ 处的动量空间匹配** [C].
  - *Statement.* Momentum-space matching in the pure ratio or RI/MOM scheme with lattice data at $|z|\sim1/\Lambda$ is not justified: $Z'_X$ contains $\alpha_s(1/|z|)$ logarithms, non-perturbative there; "it is not legitimate to use momentum-space factorization for any other scheme that differ from $\overline{\rm MS}$ non-perturbatively" (`JiEtAl2021`, read). The resulting error is a non-perturbative distortion concentrated at the end points. Coordinate-space (SDF) use at short $z$ is unaffected.
  - `\uses{prop:lamet-scheme-validity}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C] (no derivation exists; the claim of validity is unjustified rather than refuted).
* **Table `tab:lamet-schemes`** (from `def:lamet-schemes`: scheme | definition | conversion to $\overline{\rm MS}$ | momentum-space matching | references). **Figure** `fig:lamet-hybrid` (N7; the FIGURES_INDEX_v2 section label `sec:lamet-schemes` is this section).
* *Budget of §22.8.* 0.9 pp.

#### §22.9 `sec:lamet-fourier`

* **假设 `asm:lamet-gap` — 谱隙与边界值正则性** [C]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (GP1) In the channel of the two-point function considered, the external state is an isolated one-particle state of mass $m$, the vacuum and one-particle contributions are separated, and all other contributing states lie at least $\Delta m>0$ above the external state (no one-particle intermediate states, or they are treated separately, Ch23 `prop:yzliu-decay-extensions`).
  - (GP2) The analytic continuations of the ordered two-point functions in complex time have boundary values of moderate regularity near the cuts (Fredenhagen's conditions).
  - (GP3) QCD application: the Wilson-line operator reduces non-perturbatively to local operators $\bar Q\Gamma\psi$ of QCD + static source (cf. `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`), and the heavy–light sector has a gap $\Lambda_X=m_X-m_Q>0$ above the static threshold (confinement; $\Lambda_X\approx0.5$ GeV from $B^{(*)}$ masses, with a renormalon-scheme-dependent split $m_Q(\tau)+\Lambda(\tau)$).
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm}`.
* **定理 `thm:lamet-exp-decay` — 局域性蕴含类空关联函数的指数衰减（Ji–Liu–Su）** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* R_cond qualifier; 「前提：\cref{asm:intro-wightman}、\cref{asm:lamet-gap}。」
  - *Statement.* Let $\phi$ be a hermitian local scalar field obeying the Wightman axioms (locality, spectrum condition, temperedness with power-law bounds — the clauses (W1)–(W3) of `asm:intro-wightman` with $\phi$ in place of the QCD composites) and (GP1), (GP2). Then for the zero-momentum one-particle state
    $$f(\vec z)\equiv\langle\vec p=0|\phi(0,\vec z)\phi(0)|\vec p=0\rangle=2Z+O\big(e^{-\Delta m|\vec z|}\big),\qquad|\vec z|\to\infty,$$
    with $Z$ the $\vec z$-independent constant of the vacuum/one-particle channel (the writer copies its definition from `Ji:2026vir` §5.1); the rate is sharp (saturated by forward-singularity contributions). `% VF-LAM-18`
  - `\uses{asm:intro-wightman, asm:lamet-gap}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (1.0 pp; the edge-of-the-wedge theorem and Jensen's formula are cited as classical mathematics — 「文献待补」 for the classical sources if `Streater:1989vi` does not cover both): (1) insert $P_\Omega+P_1+P_{n\ge2}$ and separate the crossed vacuum channel; (2) continue in complex time $w=t-it_E$: $G_>$ is analytic for ${\rm Im}\,w<0$, $G_<$ for ${\rm Im}\,w>0$, both bounded by $Ce^{-\Delta m|{\rm Im}\,w|}$ (spectral condition, temperedness, (GP1)); (3) by locality the boundary values agree on the space-like edge $|{\rm Re}\,w|<|\vec z|$; the edge-of-the-wedge theorem glues them into one function analytic off the cuts $(-\infty,-|\vec z|)\cup(|\vec z|,\infty)$; (4) the bound is transported to $w=0$ by a conformal map and Jensen's formula (subharmonicity of $\log|G|$), exactly as in Fredenhagen's vacuum theorem (`Fredenhagen:1984bz`); (GP2) is used here. Source: `Ji:2026vir`† §5.1 (read).
  - *Status.* Content grade [C] (from `asm:lamet-gap`). The paper's extensions are Ch23 (`prop:yzliu-decay-extensions`). Terminology note: here "edge of the wedge" is correct (gluing); contrast claim E13 in Ch23.
* **猜想 `conj:lamet-decay-qcd` — QCD 准分布矩阵元的长程渐近形式** [C].
  - *Statement.* In QCD the hadronic quasi-PDF matrix elements decay exponentially with a heavy–light binding energy $\Lambda$; for the pion valence distribution
    $$\tilde h^{\rm LA}(z,P^z)=\big[A_2e^{i{\rm sgn}(z)\phi_2}+A_1e^{i{\rm sgn}(z)\phi_1}e^{-iP^zz}+A_3e^{i{\rm sgn}(z)\phi_3}e^{iP^zz}\big]e^{-\Lambda|z|},$$
    with next-to-leading asymptotics $\propto e^{-\Lambda|z|}/|z|$; nucleon unpolarized: $A_2e^{i\phi_2{\rm sgn}z}e^{-\Lambda|z|}(1+A_2'/|z|+\dots)$; analogous forms for helicity, transversity, DAs and GPDs. `% VF-LAM-18`
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-exp-decay, asm:lamet-gap, asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:lattice-quasi}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: relies on confinement ((GP3)), on the non-perturbative HQET reduction, and on extending the scalar theorem to gauge theories with Wilson lines; support: dispersive analysis, Lorentz symmetry, heavy-flavour spectra (`Ji:2026vir`† §§2–3, read), two-loop $\varphi^3$ checks (Ch23). Used in practice to extrapolate lattice data (`Gao:2021dbh`, `Su:2022fiu`). Over-claim to avoid: 「指数衰减定理在 QCD 中成立」.
* **引理 `lem:lamet-strip` — 准 PDF 的解析带** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* If $|\tilde h(\lambda,-\lambda^2/P_z^2)|\le A(1+|\lambda|)^ke^{-m|\lambda|/P^z}$ for all real $\lambda$, then $\tilde q(x,P^z)$ extends holomorphically to $|{\rm Im}\,x|<m/P^z$; in particular it is real-analytic on $\mathbb R$: no end-point singularities at $x=0,\pm1$ at finite $P^z$; the PDF's $x^a(1-x)^b$ behaviour can emerge only as $m/P^z\to0$. `% VF-LAM-17`
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (uniform absolute convergence on $|{\rm Im}\,x|\le m/P^z-\epsilon$; Fubini + Morera). Makes precise a statement of `Ji:2024oka`. Prototype: a Lorentzian with poles at $\pm im/P^z$.
  - *Pending.* Candidate 「本书新结果」 (claim N10) **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-truncation` — Fourier 截断误差界** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* Under the hypothesis of `lem:lamet-strip` with $k=0$ beyond $\lambda_L$: $\big|\tilde q(x)-\int_{|\lambda|\le\lambda_L}\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-ix\lambda}\tilde h\big|\le\frac{AP^z}{\pi m}e^{-mz_L}$, $z_L=\lambda_L/P^z$. Numbers ($\hbar c=0.1973$ GeV fm, $m=0.5$ GeV): $e^{-mz_L}=0.079$ at $z_L=1$ fm, $0.022$ at $1.5$ fm — not parametrically small at present $z_L$. `% VF-LAM-17`
  - `\uses{lem:lamet-strip}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (elementary). Claim N10 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-slepian` — 有限 Ioffe 时间窗口内可稳定恢复的自由度** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* For a distribution $\mathcal P$ supported in $[-1,1]$, the data $\tilde h(\lambda)$, $|\lambda|\le\lambda_L$, determine $\mathcal P$ uniquely (`cor:lamet-entire`), but the inversion is unstable: the time–frequency limiting operator $(Kf)(x)=\int_{-1}^1\frac{\sin\lambda_L(x-y)}{\pi(x-y)}f(y)dy$ has $\approx2\lambda_L/\pi$ eigenvalues near $1$ and the others decay super-exponentially (Slepian; Landau–Widom $N(\epsilon)=\frac{2\lambda_L}\pi+\frac1{\pi^2}\ln\frac{1-\epsilon}\epsilon\ln\lambda_L+o(\ln\lambda_L)$); numerically the number of eigenvalues $>\frac12$ is 3, 6, 10, 16 for $\lambda_L=5,10,15,25$. `% VF-LAM-17`
  - `\uses{cor:lamet-entire}`.
  - *Proof.* Cite the classical results (「文献待补：Slepian–Pollak 1961; Landau–Widom 1980」 — not in INSPIRE/bib); numbers from LAMET s7. The application to the ITD problem is claim N10 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **例 `ex:lamet-fourier-numbers` — 截断误差与可恢复模式数的数值** (no tag). $e^{-mz_L}$ for $z_L=1,1.5$ fm; eigenvalue counts; $P^z=2$ GeV, $z_L=1$ fm gives $\lambda_L\approx10.1$ ("about six modes"). `\uses{prop:lamet-truncation, prop:lamet-slepian}`. 0.15 pp. Figure `fig:lamet-fourier` (optional).
* *Budget of §22.9.* 1.4 pp.

#### §22.10 `sec:lamet-power`

* **命题 `prop:lamet-tmc` — 靶质量修正的精确形式** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* R_cond qualifier; 「前提：\cref{asm:lamet-ope}（及 \cref{thm:lamet-coordinate}、\cref{prop:lattice-renormalizability}）。」
  - *Statement.* $c\equiv M^2/(4P_z^2)$. (a) $\Gamma=\gamma^z$: $\langle x^{N-1}\rangle_{\tilde q}=K_N\langle x^{N-1}\rangle_q$, $K_N=\sum_i\binom{N-i}ic^i$; with $f_\pm=\sqrt{1+4c}\pm1$, $\tilde q_{\gamma^z}(x)=\frac1{\sqrt{1+4c}}\big[\frac{f_+}2q(\frac{2x}{f_+})-\frac{f_-}2q(-\frac{2x}{f_-})\big]$, inverse series as in LAMET §5.1; particle number conserved; support $[-f_-/2,f_+/2]\simeq[-c,1+c]$. (b) $\Gamma=\gamma^0$: $K^\perp_N=\sum_i\binom{N-i}i\frac{N-2i}Nc^i=K_N-\frac{2c}N\frac{dK_N}{dc}$, $\tilde q_{\gamma^0}(x;c)=\tilde q_{\gamma^z}(x;c)-2c\int_0^1\frac{dy}y\partial_c\tilde q_{\gamma^z}(\frac xy;c)$; $K^\perp_1=K^\perp_2=1$ (no TMC of the normalization and of $\langle x\rangle$), $K^\perp_3=1+\frac23c$, $K^\perp_4=1+\frac32c$. (c) At $O(c)$: $\tilde q_{\gamma^z}=q-c(xq'+q)$, $\tilde q_{\gamma^0}=q-c(xq'+3q)+2c\int_{|x|}^1\frac{dy}yq(\frac xy)$ (= `Braun:2018brg` eqs. (target-parallel), (target-perp) with $v^2=-1$, $(p\cdot v)^2=P_z^2$). (d) Since $c$ is $\lambda$-independent at fixed $P^z$, the leading-twist quasi-PDF is $\tilde q_\Gamma=C_\Gamma\otimes q^\Gamma_M$ to all orders in $\alpha_s$, with $q^\Gamma_M$ the TMC-transformed PDF of (a)/(b): TMC commutes with matching. For the pseudo-PDF the TMC parameter $-M^2z^2/(4\lambda^2)$ varies with $\lambda$; the correction is $O(z^2M^2)$, suppressed as $O(1-x)$. `% VF-LAM-11`
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-ope, thm:lamet-coordinate, prop:lattice-renormalizability, def:lattice-quasi}` (the DIS analogue `prop:dis-tmc`, [R, R_OPE], is a pointer).
  - *Proof.* Full (0.5 pp): $T_N(z)=z_{\mu_1}\cdots z_{\mu_N}(P^{\mu_1}\cdots P^{\mu_N}-{\rm tr})=(P\cdot z)^N\sum_i\binom{N-i}i\big(-\frac{P^2z^2}{4(P\cdot z)^2}\big)^i$ (Chebyshev $U$/Gegenbauer $C^{(1)}$); for $z=z\hat e_z$ the variable is $c$, independent of $z$; for $\gamma^0$ use $u_{\mu_1}z_{\mu_2}\cdots z_{\mu_N}T^{\mu_1\cdots\mu_N}=\frac1Nu\cdot\partial_zT_N(z)$ with $u=\hat e_t$, $u\cdot z=0$; in $x$ space $g\mapsto\int_0^1\frac{dy}yg(x/y)$ divides the $N$-th moment by $N$. Checks LAMET s6 (40/40). (a): `Chen:2016utp` App. B (read); `Alexandrou:2015rja`'s earlier formula does not conserve particle number (`Chen:2016utp`). Size: `Radyushkin:2017ffo` (abstract).
  - *Status.* Content grade [A]. *Pending:* (b) and (d) are candidate 「本书新结果」 (claim N07) **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **例 `ex:lamet-tmc-moments` — $K_N$ 与 $K^\perp_N$（$N\le4$）** (no tag). Compute $K_{1..4}$, $K^\perp_{1..4}$, the $O(c)$ formulas, and explain why $\langle x\rangle$ is TMC-free for $\gamma^0$ ($T^{0z}$ and $g^{0z}=0$). `\uses{prop:lamet-tmc}`. 0.2 pp.
* **引理 `lem:lamet-fourier` — Fourier 引理：$z^2\leftrightarrow\partial_x^2$** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* Let $\rho$ be a distribution supported in $[-1,1]$, $r(\lambda)=\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,e^{i\alpha\lambda}\rho(\alpha)$. At fixed $P^z$ ($z^2=-\lambda^2/P_z^2$): $\int\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-ix\lambda}[z^2\Lambda^2r(\lambda)]=+\frac{\Lambda^2}{P_z^2}\rho''(x)$ as distributions on $\mathbb R$, pointwise on open intervals where $\rho\in C^2$; if $\rho\sim x^a$ ($x\to0^+$) and $\sim(1-x)^b$ ($x\to1^-$), then $\rho''/\rho\to a(a-1)/x^2$ and $b(b-1)/(1-x)^2$. `% VF-LAM-12`
  - `\uses{cor:lamet-entire}`.
  - *Proof.* Full ($\lambda^2e^{-ix\lambda}=-\partial_x^2e^{-ix\lambda}$). Remarks: a logarithmic $z^2$ dependence adds convolutions with the transform of $\ln\lambda^2$ ($-2\gamma_E\delta(x)-[1/|x|]_{\rm reg}$) — logarithmic modifications only; this is the mechanism behind every "$\Lambda^2/(x^2P_z^2)$" statement. Check LAMET s5 L1.
  - *Pending.* Claim N08 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **推论 `cor:lamet-endpoint` — 端点二分** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* If $q\sim(1-x)^b$ and $\rho_4\sim(1-x)^{b+\delta}$ at $x\to1$, the relative correction $\frac{\Lambda^2}{P_z^2}\rho_4''/q$ behaves as $\frac{\Lambda^2}{P_z^2}(b+\delta)(b+\delta-1)(1-x)^{\delta-2}\times$const: $\delta=1$ gives $\Lambda^2/((1-x)P_z^2)$ (renormalon model, `prop:lamet-bvz`), $\delta=0$ gives $\Lambda^2/((1-x)^2P_z^2)$ (the "spectator momentum $(1-x)P^z$" counting of `JiEtAl2021`); at $x\to0$ with $q\sim\rho_4\sim x^a$: $\Lambda^2/(x^2P_z^2)$. The two published large-$x$ counting rules are therefore statements about the unknown end-point behaviour of $\rho_4$, not in conflict. `% VF-LAM-12`
  - `\uses{lem:lamet-fourier}`.
  - *Proof.* Full. Claim N08 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**. The value of $\delta$ in QCD is open (`rem:lamet-open` item 5).
* **命题 `prop:lamet-bvz` — 扭度 4 修正的重整子模型（大 $\beta_0$）** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在大 $\beta_0$（泡链）近似中，」 the Borel transform of the coordinate-space coefficient function has: a UV renormalon at $w=\frac12$, $B[H]\to\frac{-4C_F}{w-1/2}\sqrt X$, which is the Wilson-line linear divergence (absent after normalization); the leading IR renormalon at $w=1$, $B[H^\parallel]\to\frac{-4C_F}{1-w}[\alpha+\bar\alpha\ln\bar\alpha]X$, $B[H^\perp]\to\frac{-4C_F}{1-w}[\alpha+\bar\alpha\ln\bar\alpha+\alpha\bar\alpha]X$ ($\parallel/\perp$ = $\Gamma=\gamma^z$/$\gamma^0$). The model in which the ambiguity is cancelled by a twist-4 term of the same functional form gives $\mathcal I^\parallel(\tau)=I(\tau)+\kappa z^2\Lambda^2\int_0^1d\alpha(\alpha+\bar\alpha\ln\bar\alpha)I(\alpha\tau)$, $|\kappa|\simeq\frac{\pi e^{5/3+2\gamma_E}}{4\beta_0}\simeq1.5$ (indicative, sign undetermined), and in momentum space (`lem:lamet-fourier`)
    $$\mathcal Q^\parallel(x)=q(x)+\frac{\kappa\Lambda^2}{x^2P_z^2}\Big\{\int_{|x|}^1\frac{dy}y\frac{y^2}{[1-y]_+}q\Big(\frac xy\Big)+q(x)-|x|q'(x)\Big\}$$
    with the $[0,1]$ plus distribution (so $q(x)\ln(1-x)$ appears when the integral is written over $[x,1]$); hence $\mathcal Q=q\{1+O(\frac{\Lambda^2}{x^2(1-x)P_z^2})\}$ while $\mathcal P(x,z)=q\{1+O(z^2\Lambda^2(1-x))\}$; normalizing by the $P=0$ matrix element adds $-\frac14\bar xx^2q''/q$ to $R^\parallel$ ($-\frac5{12}$ for $\perp$), a $\Lambda^2/((1-x)^2P_z^2)$ term ($[k]_+$ contains $-\frac14\delta(\bar\alpha)$, $\int_0^1k=\frac14$). `% VF-LAM-13`
  - `\uses{lem:lamet-fourier, cor:lamet-endpoint, def:power-borel, prop:power-renormalon-examples}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch; `Braun:2018brg` (read). The Borel variable $w$: the writer checks on the source that $w$ coincides with our $u$ (§F0.3 row 11).
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: a statement about the bubble chain; the identification of the model with the actual twist-4 matrix element (form, $\kappa$) is a conjecture (`conj:power-renormalon-ht`). Extension to quasi-/pseudo-GPDs and the special point $x=\pm\xi$: `Braun:2024snf` (abstract) → Ch23 `conj:yzliu-gpd-xi` (pointer).
  - *Pending.* `Braun:2018brg` eq. (cR) omits $q(x)\ln(1-x)$ for both $R^\parallel_{\mathcal Q}$ and $R^\perp_{\mathcal Q}$ (claim E03, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**, App Q candidate); print the correct form silently. Verify s1 (`batch_E01_s1.md` §4): cite the journal equation numbers (definition (55), (61)–(64)); the 30 %/24 % sizes refer to their model (60); for $x<0$ the term is $-xq'(x)$, not $-|x|q'(x)$.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-uniform` — 扭度展开对 Ioffe 时间的一致性** [C]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - Notation: $\tilde h_{\rm tw2}$ = leading-twist part (`thm:lamet-coordinate` with the target-mass completion of `prop:lamet-tmc`); $\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ = the twist-4 term of `asm:lamet-twist4`.
  - (UI-a) (coordinate form, used by SDF and by the hadronic reading of `thm:lamet-lcs`) For hadron states and $0<-z^2\le z_0^2$: $|\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}(\lambda,z^2)|\le K(-z^2)\Lambda^2(1+|\ln(-z^2\mu^2)|)^k$ uniformly in $\lambda\in\mathbb R$.
  - (UI-b) (ray form, used by `thm:lamet-hadronic`) Along the quasi-PDF ray $z^2=-\lambda^2/P_z^2$, the remainder $R\equiv\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}-\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ restricted to $|\lambda|\le z_0P^z$ has Fourier transform $o(P_z^{-2})$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$.
  - Writer note (a correction of the frontier report's formulation, not a literature erratum): a pointwise bound of the type (UI-a) does **not** give a $P^z$-suppressed Fourier transform, since $\int_{|\lambda|\le z_0P^z}\lambda^2d\lambda/P_z^2\sim z_0^3P^z$; the twist-4 term contributes $O(P_z^{-2})$ only because of its structure (`lem:lamet-fourier`). Hence the separate ray form (UI-b).
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, prop:lamet-tmc}`.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-twist4` — 领头幂次修正的结构（扭度 4）** [C]. Register entry §F1.1.
