  - (TW1) The $O(z^2)$ term of the hadronic ITD has the form $\tilde h_{\rm tw4}=z^2\Lambda^2\int_{-1}^1d\alpha\,e^{i\alpha\lambda}\rho_4(\alpha,z^2)$ with $\rho_4$ a distribution supported in $[-1,1]$, smooth inside.
  - (TW2) The $z^2$ dependence of $\rho_4$ is at most logarithmic (twist-4 anomalous dimensions).
  - `\uses{prop:lamet-support, cor:lamet-entire}`.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-large-z` — 大距离区域的控制** [C]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (LZ1) $\int_{|\lambda|>z_0P^z}\frac{d\lambda}{2\pi}e^{-ix\lambda}[\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}](\lambda,-\lambda^2/P_z^2)=o(1)$ as $P^z\to\infty$ at fixed $x\in(0,1)$.
  - (LZ2) The same with $\tilde h-\tilde h_{\rm tw2}-\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ is $o(P_z^{-2})$.
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, asm:lamet-twist4}`.
* **定理 `thm:lamet-hadronic` — 强子层次的 LaMET 匹配与幂次修正** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* R_cond qualifier; 「前提：\cref{asm:lamet-uniform}、\cref{asm:lamet-twist4}、\cref{asm:lamet-large-z}（及 \cref{thm:lattice-lamet}、\cref{prop:lamet-tmc}）。」
  - *Statement.* For hadron states, fixed $x\in(0,1)$ and $P^z\to\infty$,
    $$\tilde q_\Gamma(x,P^z)=\int_{-1}^1\frac{dy}{|y|}C_\Gamma\Big(\frac xy,\frac\mu{|y|P^z}\Big)q^\Gamma_M(y,\mu)+\frac{\Lambda^2}{P_z^2}\rho_4''(x)+o(P_z^{-2}),$$
    with $q^\Gamma_M=f+O(M^2/P_z^2)$ the TMC-transformed PDF (`prop:lamet-tmc`); under (TW2) $\rho_4''$ is supplemented by convolutions with the transform of $\ln\lambda^2$ (logarithmic modifications only). Consequently the relative corrections are $O(\Lambda^2/(x^2P_z^2))$ at small $x$ and $O(\Lambda^2/((1-x)^{2-\delta}P_z^2))$ at large $x$ if $\rho_4/f\sim(1-x)^\delta$ (`cor:lamet-endpoint`); at leading power (LZ1 suffices) $\tilde q=C\otimes q_M+o(1)$.
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：`asm:intro-wightman`、`asm:intro-composite`（MA0）。
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-uniform, asm:lamet-twist4, asm:lamet-large-z, thm:lattice-lamet, prop:lamet-tmc, lem:lamet-fourier, cor:lamet-endpoint}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.3 pp): $\tilde q={\rm FT}[\tilde h_{\rm tw2}]+{\rm FT}[\tilde h_{\rm tw4}]+{\rm FT}[R\,\theta(|\lambda|\le z_0P^z)]+{\rm FT}[R\,\theta(|\lambda|>z_0P^z)]$; the first term is the matching formula (`thm:lattice-lamet`, `prop:lamet-tmc`); the second is exactly $\frac{\Lambda^2}{P_z^2}\rho_4''$ (`lem:lamet-fourier`; $\tilde h_{\rm tw4}$ is tempered along the ray because $\rho_4$ has compact support); the third is $o(P_z^{-2})$ by (UI-b), the fourth by (LZ2).
  - *Status.* Content grade [C]. Evidence for the hypotheses: renormalon model (`prop:lamet-bvz`), solvable models (Ch23), exponential decay at fixed $P^z$ (`thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`). *Pending:* the implication is part of the frontier agent's "this work" (claims N07, N08) **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **注 `rem:lamet-domain` — LaMET 预言的适用域** (no tag).
  - The expansion parameter is $\Lambda/(|x|P^z)$ at small $x$ and $\Lambda/(|1-x|P^z)$ at large $x$; the matching itself involves $\alpha_s(2|x|P^z)$; RG improvement at $Q_{\rm eff}=2xP^z$ (`Su:2022fiu`, read): fixed order and RG-improved agree for $2xP^z\gtrsim1.5$ GeV, RG improvement matters for $0.7\lesssim2xP^z\lesssim1.5$ GeV, perturbative matching breaks down for $2xP^z\lesssim0.7$ GeV (empirical numbers, not theorems); in coordinate space the RG-improved coefficients fail beyond $z\approx0.2$–$0.3$ fm. The regions $x\lesssim\Lambda/P^z$ and $1-x\lesssim\Lambda/P^z$ are excluded or need other input (moments, `sec:lamet-sdf`). At fixed order the matching is uniform only on $[x_0,1-x_0]$, $x_0P^z\gg\Lambda$; with threshold resummation the large-$x$ boundary becomes $(1-x)P^z\gg\Lambda$ (Ch23 `thm:yzliu-threshold`, pointer). This is part of the theorem's domain, not a defect. Figure `fig:lamet-validity` (optional).
* *Budget of §22.10.* 1.8 pp.

#### §22.11 `sec:lamet-sdf`

* **命题 `prop:lamet-sdf` — LaMET 与短距离因子化：同一函数的两种展开** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* R_cond qualifier; 「前提：\cref{asm:lamet-ope}（及 \cref{thm:lamet-coordinate}、\cref{thm:lamet-parton-momentum}、\cref{prop:lattice-renormalizability}）。」
  - *Statement.* (a) The quasi-PDF (fixed $P^z$) and the pseudo-PDF (fixed $z^2$) are Fourier transforms of the same function $\tilde h(\lambda,z^2)$ along different lines (R_def content); at leading twist their kernels are related by `thm:lamet-parton-momentum` to all orders. (b) At finite $P^z$ they are different expansions: LaMET expands in $\Lambda/(x(1-x)P^z)$ at fixed $x$ and yields $f(x)$ on $[x_{\min},1-x_{\min}]$, $x_{\min}\sim\Lambda/P^z$; SDF expands in $z^2\Lambda^2$ at fixed $\lambda$ and yields the leading-twist ITD on $[0,\lambda_{\max}]$, $\lambda_{\max}=z_{\max}P^z$ (≈3 for $P^z=3$ GeV, $z_{\max}\approx0.2$ fm). (c) With ITD data up to $\lambda_L$, about $2\lambda_L/\pi$ modes of $\mathcal P$ are recoverable stably (`prop:lamet-slepian`); every sharp reconstruction of $f(x)$ from SDF data involves additional assumptions.
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-coordinate, asm:lamet-ope, thm:lamet-parton-momentum, prop:lattice-renormalizability, prop:lamet-slepian}`.
  - *Proof.* Full for (a), (c) (combination); (b) is a description of the two expansions (no claim beyond `thm:lamet-hadronic`, whose content is [C]).
  - *Status.* Equivalence at $P^z=\infty$: content [A]; the claim that the LaMET Fourier transform "filters higher twist" at fixed $x$ is `thm:lamet-hadronic` ([C]). Literature: `Ji:2022ezo` (read; published title differs from the arXiv title), `IzubuchiEtAl2018`, `Radyushkin2017`, `Radyushkin:2017lvu`, `Radyushkin:2018cvn`, `Orginos:2017kos`, `Karpie:2018zaz`, `Joo:2019jct`, `Joo:2020spy`, review `Radyushkin:2019mye`.
* **注 `rem:lamet-inverse-problem` — 逆问题之争：数学陈述与经验问题** (no tag). `Dutrieux:2025jed` (abstract): with realistic data the inverse-problem uncertainty "remains significant without very restrictive assumptions", the crux at $\lambda\sim5$–15; `Chen:2025cxr` (abstract): LaMET "theoretically solves the ill-posed inverse problem" at fixed $x$, some data qualify for asymptotic extrapolation; `Ji:2026vir`† provides asymptotic forms and error estimates; Bayesian/NN reconstructions `Karpie:2019eiq` (abstract). Assessment (to print): `lem:lamet-strip` + `prop:lamet-truncation` make the fixed-$x$ quasi-PDF well posed *given* a theorem-level tail bound with known constants (only [C] in QCD: `conj:lamet-decay-qcd`); `prop:lamet-slepian` shows that without such information the data fix only $\approx2\lambda_L/\pi$ modes. Both sides' mathematical statements are compatible; the disagreement is about the size of $e^{-mz_L}$-type effects with present data — an empirical question. Over-claim to avoid: 「格点直接计算了 $x$ 依赖」.
* *Budget of §22.11.* 0.6 pp.

#### §22.12 `sec:lamet-offforward`

* **定义 `def:lamet-quasi-gpd` — 准 GPD 与准 DA** (no tag).
  - Quasi-GPD in a symmetric frame ($P$ in, $P'$ out, $\bar P=\frac{P+P'}2$, $\Delta=P'-P$, $t=\Delta^2$, skewness $\tilde\xi=-\Delta^z/(2\bar P^z)$, which tends to our $\xi$ of `def:excl-gpd` at large $\bar P^z$ — convert $\xi_{\rm Ji}=2\xi$, §F0.3 row 15):
    $\tilde F_\Gamma(y,\tilde\xi,t,\bar P^z)\propto\int\frac{dz}{4\pi}e^{iy\bar P^zz}\langle P'|[\bar\psi(\tfrac z2\hat e_z)\Gamma W[\tfrac z2\hat e_z,-\tfrac z2\hat e_z]\psi(-\tfrac z2\hat e_z)]_R|P\rangle$, decomposed into the $H$- and $E$-type structures; prefactor fixed such that the forward limit is `def:lattice-quasi` (writer derives it; conventions of `Su:2026eta` read; frame-independent Lorentz-amplitude definition of `Bhattacharya:2022aob`, abstract).
  - Quasi-DA: $\tilde\phi(x,P^z)$ from $\langle0|\bar\psi(z\hat e_z)\gamma^z\gamma_5W[z\hat e_z,0]\psi(0)|\pi(P)\rangle$, normalized by $f_\pi$ such that $\int dx\,\tilde\phi=1$ (writer derives from `def:excl-da`).
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, def:excl-gpd, def:excl-da}`.
* **定理 `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd` — 准 GPD 与准 DA 的匹配** [A].
  - *Statement.* $\tilde F(y,\xi,t,\bar P^z)=\int_{-1}^1\frac{dx}{|x|}\tilde C\big(\frac yx,\frac\xi x,\frac\mu{x\bar P^z}\big)F(x,\xi,t,\mu)+$ power corrections, with $\tilde C$ independent of $t$ (at least at one loop) and equal to the PDF kernel at $\xi=0$; $\tilde\phi(x,P^z)=\int_0^1dy\,C(x,y,\mu/P^z)\phi(y,\mu)+$ power corrections. LaMET predictions are reliable on $[-1+x_0,-\xi-x_0]\cup[-\xi+x_0,\xi-x_0]\cup[\xi+x_0,1-x_0]$ with $x_0$ set by the hard parton momenta (threshold study `Holligan:2025baj`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* Partonic statement; hadrons as in `thm:lamet-hadronic` (no separate R_cond theorem is formulated for GPDs).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-gpd, asm:lamet-ope, prop:lattice-renormalizability, prop:lamet-support}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: OPE route with conformal non-forward twist-2 operators; the support/analyticity argument extends to non-forward kinematics. One loop (abstracts): unpolarized and helicity `Ji:2015qla` ("no matching is required for $E$ ($\tilde E$)" at one loop, leading power), transversity `Xiong:2015nua`, RI/MOM `Liu:2019urm`, all quasi-GPDs incl. gluons and arbitrary spin `Ma:2022ggj`, coordinate space `Radyushkin:2019owq`, unified NLO `Yao:2022vtp`; meson DA one loop with the GPD paper, RI/MOM `Liu:2018tox` (the quasi-PDF renormalization factor can be used "provided that they are implemented on lattice and in perturbation theory in the same manner"), lattice `Zhang:2017bzy`, `LatticeParton:2022zqc`, `Holligan:2023rex`; NNLO coordinate-space kernels for non-singlet GPDs and the meson DA `Ji:2025mvk`† (abstract; single group).
  - *Status and gap.* [A]; bottleneck `asm:lamet-ope` (non-forward version). Kinematic twist-3 contributions needed for translation invariance are logarithmically enhanced and discontinuous at $x=\pm\xi$ (`Braun:2023alc`, abstract). The point $x=\pm\xi$: Ch23 `conj:yzliu-gpd-xi` (pointer). No [R] item: all fixed-order sources are at abstract level in our material.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-other-objects` — 其他准分布：重子与重介子 LCDA、$\Delta G$、双部分子分布** [P].
  - *Statement.* (i) Baryon LCDAs: one-loop factorization of quasi-DAs "explicitly demonstrated at the one-loop level" with $\overline{\rm MS}$ and RI/MOM kernels (`Deng:2023csv`); (ii) heavy-meson LCDAs: two-step factorization, QCD LCDA = perturbative jet-like function ⊗ HQET LCDA (`Beneke:2023nmj`), LaMET access to the HQET LCDA through equal-time correlators with a dynamic fast heavy quark (`Han:2024fkr`), hierarchy $\Lambda\ll m_Q\ll P^z$; (iii) $\Delta G$ as the large-momentum limit of a gauge-invariant, frame-dependent gluon spin $\vec E\times\vec A_\perp$ (`Ji:2013fga`) and its universality class with only $O(1/P_z^n)$, $n\ge2$, corrections (`Hatta:2013gta`); (iv) double parton distributions: general DPD ↔ quasi-DPD matching for all flavour, spin and colour structures with one-loop quark–quark coefficients (`Jaarsma:2023woo`), colour-singlet unpolarized quark DPDs (`Zhang:2023wea`†). All abstracts.
  - `\uses{def:lattice-quasi, thm:lattice-lamet}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: one-loop or structural statements; the all-order soft/rapidity structure for colour-non-singlet DPDs and the renormalization of multi-link baryon operators are not established; heavy-meson chain combined status [P].
* *Budget of §22.12.* 0.7 pp.

#### §22.13 `sec:lattice-tmd` (v1)

* **假设 `asm:lamet-staple` — 订书钉形类空 Wilson 线算符的重整化与 $L\to\infty$ 极限** [A]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (ST1) For $z\ne0$, $b_T\ne0$, the staple operator $\bar\psi(\frac z2\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2)\Gamma\mathcal W_z(\cdots;L)\psi(-\frac z2\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2)$ divided by $\sqrt{Z_E(2L,b_T,\mu)}$ is multiplicatively renormalizable with a factor that depends only on the end points (heavy–light currents, quark fields): the linear divergences $\propto L$ and the cusp divergences of the staple cancel in the ratio.
  - (ST2) The limit $L\to\infty$ of the ratio exists in every order of PT: the pinch-pole singularity of infinitely long space-like links cancels between the staple self-interactions and $\sqrt{Z_E}$; ultrasoft modes cancel after resummation of dipole self-interactions.
  - (ST3) On the lattice, $\sqrt{Z_E}$ together with the short-distance hadron matrix element removes all UV divergences (RI/MOM fails to remove all linear divergences); same-dimension mixing as in `prop:lattice-renormalizability`(b).
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-aux-renorm, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **定义 `def:lamet-quasi-tmd` — 准 TMD** (no tag).
  - In our conventions (§F0.3 rows 1–3; `Ji:2019ewn`, `Liu:2022nnk` §3, `Ebert:2022fmh` converted):
    $$\tilde f_\Gamma(x,b_T,\mu,\zeta_z)=\lim_{L\to\infty}N_\Gamma\int\frac{dz}{4\pi}e^{ixP^zz}\frac{\langle P|\bar\psi(\frac z2\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2)\,\Gamma\,\mathcal W_z(\tfrac z2\hat e_z+\tfrac{\vec b_T}2,-\tfrac z2\hat e_z-\tfrac{\vec b_T}2;L)\,\psi(-\frac z2\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2)|P\rangle}{\sqrt{Z_E(2L,b_T,\mu)}},\qquad\zeta_z=(2xP^z)^2,$$
    $\mathcal W_z$ = the staple from $-\frac z2\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2$ along $+\hat e_z$ to $L\hat e_z-\frac{\vec b_T}2$, a transverse link to $L\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2$, and back along $-\hat e_z$ to $\frac z2\hat e_z+\frac{\vec b_T}2$ (DY: staple toward $+\hat z$; SIDIS: toward $-\hat z$); $Z_E(2L,b_T,\mu)=\frac1{N_c}{\rm Tr}\langle0|W_\perp\mathcal W_z(\vec b_T;2L)|0\rangle$ (rectangular $2L\times b_T$ loop); $\Gamma=\gamma^0$ or $\gamma^z$ (any combination except $\gamma^0-\gamma^z$). The quasi-TMD has **no rapidity divergence**: $P^z$ acts as an off-light-cone rapidity regulator. ESSZ's "proper" quasi-TMD and their Lorentz-invariant correlator $\Omega(b,P,\epsilon,\eta v,\delta)$ covering the quasi, Collins, large-rapidity (LR), JMY and MHENS schemes (`Ebert:2022fmh`, read) are mentioned in text. Figure `fig:lamet-lattice-geometries`(b),(c); note that `fig:tmd-staple` shows light-like staples (FIGURES_INDEX_v2 §1 note).
  - `\uses{asm:lamet-staple, def:lattice-quasi, def:prelim-wilson}`.
* **定义 `def:lamet-soft-offlc` — 离光锥软函数（Ji–Liu–Liu 归一化）** (no tag).
  - Time-like $v=\gamma(1,\beta,\vec0_\perp)$, $v'=\gamma'(1,-\beta',\vec0_\perp)$ in $(t,z,\vec b_\perp)$, $\beta=\tanh Y$, $\beta'=\tanh Y'$; $W_v(\xi;t)=W[\xi,\xi-\frac t\gamma v]$ (our convention); staples $\mathcal W_v(\vec b_\perp,t)=W_v(0;t)W_\perp W_v^\dagger(\vec b_\perp;t)$;
    $$W(t,t',b_\perp,Y,Y')=\tfrac1{N_c}{\rm Tr}\langle\Omega|T[\mathcal W^\dagger_{v'}(\vec b_\perp,t')\mathcal W_v(\vec b_\perp,t)]|\Omega\rangle,\quad Z(2t,b_\perp,Y)=W(t,t,b_\perp,Y,-Y),\quad S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=\lim_{t,t'\to\infty}\frac{W(t,t',b_\perp,Y,Y')}{\sqrt{Z(2t,b_\perp,Y)Z(2t',b_\perp,Y')}},$$
    $\overline{\rm MS}$-renormalized. $S$ depends on $Y+Y'$ only (boost invariance). One loop: $S^{(1)}=a[2-2(Y+Y')]L_b+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$ (`% VF-YZL-2`; = the time-like, self-energy-included case $\sigma=1$ of `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, VF-TMD-8).
  - Writer trap (print as a footnote): with Collins' space-like lines and self-interactions excluded (`def:tmd-soft`) the rapidity-independent constant vanishes at one loop ($\sigma=0$); individual soft functions are regulator dependent, only combinations are physical (critic C10).
  - `\uses{def:prelim-wilson, def:tmd-soft, lem:tmd-soft-oneloop}`.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-soft-rapidity` — 离光锥软函数的大快度结构** [A]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (SR) As $Y+Y'\to\infty$: $\ln S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')=(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)+\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)+O(e^{-(Y+Y')})$, with $\tilde K$ the Collins–Soper kernel of `prop:tmd-evolution` and $\mathcal D$ finite.
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, prop:tmd-evolution}`.
* **定义 `def:lamet-reduced-soft` — 约化软函数 $S_r$** (no tag).
  - $S_r(b_\perp,\mu)\equiv e^{-\mathcal D(b_\perp,\mu)}=\lim_{Y+Y'\to\infty}e^{(Y+Y')\tilde K(b_\perp,\mu)}/S(b_\perp,\mu,Y,Y')$; one loop $S_r=1-\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi L_b$ (`% VF-YZL-2`). Called the intrinsic soft function $S_I$ in Ch23, where its on-light-cone definition, scheme independence and anomalous dimension $\mu^2\frac{d\ln S_r}{d\mu^2}=\gamma_{HH}$ are established (pointer: `thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft`). $S_r\ne e^{-\mathcal D_{\rm Collins}}$ (trap above).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-soft-offlc, asm:lamet-soft-rapidity}`.
* **假设 `asm:lamet-order-limits` — 极限次序** [A]. Register entry §F1.1.
  - (OL1) $\varepsilon\to0$ commutes with $L\to\infty$ and with the off-light-cone rapidity regulator limit $\tilde\eta\to\infty$ for the ratio $\Omega/\sqrt S$ (self-energies cancel because the staple of $\Omega$ is half that of $S$; exchanges between the halves are exponentially suppressed; eikonal poles $1/(n_B\cdot k)$ give the same result with either regulator — verified at one loop).
  - (OL2) The large-rapidity and the Collins schemes differ only in the order of $\varepsilon\to0$ and $y_B\to-\infty$; by asymptotic freedom this is a UV effect: $f^{\rm LR}=C_q(\sqrt{\zeta_{\rm LR}}/2,\mu)f^{\rm TMD}+O(y_B^ke^{y_B})$, with $C_q$ independent of $y_n-y_B$ (otherwise the non-perturbative CS kernel would enter).
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:tmd-collins}`.
* **定理 `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd` — 准 TMD 因子化** [A].
  - *Statement.* For $b_T\gg1/(xP^z)$, $\zeta_z\gg\Lambda^2$, at leading power,
    $$\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,\zeta_z)\sqrt{S_r(b_T,\mu)}=H\Big(\frac{\zeta_z}{\mu^2}\Big)\exp\Big[\frac12\tilde K(b_T,\mu)\ln\frac{\zeta_z}\zeta\Big]f^{\rm TMD}(x,b_T,\mu,\zeta)\Big\{1+O\Big(\frac{\Lambda^2}{\zeta_z},\frac{M^2}{\zeta_z},\frac1{b_T^2\zeta_z}\Big)\Big\},$$
    $f^{\rm TMD}$ the Collins TMD (`def:tmd-collins`), $2\zeta\partial_\zeta\ln f^{\rm TMD}=\tilde K$. The relation is **multiplicative in $x$** (no convolution), flavour diagonal (no quark–gluon or flavour mixing), spin independent ($H$ depends only on the colour representation), and holds for quarks and gluons. Momentum evolution: $2\zeta_z\partial_{\zeta_z}\ln\tilde f=\tilde K+G$, $G=2\zeta_z\partial_{\zeta_z}\ln H$. Equivalent ESSZ form: $\tilde f^{[s]}_{i/h}(x,\vec b_T,\mu,\tilde\zeta,x\tilde P^z)=C_i(x\tilde P^z,\mu)\exp[\frac12\gamma^i_\zeta\ln\frac{\tilde\zeta}\zeta]f^{[s]}_{i/h}(x,\vec b_T,\mu,\zeta)\{1+O[\frac1{(x\tilde P^zb_T)^2},\frac{\Lambda^2}{(x\tilde P^z)^2}]\}$ with $\gamma_\zeta=\tilde K$ (their proper quasi-TMD contains $S_r$). (b)（单圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）$H=1+a(-2+\frac{\pi^2}{12}-\frac12L_\zeta^2+L_\zeta)$, $G^{(1)}=\frac{\alpha_sC_F}\pi(1-L_\zeta)$, and the coefficient of $\tilde K\ln(\zeta_z/\zeta)$ is fixed to $\frac12$ by the one-loop expressions (`ex:lamet-qtmd-oneloop`). (c)（两圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）the NNLO coefficient (`delRio:2023pse`, read) agrees with the two-loop heavy–light Sudakov kernel of Ji–Liu–Su (values in Ch23 `prop:yzliu-hard-kernel`, pointer). `% VF-LAM-15`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：`asm:intro-bridge`、`asm:intro-composite`（MA0、MA1、MA2）。
  - `\uses{def:lamet-quasi-tmd, def:lamet-reduced-soft, def:tmd-collins, prop:tmd-evolution, asm:lamet-staple, asm:lamet-soft-rapidity, asm:lamet-order-limits, lem:tmd-soft-oneloop, prop:tmd-evolution-oneloop, prop:lattice-renormalizability}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.7 pp) with independent routes: **(A)** Ji–Liu–Liu leading regions (`Ji:2019ewn`, read; a "sketch of mathematical proof", details "to a future publication"): collinear subgraphs identical to the light-front TMD; soft radiation between the fast colour charge and the staple captured by the off-light-cone soft function; hard subgraphs at $\zeta_z$ localized at the two quark–link vertices, since any hard momentum flowing between $0$ and $\vec b_T$ costs a power of $1/P^z$ ⇒ no convolution, in sharp contrast with the collinear quasi-PDF. **(B)** Ebert–Schindler–Stewart–Zhao (`Ebert:2022fmh`, read; all orders, unpolarized quark in detail, gluons and spin by "minor, straightforward modifications"): (1) quasi, LR and Collins beam functions as Fourier transforms of one Lorentz-invariant correlator $\Omega$, leading power keeps the $P^\mu\Omega$ structure; (2) boost by $y_B$: $\tilde b^+=-b^-e^{2y_B}\to0$, $\tilde b^z\to0$, $y_{\tilde P}=y_P-y_B\to\infty$, so the quasi correlator equals the LR correlator with finite-length links up to terms suppressed at large proton rapidity; (3) (OL1); (4) (OL2) ⇒ $C_q=C_q(\zeta_{\rm LR}/\mu^2,\alpha_s)$; (5) no mixing: the quark fields stay space-like separated, so $y_B\to-\infty$ cannot generate a UV counterterm contracting flavour indices (contrast: in the collinear quasi-PDF the fields approach the light cone and mixing occurs); cross-check through the JMY scheme (space-like ↔ time-like continuation). **(C)** SCET (`Vladimirov:2020ofp`, abstract): factorized expression with a "nonperturbative lattice related factor" that cancels in ratios; one loop. **(D)** Coulomb-gauge quasi-TMDs (`Zhao:2023ptv`, abstract): reduce to standard TMD correlators under a principal-value prescription; T-even TMDs only. **(E)** Liu's Collins-style power counting: Ch23 `prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount` (pointer).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (several independent arguments); the decisive step (OL2) is an order-of-limits argument, not a diagram-by-diagram subtraction proof; power-counting steps give E-S statements; no dedicated published Glauber/soft-exchange analysis for finite-length staples (literature-absence claim A06, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**); the hadronic statement needs `asm:intro-bridge`. Over-claim to avoid: 「准 TMD 因子化已严格证明」.
  - *Pending.* The printed exponent $e^{\tilde K\ln(\zeta_z/\zeta)}$ of `Ji:2019ewn` eq. (22), `JiEtAl2021` eqs. (209)–(210) (and `Liu:2022nnk` eqs. (53), (75); `Ji:2021znw` only in its arXiv v1 — the journal version is correct and its footnote 1 notes the omission; verify s1, `verify/claims/batch_E01_s1.md` §3) is wrong by a factor 2 (claim E02, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**, App Q candidate); print the correct form silently.
* **例 `ex:lamet-qtmd-oneloop` — 单圈检验：指数中的因子 $\frac12$** (no tag). With $a=\alpha_sC_F/(2\pi)$: $\tilde f^{(1)}=a[-\frac12L_b^2+L_b(\frac52-L_\zeta)-\frac32-\frac12L_\zeta^2+L_\zeta]$, $f^{{\rm TMD}(1)}=a[-\frac12L_b^2+(\frac32-\ln\frac\zeta{\mu^2})L_b+\frac12-\frac{\pi^2}{12}]$, $S_r^{(1)}=\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$; insert into $\ln\tilde f+\frac12\ln S_r-\ln H-c\tilde K\ln\frac{\zeta_z}\zeta-\ln f^{\rm TMD}$: the $L_bL_\zeta$ and $L_b\ln(\zeta/\mu^2)$ terms cancel iff $c=\frac12$; the constants give $H^{(1)}$ ($-\frac32=-2+\frac12$). The CS ratio of `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` then returns $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$. Checks: LAMET s4, YZLIU `check_qtmd_1loop.py`, spec-v2 drafter by hand (re-derived for this outline). `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd}`. 0.3 pp.
* **命题 `prop:lattice-cs-kernel` — 格点 Collins–Soper 核** [A] (v1 label and tag).
  - *Statement.* At fixed $x$, $b_T$ and two momenta $P_1^z,P_2^z$:
    $$\tilde K(b_T,\mu)=\frac1{\ln(P_1^z/P_2^z)}\ln\frac{C(\mu,xP_2^z)\,\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,P_1^z)}{C(\mu,xP_1^z)\,\tilde f(x,b_T,\mu,P_2^z)}+\text{power corrections},\qquad C(\mu,xP^z)\equiv H\Big(\frac{(2xP^z)^2}{\mu^2}\Big),$$
    equivalently $\frac{\tilde f(\zeta_{z,1})}{\tilde f(\zeta_{z,2})}=\frac{H_1}{H_2}(\frac{\zeta_{z,1}}{\zeta_{z,2}})^{\tilde K/2}=\frac{H_1}{H_2}(\frac{P^z_1}{P^z_2})^{\tilde K}$; $S_r$ and all $\Gamma$-, flavour- and spin-independent factors cancel; the $x$ independence of the right-hand side tests leading power. (单圈内容，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$) the formula returns $\tilde K^{(1)}=-2aL_b$ exactly. `% VF-LAM-16`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：`asm:intro-bridge`（MA1、MA2）。
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd, prop:tmd-evolution}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Full given the theorem (two lines). Literature: `EbertStewartZhao2019` (read; original proposal), `Ebert:2019tvc` (abstract; RI′/MOM for staples, position-space procedure); lattice determinations (evidence): `Shanahan:2020zxr`, `Shanahan:2021tst`, `Schlemmer:2021aij`, `LatticePartonLPC:2022eev`, `LatticePartonLPC:2023pdv`, `Avkhadiev:2023poz`, `Avkhadiev:2024mgd` (abstract: "first such determination with systematic control of quark mass, operator mixing, and discretization effects"), `Bollweg:2024zet`, `Tan:2025ofx` (abstract). The slow convergence of the imaginary part in quasi-TMDWF-based extractions is explained by the linear renormalon of Ch23 (pointer).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (inherits `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`); M17: $\tilde K$ at $b_T\sim1/\Lambda$ is non-perturbative and the lattice supplies it only through the [A] theorem.
* **命题 `prop:lamet-tmd-ratios` — 准 TMD 之比与 TMD 之比** [A].
  - *Statement.* At fixed $(x,b_T,P^z)$, ratios of quasi-TMDs with different $\Gamma$ in the spin-universality class, different flavours or different target polarizations equal the corresponding ratios of TMDs at leading power ($S_r$, $H$ and the CS factor cancel because $H$ is spin and flavour independent and $S_r$, $\tilde K$ depend on $b_T$ only); this justifies the earlier staple "ratio" lattice programme (`Hagler:2009mb`, `Musch:2010ka`, `Musch:2011er`, `Yoon:2017qzo`) in the large-momentum limit (`Ebert:2022fmh`, `Vladimirov:2020ofp`). Spin universality: helicity and transversity quasi-TMDs have the same one-loop coefficient as the unpolarized one, argued for pretzelosity and to all orders (`Ebert:2020gxr`, abstract). Gluon quasi-TMDs: one-loop hard kernel, independent of the pinch-pole prescription (`Zhu:2022bja`, abstract). NLP quasi-TMDs (`Rodini:2022wic`, abstract): twist-2, twist-3 TMDs and a lattice-specific non-perturbative function. Ratios at **different** $b_T$ do not cancel $S_r$ and the CS factor (the frontier report's phrase "$b_T$ at fixed $x$" is a slip; never print).
  - `\uses{thm:lamet-quasi-tmd}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (inherits the theorem); abstract-level items hedged.
* *Budget of §22.13.* 2.0 pp.

#### §22.14 `sec:lattice-status` (v1)

* **推论 `cor:lamet-principle-pt` — LaMET 原理的微扰内容** [A].
  - *Statement.* For partonic external states at leading power, the expansion of `conj:lamet-principle` holds order by order in renormalized perturbation theory for: non-singlet quark quasi-PDFs (`thm:lattice-lamet`), singlet quark and gluon quasi-PDFs with mixing (`prop:lamet-gluon-singlet`), quasi-GPDs and quasi-DAs (`thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`), quasi-TMDs with the reduced soft function (`thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`); explicit at one loop (`prop:lamet-oneloop`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`(b)) and at two loops for the non-singlet PDF kernel and the quasi-TMD hard kernel.
  - `\uses{thm:lattice-lamet, prop:lamet-gluon-singlet, thm:lamet-quasi-gpd, thm:lamet-quasi-tmd}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]; the order-of-limits picture ("IR physics unchanged") is a heuristic; the actual arguments are the OPE route, the collinear-subtraction route and the boost/LR route.
* **注 `rem:lattice-critiques` — 批评与回应** (v1 label; content extended).
  - (a) Power-divergent mixing (`Rossi:2017muf`, `Rossi:2018zkn`, abstracts): higher moments of DIS operators mix with lower-dimensional operators on the lattice; claimed to affect equal-time correlators, reduced ITDs and current–current T-products. Responses: the only power divergence of the non-local operator is the Wilson-line self-energy, which factorizes (`Ji:2017rah`, abstract; `prop:lattice-renormalizability`); after the continuum limit at fixed $z\ne0$ the moments from non-local matrix elements are finite and agree with local twist-2 moments (`Karpie:2018zaz`, abstract; quenched test). The order of limits ($a\to0$ at fixed $z$, then $z\to0$ inside the OPE) avoids the problem; no published refutation of this resolution was found (A06 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**).
  - (b) Euclidean vs Minkowski: `prop:lamet-euclid-minkowski`.
  - (c) The inverse problem: `rem:lamet-inverse-problem`.
  - (d) C12: lattice results supply matrix elements; they are not tests of cross-section factorization.
* **Table `tab:lamet-status` — 第 22 章状态表.** Columns: label | statement (short) | tag (+ qualifier; content grade for R_cond) | premises / bottleneck | error type of the weakest step | refereed? Rows: every canonical statement of this chapter. Generated from the registry §F2 by the writer.
* **注 `rem:lamet-open` — 开放问题** (no tag). (1) A Zimmermann-level OPE theorem for Wilson-line operators (`asm:lamet-ope`); (2) written renormalizability of QCD + space-like static source (`asm:lamet-aux-renorm`); (3) a uniform twist expansion in Ioffe time even in a solvable model (`asm:lamet-uniform`; Liu's GN theorem covers only imaginary Ioffe time, Ch23); (4) exponential decay of hadronic Wilson-line correlators in a gauge theory with usable constants (`asm:lamet-large-z`, `asm:lamet-gap`); (5) the end-point exponent $\delta$ of $\rho_4$ (`cor:lamet-endpoint`); (6) GPDs at $x=\pm\xi$ (Ch23); (7) quasi-TMD factorization beyond arguments: a diagrammatic proof of (OL1) beyond one loop and of (OL2), a Glauber analysis for finite-length staples, the factorization of the large-momentum meson form factor used for $S_r$ (Ch23); (8) coordinate-space analyticity of DR Wightman functions (Ch23 `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt`); (9) higher orders: N³LO non-singlet kernel, NNLO singlet/gluon kernels, NNLO momentum-space quasi-GPD kernels, three-loop heavy–light kernel; (10) all-order twist-3 factorization with zero modes; (11) renormalon prescriptions: universality of $m_0(\tau)$; (12) check $\int d\xi\,C_{\rm hyb}=1$ for the higher-order hybrid kernels used in lattice fits; (13) the asymptotic (non-convergent) nature of the momentum-space power expansion (Ch23 O($N$) model); (14) colour-non-singlet DPDs and heavy-meson LCDA chains.

### Misconception box (Ch22)

## Chapter 23 — Yizhuang Liu 的严格因子化研究 / The rigorous factorization research of Yizhuang Liu

`\label{ch:yzliu}` · `ch23_yzliu.tex` · **15 pp** · frontier format

**Name and biography rule.** "Yizhuang Liu" is never transliterated into Chinese characters (unverified; claim A02); no career or biographical information beyond an affiliation printed in a cited paper (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §2 item 3, §6.9).

**Purpose.** Present, with explicit hypotheses and honest grades, the part of TMD and LaMET factorization theory that Yizhuang Liu (alone and with collaborators) turned into theorem-level statements: (i) the Euclidean realization of TMD soft physics — the off-light-cone soft function as a heavy-quark form factor, the partial-exponentiation lemma, pinch poles and ultrasoft modes; (ii) the analytic structure behind the CSS-type arguments — analyticity in the complex hyperbolic angle, universality classes, the factorization (linearity) of rapidity divergences, the intrinsic soft function, the 2024 time-ordering theorem; (iii) Liu's Collins-style power-counting proof route of the quasi-TMD theorem (stated in Ch22), the hard kernel, LFWF amplitudes, the meson form factor, DY in quasi-TMDs, Sivers ratios; (iv) threshold factorization of the quasi-PDF kernel with the heavy–light Sudakov kernel and the space-like jet function, the anomalous-dimension algebra and the linear renormalon; (v) GPDs near $x=\pm\xi$; (vi) exactly solvable 2D laboratories; (vii) extensions of the exponential-decay theorem. Liu's own assessment, to be quoted (`Liu:2022nnk` §6, read): "all the proofs in the paper, except the ones for the analyticity, follow the typical rigorous standards in the TMD factorization literature"; promoting them to "fully mathematically consistent proofs" requires a multi-scale analysis of singular Minkowski integrals.

**Depends on.** Ch3 (`def:prelim-wilson`); Ch2 (`def:prelim-qcd`, `def:prelim-brst`); Ch5 (`def:sudakov-cusp`); Ch14 (`def:tmd-soft`, `def:tmd-collins`, `rem:tmd-schemes`, `prop:tmd-regions`, `lem:tmd-soft-oneloop`, `lem:tmd-glauber`, `prop:tmd-evolution`, `thm:tmd-factorization`, `thm:tmd-sivers-sign`); Ch19 (`def:excl-gpd`); Ch21 (`def:power-borel`, `prop:power-renormalon-examples`, `prop:power-thooft-model`); Ch22 (`def:lattice-quasi`, `prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`, `prop:lamet-oneloop`, `prop:lamet-twoloop`, `thm:lattice-lamet`, `prop:lamet-lrr`, `thm:lamet-quasi-gpd`, `def:lamet-quasi-gpd`, `asm:lamet-gap`, `thm:lamet-exp-decay`, `asm:lamet-staple`, `def:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `def:lamet-soft-offlc`, `def:lamet-reduced-soft`, `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`, `prop:lamet-tmd-ratios`); App B (`prop:appB-webs`, `prop:appB-cusp-oneloop`). Forward pointer (text only): Ch24 `prop:front-rapidity-renorm`.

**Box 「本章证明了什么／没有证明什么」.**
- 证明了：`lem:yzliu-matrix-tree`、`lem:yzliu-final-state`（数学）；`prop:yzliu-sI-twoloop`、`prop:yzliu-gpd-oneloop`（[R, R_PT]，限于所述阶）；`cor:yzliu-soft-form-factor`（[R, R_cond]，前提 `asm:yzliu-heavy-pair`；内容等级 [C]）；在所述模型中：`thm:yzliu-gn-convergence`（作为 QCD 陈述为 [P]）。
- 给出全阶论证：`lem:yzliu-partial-exp`、`prop:yzliu-pinch-ultrasoft`、`thm:yzliu-soft-hqet`（[A]）；`thm:yzliu-analyticity`、`thm:yzliu-time-ordering`、`thm:yzliu-jet-continuation`（[A]；瓶颈 `asm:yzliu-wightman-pt`）；`prop:yzliu-rapidity-linear`、`thm:yzliu-intrinsic-soft`（[A]）；`prop:yzliu-qtmd-powercount`、`prop:yzliu-hard-kernel`、`prop:yzliu-lfwf`、`prop:yzliu-meson-ff`、`prop:yzliu-dy-quasi`、`prop:yzliu-sivers`、`thm:yzliu-threshold`、`prop:yzliu-ad-algebra`（[A]）。
- 没有证明：`asm:yzliu-wightman-pt` 作为数学命题；普适类的一般陈述（`prop:yzliu-universality-classes`，[P]，瓶颈 `asm:yzliu-lightcone-uniform`）；复时空 Wilson 圈猜想（`conj:yzliu-complex-wilson`，[C]）；大 $\beta_0$ 之外的重整子归一化与强子阈值渐近（`conj:yzliu-renormalon-norm`、`conj:yzliu-threshold-hadronic`，[C]）；GPD 在 $x=\pm\xi$（`conj:yzliu-gpd-xi`，[C]）；$e^+e^-$ 软因子的普适性。
