  - `\uses{def:front-trap}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V], evidence: explicit counterexamples (preprint). The one-dimensional example of App. A is not in VERIFIED_FORMULAS_v2 (verify s1 reproduced its value, `batch_E13_s1.md` §4; add it to VF in the patch before printing a formula). E15 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **推论 `cor:front-coleman-norton` — 动量空间中的 Coleman–Norton 图像（含无质量线与 Wilson 线）** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* 「（$R_{\mathrm{cond}}$：在所列编号假设之下证明完整；结论的地位不高于其中最弱的假设。）」「前提：\cref{thm:front-collins-pinch}。」
  - *Statement.* For a momentum-space graph with lines $k_j=\sum_L\eta_{jL}\ell_L+(\text{external})$, $\eta_{jL}\in\{0,\pm1\}$, quadratic denominators $k_j^2-m_j^2$ ($m_j=0$ allowed) and eikonal denominators $n_j\cdot k_j$, the integration is trapped at real loop momenta $\{\ell_L\}$ iff there are $\lambda_j\ge0$, not all zero, supported on the lines with $k_j^2=m_j^2$ (resp. $n_j\cdot k_j=0$), such that for every loop $L$
    $$\sum_j\eta_{jL}\lambda_j\Delta_j^\mu=0,\qquad\Delta_j^\mu=\begin{cases}k_j^\mu&\text{(propagator)}\\ n_j^\mu&\text{(eikonal line)}\end{cases}.$$
    Equivalently, the displacements $\Delta x_j=\lambda_j\Delta_j$ close around every loop: the pinch is a classical process of on-shell particles (light-like for massless lines; eikonal lines move along $n_j$), off-shell lines collapse to points (hard vertices), zero-momentum massless lines have $D_j=0$ and are always pinched (soft lines). `% VF-FRO-3`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本推论是关于 Feynman 积分的陈述。）
  - `\uses{thm:front-collins-pinch, prop:ir-coleman-norton}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (5 lines): $\partial(k_j^2-m_j^2)/\partial\ell_L^\mu=2\eta_{jL}k_{j\mu}$, $\partial(n_j\cdot k_j)/\partial\ell^\mu_L=\eta_{jL}n_{j\mu}$, so $\sum_j\lambda_jD_j=0$ is the displayed loop equation (factors 2 absorbed into $\lambda_j$); `thm:front-collins-pinch` gives trapped ⇔ Landau point (its hypotheses hold for these denominators); the loop equations are the closure conditions for vertex positions (`prop:ir-coleman-norton`). No Feynman parameters are used, so the objection to the Coleman–Norton *proof* (`rem:front-cn-history`) does not apply. Checks (report): OTHERS `s1(b,d)`.
  - *Status.* Content grade [A] (that of `thm:front-collins-pinch`, preprint). Not given: (i) which vanishing denominators actually trap and the cone of allowed deformations of the others (needed to justify the approximations of Ch9); (ii) pinch surfaces of approximated graphs $t_\sigma A$ (momentum is not conserved between the hard/jet/soft bubbles; Ma's analysis, `prop:front-ir-forest`); (iii) power counting near pinch surfaces (Libby–Sterman, `LibbySterman1978b`; [A], `thm:ir-leading-regions`). *Pending:* claim N03 (K1; inherits the preprint status) **[PENDING-VERIFY]**.
* **例 `ex:front-collinear-pinch` — 无质量自能的共线钳夹** (no tag). Lines $k$ and $p-k$: $\lambda_1k-\lambda_2(p-k)=0$ ⇒ $k=xp$, $x=\lambda_2/(\lambda_1+\lambda_2)\in[0,1]$; both on shell ($x^2p^2=(1-x)^2p^2=0$) iff $p^2=0$; Landau point $(\lambda_1,\lambda_2)\propto(1-x,x)$; displacements $\Delta x_1=\lambda_1k=x(1-x)p=\lambda_2(p-k)=\Delta x_2$ — both partons travel together along $p$. At $x=0$ line 1 is soft ($D_1=0$, always trapped). A single on-shell massive line with the other line off shell is not trapped ($\lambda_1k=0$ forces $\lambda_1=0$; $v$ with $k\cdot v>0$ is a good direction). `\uses{cor:front-coleman-norton}`. 0.15 pp.
* **注 `rem:front-cn-history` — Coleman–Norton 与 ELOP 原证明的缺口** (no tag).
  - `ColemanNorton1965` (abstract level: "singularities on the physical boundary if and only if the relevant Feynman diagram can be interpreted as a picture of an energy- and momentum-conserving process"): the proof works in the Feynman-parameter representation and tacitly assumes "momentum-space pinch ⇔ parameter-space pinch" (diagnosis of `Collins:2020euz` §I, §IV, App. D). For the massless bubble $\mathcal F=-p^2\alpha_1\alpha_2$ has a $p$-independent $\alpha$ dependence, so the $\alpha$ contour is never pinched and the collinear singularity sits in the prefactor $(-p^2-i0)^{-\varepsilon}$; at $p^2=0$ the parametric Landau equations hold identically and the representation degenerates (do **not** write "$\mathcal F\equiv0$, so there is no parameter pinch"). With linear denominators the parameter integral can be pinched independently of the momentum integral (Collins App. D.2, non-relativistic example; the same mechanism applies to an eikonal pair $1/(l^++i0)\cdot1/(-l^++i0)$). For massive graphs the Coleman–Norton proof is accepted except at zero-eigenvalue ("very exceptional") points, whose physical-region status is unresolved. "It is not the result that breaks down, but the proof" (Collins §IV) — the "result" being the pinch criterion and the classical picture, not the literal "singularity iff classical process" (false in massless theories, `prop:front-landau-not-singular`). ELOP §2.1 (`EdenEtAl1966`): avowedly heuristic ("a proper proof needs the use of topology … we shall be content with plausibility arguments", p. 48 as quoted in `Collins2011` §5.4.3 and `Collins:2020euz` §I; A13: check the page).
  - Ch4 prints the results with their tags (`thm:ir-landau-alpha`, `thm:ir-landau-momentum`, `rem:ir-landau-status`, `prop:ir-coleman-norton`); this remark gives the history. *Pending:* E14, E15 **[PENDING-VERIFY]** (all diagnoses are Collins'; verify s1 precision notes applied).
* **命题 `prop:front-ir-forest` — 宽角振幅的红外森林公式（Ma 2020）** [A].
  - *Statement.* For every massless gauge-theory amplitude $A$ with wide-angle kinematics (any number of external legs, Feynman gauge), with approximation operators $t_\sigma$ (hard-collinear and soft-collinear) attached to the leading pinch surfaces $\sigma$,
    $$\Big[\sum_{F\in\mathcal F[A]}\prod_{\sigma\in F}(-t_\sigma)A\Big]_{\rm div}=0,$$
    i.e. all IR divergences, including those generated by the subtraction terms, cancel; with the Ward identity $A$ is rewritten in a gauge-invariant factorized form (soft × jet × hard; `Ma2020` eqs. (1.1), (5.28), read). Hypotheses: wide angle (Glauber region not pinched, by contour deformation; forward/Regge kinematics excluded), massless partons, amplitude level. `% VF-FRO-16`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子振幅。）
  - `\uses{def:ir-pinch-surface, prop:ir-power-counting, thm:ir-leading-regions, lem:method-ward-multi}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: power counting for pinch surfaces $\rho\{\sigma_n\cdots\sigma_1\}$ of repeatedly approximated amplitudes (at worst logarithmic); enclosed pinch surfaces are leading pinch surfaces of $A$ (an "algebra of normal spaces"); case-by-case pairwise cancellation (nested, overlapping, exotic); Ward identities.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: a long refereed all-order argument, not re-derived here; power-counting steps are E-S statements. It generalizes Collins' Sudakov-form-factor subtraction (`thm:method-subtraction`, pointer) to wide-angle amplitudes and refines `prop:sudakov-amplitude-factorization` (Ch5, [A], pointer).
* **命题 `prop:front-coordinate-space` — 振幅的全阶硬—软—共线因子化：坐标空间路线与算符路线** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) (Erdoğan–Sterman; `Erdogan:2013bga`, `Erdogan:2014gha`, `Erdogan:2017gyf`, `Sterman:2025qsq`, abstracts) Coordinate-space pinch surfaces of fixed-angle amplitudes — the collinear and short-distance limits appear as UV divergences — form a finite set of regions per diagram; nested subtractions and Ward identities give hard × jet × soft; products of light-like Wilson lines are multiplicatively renormalizable; webs are integrals of UV-finite functions over surfaces. (b) (`FeigeSchwartz2014`, read) At leading power QCD amplitudes equal matrix elements of products of collinear and soft operators (kinked Wilson lines for the soft-collinear overlap), regulator and gauge independent; soft currents and splitting functions are process independent to all orders. Scope (verified in the text): only final-state collinear directions ("we can ignore Glauber scaling") — consistent with the violation of strict collinear factorization for space-like splittings (`prop:break-spacelike`, [V], pointer).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子振幅。）
  - `\uses{def:ir-pinch-surface, def:prelim-wilson}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (all-order arguments; (a) at abstract level 「据该文摘要」).
* **命题 `prop:front-local-ir` — 被积函数层次的局域红外因子化** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在两圈、对所列电弱产生过程，」 explicit locally finite two-loop amplitude integrands are constructed from IR universality (factorization of soft and collinear singularities at the integrand level) (`Anastasiou:2018rib`, `Anastasiou:2020sdt`, `Anastasiou:2022eym`, `Anastasiou:2024xvk`; preprints `Anastasiou:2025cvy`, `Anastasiou:2026kpm`, `Anastasiou:2026jta`; abstracts).
  - `\uses{thm:sudakov-ir-poles}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: an existence proof of integrand-level factorization at two loops for these processes, not a general theorem.
* **注 `rem:front-landau-algebraic` — 代数几何的 Landau 分析与偶极子公式** (no tag). Landau discriminants (`Mizera:2021icv`), principal Landau determinants (`Fevola:2023kaw`, `Fevola:2023fzn`), relative Picard–Lefschetz hierarchies (`Berghoff:2022mqu`), Whitney stratifications (`Helmer:2024wax`), the $i\epsilon$ of the S-matrix (`Hannesdottir:2022bmo`) — abstracts: algorithms and rigorous algebraic-geometric descriptions of the singular loci of Feynman integrals as functions of complexified external kinematics on all sheets; complementary to `thm:front-collins-pinch`, they do not address physical-region traps of the integration contour with $\lambda_j\ge0$. Dipole formula: exact through two loops (`Becher:2009cu`, `Gardi:2009qi`); non-dipole terms from three loops (`Almelid:2015jia`, abstract) and incompatible with BFKL from four loops (`Caron-Huot:2013fea` §4) — the core statement is `prop:sudakov-dipole-violation` (Ch5, [V]) (A12).
* *Budget of §24.2.* 2.0 pp.

#### §24.3 `sec:front-ope`

* **命题 `prop:front-ope-scalar` — 标量场论中 OPE 的收敛、结合律与弯曲时空** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在欧氏 $g\varphi^4_4$（有质量与无质量）中，在每个固定圈数下，」 (a) the OPE converges at every finite $|x|$ in states of compact momentum support — massive case `thm:power-ope-convergence` (Ch21; bound of `Hollands:2011gf` eq. (7), VF-FRO-4, printed there), massless case `Holland:2014pna` (abstract; new techniques for exceptional-momentum singularities, "tree dominance"); (b) the OPE coefficients obey a UV-finite recursion formula for $\partial_g\mathcal C$ in terms of OPE coefficients only, recursive construction from free-field coefficients (`Holland:2014ifa`, abstract) and strong associativity to all orders with a "coherence theorem" (two-point coefficients determine the $n$-point ones; `Holland:2015tia`, abstract); (c) on arbitrary globally hyperbolic Lorentzian spacetimes, perturbative interacting scalar QFT has an asymptotic OPE to arbitrary order with remainder vanishing at short distances in arbitrary Hadamard states; the coefficients are local and covariant, associative, obey an RG equation and a microlocal condition (`Hollands:2006ag`, abstract). `% VF-FRO-4`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题是标量模型中的陈述。）
  - `\uses{thm:power-ope-convergence}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Cite; architecture of (a) (Wilson–Wegner–Polchinski flow equations with IR and UV cutoffs; inductive bounds uniform in the UV cutoff; a self-reproducing refined induction hypothesis; side result: convergence of the gradient expansion of the effective action at fixed loop order) as summarized in Ch21.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] as a QCD statement (scalar model, refereed CMP results). Caveats from the papers: convergence depends on the state (compact spectator momentum support; worse high-energy behaviour of the state, worse convergence); the loop series itself is not addressed; in Minkowski space convergence is expected only for space-like separations, so nothing follows for the light-cone expansion of DIS beyond moments. The tag of `thm:power-ope-convergence` ([R, R_PT] in v1) is a coordination item (§F5): the frontier mapping makes model results [P] as QCD statements.
* **命题 `prop:front-ope-ym` — 纯 Yang–Mills 理论中 OPE 在规范不变算符上闭合** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在纯 Yang–Mills 理论（半单规范群）中，」 (a) perturbative YM theory on any globally hyperbolic spacetime exists as a formal-power-series algebra of observables (BRST cohomology of an auxiliary gauge-fixed algebra; renormalized Ward identities ensure conservation of the interacting BRST current and nilpotency of the interacting BRST charge), and the OPE closes on gauge-invariant operators; the RG flow does not leave them (`Hollands:2007zg`, abstract and scope; matter fields only discussed — possible anomalies with chiral fermions; QCD with quarks is not constructed there); (b) 「（预印本）」 for any massless superficially renormalizable theory, to arbitrary fixed order, the OPE exists with remainder bound $\lim_{\tau\to0}\tau^{[\mathcal O_A]-D+\delta}\big[\langle\mathcal O_{A_1}(x_1)\cdots\mathcal O_{A_s}(x_s)\rangle-\sum_{[B]<D}\mathcal C^B_{A_1\cdots A_s}(x)\langle\mathcal O_B(x_s)\rangle\big]=0$ for all $\delta>0$ (also in states generated by smeared fields without exceptional momenta); for massless renormalizable gauge theories the OPE coefficients satisfy the BRST Ward identity provided the $E(4)$-equivariant cohomology class $H^{1,4}_{E(4)}(\hat s|d)$ vanishes — true for pure YM with semisimple group, "but not, for instance, in the presence of chiral fermions in non-real representations"; recursion relations, associativity (`Frob:2016mzv`, read §1.1).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题是纯 Yang–Mills 理论中的微扰陈述。）
  - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] as a QCD statement (no quarks); (b) 「（证明仅见于预印本）」 and the bounds it uses (`Frob:2015uqy`) are established for pure YM only. For QCD with quarks the perturbative OPE stays [A] (`thm:dis-ope`; R_OPE qualifier; decision D3, A09, A26).
* **命题 `prop:front-flow-renorm` — 流方程（Wilson–Polchinski）重整化证明** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「对下列理论，」 perturbative renormalizability is proven with explicit bounds by flow equations: massive $\varphi^4_4$ (`Keller:1990ej`); QED with electrons (`Keller:1991cj`, `Keller:1995qn`); spontaneously broken SU(2) Yang–Mills–Higgs with restoration of the Slavnov–Taylor identities (`Kopper:1999qx`, `Kopper:2009zw`); massless $\varphi^4_4$, all-order uniform momentum bounds (`Guida:2011np`, 「（预印本）」); pure SU(2) YM with physical renormalization conditions and IR control of massless correlators (`Efremov:2017sqi`); Euclidean YM with semisimple group and arbitrary composite operators, with BRST Ward identities, tree-weighted bounds, UV and IR controlled (`Frob:2015uqy`). All abstracts (FHH 2016 abstract and journal reference verified by OTHERS).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题是所列模型中的微扰陈述。）
  - `\uses{def:prelim-qcd, def:prelim-brst}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] as a QCD statement: a self-contained flow-equation proof for QCD with quarks is not available — wording 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索（INSPIRE："flow equation" + QCD/fermion），尚未见含夸克的 QCD 的自洽流方程证明」 (A06 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**).
* **猜想 `conj:front-ope-convergence` — Yang–Mills 与 QCD 中 OPE 的收敛** [C].
  - *Statement.* For pure YM and for QCD with quarks, at each fixed loop order, the Euclidean OPE of gauge-invariant composite operators converges at every finite $|x|$ in states of compact momentum support (the analogue of `thm:power-ope-convergence`).
  - `\uses{thm:dis-ope, prop:front-ope-ym}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: Fröb–Holland see "no obstacle in principle" but refined bounds are needed; even if true, the statement is Euclidean (space-like) and perturbative.
* **注 `rem:front-ope-qcd-status` — 对含夸克的 QCD，本书能断言什么** (no tag). (1) Asymptotic OPE to all orders with gauge-invariant operators: [A] in this book (`thm:dis-ope`); the frontier report's "[R] in renormalized PT as a combination of classical results" (Zimmermann normal products `Zimmermann1973`, BRST, `Joglekar:1975nu`, algebraic renormalization `Piguet:1995er`; textbook form `Collins1984`) is **not** adopted (decision D3; REFS_NOTES_v2 §1.4; A09, A26). (2) Convergence: only $\varphi^4_4$. (3) The Minkowski light-cone OPE of DIS comes from the Euclidean OPE plus analyticity and dispersion relations for moments (Ch7). (4) Nothing here is non-perturbative; the OPE as an axiom of QFT (Hollands–Wald programme) is motivated, not derived (`asm:intro-np-ope`). Over-claim to avoid: 「OPE 已被严格证明」 without theory and order.
* *Budget of §24.3.* 1.2 pp.

#### §24.4 `sec:front-regions`

* **引理 `lem:front-jantzen` — 区域展开的主恒等式（Jantzen）** (数学，无标签).
  - *Statement.* Let $\{x\in R\}$ be regions with expansion operators $T^{(x)}$ and domains $D_x$ on which $T^{(x)}$ converges absolutely. Assume (J1) the $D_x$ are disjoint and cover the integration domain $D$; (J2) all expansions commute, $T^{(x)}T^{(x')}=T^{(x')}T^{(x)}$; (J3) every integral below (original, expanded, restricted or not) is regularized; (J4) each expansion still converges absolutely (or is properly regularized) when integrated over all of $D$, and the infinite summations may be interchanged. Then
    $$\int_DI=\sum_xF^{(x)}-\sum_{\{x,x'\}}F^{(x,x')}+\sum_{\{x,x',x''\}}F^{(x,x',x'')}-\dots-(-1)^NF^{(x_1\cdots x_N)},\qquad F^{(x_1\cdots x_n)}=\int_DT^{(x_1)}\cdots T^{(x_n)}I;$$
    the overlap terms vanish when scaleless (each single expansion homogeneous in the expansion parameter with a region-specific power) and are otherwise the method-of-regions counterpart of SCET zero-bin subtractions (`def:scet-zero-bin`, pointer). `% VF-FRO-5`
  - `\uses{def:ir-regions}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.3 pp; inclusion–exclusion over the covering, `Jantzen2011` §3, read). For truncated (finite-order) expansions the summation issue in (J4) is absent.
  - *Remark in text.* Verified by Jantzen in examples (one-loop large-momentum expansion; threshold expansion with hard, potential, soft, ultrasoft regions, convergence checked in his App. A.2; forward scattering without analytic regulators, §7). Jantzen: "I cannot provide a rigorous proof for this convergence issue in the general case" (§3). Checking (J1)–(J4) for multi-loop Minkowski integrals is the open part ([P], `conj:front-mor-minkowski`). Attribution A10: the method is `BenekeSmirnov1998` (threshold expansion); the off-shell Sudakov regions example of Ch4 is `BecherBroggioFerroglia2015` §2.2 (massive exchange: `Jantzen2011` §6).
* **定义 `def:front-facets` — Lee–Pomeransky 表示、Newton 多面体与本质面** (no tag).
  - $G(t,\varepsilon)=\int_0^\infty d^nx\,P(x,t)^{-\delta}$ (up to $\Gamma$ factors), $\delta=2-\varepsilon$, $P=\mathcal U+\mathcal F$ (Symanzik polynomials of `def:ir-landau-setup`). For $P=\sum_{w\in S}c_w\,x_1^{w_1}\cdots x_n^{w_n}t^{w_{n+1}}$ the Newton polytope is $NP={\rm conv}(S)\subset\mathbb R^{n+1}$; an *essential facet* $\gamma$ has inward normal $r^\gamma$ with $r^\gamma_{n+1}>0$ (normalized to 1); the facet operator $\mathcal M_\gamma$ substitutes $x_i\to t^{r^\gamma_i}x_i$ and expands in $t$ (each term defined with an auxiliary analytic regularization). *Euclidean type*: all $c_w>0$. A *region* of the expansion is identified with an essential facet (lower facet).
  - `\uses{def:ir-landau-setup, def:ir-regions}`.
* **命题 `prop:front-lp-facets` — 系数全正时的面展开（Semenova–Smirnov–Smirnov）** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\mathrm{PT}}$：在重整化微扰论中对红外正规化的部分子态严格。）For $P$ with all coefficients $c_w>0$: (i) the dimensionally regularized integral is defined through analytic regularization (generalized Speer theorem) and the Feynman-parametric ($\mathcal U\mathcal F$) and Lee–Pomeransky prescriptions are equivalent; (ii) the leading order of each essential facet's contribution is correct; (iii) the full asymptotic expansion in $t$ is reproduced to all orders when only one facet is essential (`Semenova:2018cwy` §§1–2, 8, read; algorithm of `Pak:2010pt`).
  - `\uses{def:front-facets}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Cite (§8 of the paper); the writer summarizes the convergence analysis of the LP representation.
  - *Status.* The general-order statement with several essential facets is the main conjecture of SSS ([C], `conj:front-mor-minkowski`(a)); positivity of the coefficients is essential — Minkowski kinematics with mixed-sign $\mathcal F$ lies outside the theorem. Relation: `thm:ir-regions-euclid` ([R, R_PT], Ch4; Smirnov's Euclidean asymptotic expansions, `Smirnov:1990rz`, `Smirnov2002`) is the older rigorous core.
* **命题 `prop:front-onshell-facets` — 宽角 Green 函数在壳展开：区域 = Landau 解 = 面** [R, R_PT].
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\mathrm{PT}}$：在重整化微扰论中对红外正规化的部分子态严格。）For massless propagators, off-shell Green functions with non-exceptional momenta and the expansion $p_i^2\to0$: every IR region (hard $H$, jets $J_i$ collinear to $p_i$, soft $S$) is a solution of the Landau equations and a facet of the Newton polytope of $\mathcal U\mathcal F$ (resp. $\mathcal U+\mathcal F$); explicit graph-theoretical requirements on $H$, $J_i$, $S$ are necessary and sufficient for a facet (`Gardi:2022khw` App. C, read); a graph-finding algorithm reproduces the polytope regions. Generic all-order forms of the lower facets for the on-shell and soft expansions of wide-angle scattering and for the mass expansion of heavy-to-light decays: `Ma:2023hrt` (abstract).
  - `\uses{def:front-facets, def:ir-landau-setup, def:ir-pinch-surface}`.
  - *Status.* Facet characterization [R, R_PT]; whether the facet regions are *all* the regions is `asm:front-mor-complete`.
* **假设 `asm:front-mor-complete` — 区域集合的完备性** [C]. Register entry §F1.3.
  - (MR) For the expansion considered, the sum over the listed regions (essential facets of the Newton polytope, or the regions obtained after dissecting the polytope along singular loci), each expanded and integrated over the whole domain with analytic or dimensional regularization, reproduces the asymptotic expansion of the integral to all orders in the expansion parameter: no hidden regions and no non-scaleless overlap contributions beyond those of `lem:front-jantzen`.
  - `\uses{def:front-facets, def:ir-regions}`.
* **命题 `prop:front-commuting` — 多重在壳展开的可交换性** [R, R_cond].
  - *First two sentences.* 「（$R_{\mathrm{cond}}$：在所列编号假设之下证明完整；结论的地位不高于其中最弱的假设。）」「前提：\cref{asm:front-mor-complete}。」
  - *Statement.* In the setting of `prop:front-onshell-facets`, for *jet-pairing soft* regions (each connected soft component attaches to exactly two jets) the non-analytic dependence factorizes into powers of the $p_i^2$; hence multiple on-shell expansions commute. This covers all Sudakov form-factor diagrams and all planar wide-angle diagrams; non-commutativity requires non-planar graphs with at least three on-shell legs and a soft vertex (`Gardi:2022khw` §5, Thm. 4, read).
  - `\uses{asm:front-mor-complete, prop:front-onshell-facets}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.4 pp), reproducing `Gardi:2022khw` §5. If the writer cannot reproduce the argument completely, the statement is downgraded to [A] and reported (WRITER_SPEC_v2 §4.10 item 3).
  - *Status.* Content grade [C] (from `asm:front-mor-complete`). Explicit caveat of the authors (§6): the facet-based method is guaranteed to capture all regions only when there is an analytic continuation from a Euclidean domain (no cancellations in $\mathcal F$); for $2\to2$ massless scattering "we have not provided a proof of this".
* **命题 `prop:front-hidden-regions` — "区域 = Newton 多面体的下表面"在 Minkowski 运动学中不成立** [V].
  - *Statement.* In massless $2\to2$ scattering, from three loops in particular non-planar graphs, cancellations between opposite-sign terms of $\mathcal F$ produce pinches inside the parameter domain (not endpoint singularities); standard sector decomposition and facet-based regions then fail. Dissecting the polytope (linearizing the graph polynomial and splitting the domain at the singular locus) produces new facets, i.e. new regions, sometimes at leading power: in the on-shell expansion they have at least two connected hard subgraphs, in the Regge limit they are Glauber modes (`Gardi:2024axt`, 「据该文摘要」). Independently, a three-loop active–active Glauber region in gap-between-jets box/pentagon integrals is "missed by the available computer codes used to search for regions" (`Becher:2024kmk`, read). Earlier examples of potential/Glauber regions from Landau equations, power geometry and Gröbner bases: `Ananthanarayan:2018tog`; implementation `Heinrich:2021dbf` (abstracts).
  - `\uses{def:front-facets}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V] for the general statement, evidence: explicit three-loop counterexamples (calculation). Consequences: SCET factorization theorems presuppose a complete list of modes (`rem:scet-assumptions`, Ch15; critic C6, M18), and Glauber modes are exactly the regions that hide in parameter space; the method of regions cannot "confirm" leading regions — their identification must come from pinch analysis and power counting (critic C5, misconception box). Over-claim to avoid: 「区域 = Newton 多面体的下表面」 in Minkowski kinematics.
* **猜想 `conj:front-mor-minkowski` — Minkowski 极限下区域方法的有效性** [C].
  - *Statement.* (a) (main conjecture of `Semenova:2018cwy`) For $P$ with positive coefficients the sum over all essential facets gives the full asymptotic expansion to all orders. (b) For Minkowski limits (threshold, Sudakov, Regge, on-shell), with the region set completed by dissecting the polytope and with analytic regulators wherever dimensional regularization does not separate regions (collinear anomaly, rapidity divergences), the method of regions reproduces the asymptotic expansion to all orders — i.e. `asm:front-mor-complete` holds for these classes.
  - `\uses{def:front-facets, prop:front-lp-facets}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]. Proven: Euclidean limits (`thm:ir-regions-euclid`), leading order per facet and the one-facet case (`prop:front-lp-facets`), the conditional identity (`lem:front-jantzen`). Core statement: `prop:ir-regions-minkowski` (Ch4, [P]). Misconceptions M19 (区域方法是定理) and C5.
* *Budget of §24.4.* 1.6 pp. Figure `fig:front-regions-polytope` (N15).

#### §24.5 `sec:front-glauber`

* Background sentence (text): in inclusive DY the Glauber region is leading power but cancels after the sum over cuts and contour deformation (`Bodwin1985`, `CollinsSoperSterman1985b`, `CollinsSoperSterman1988`; `thm:dy-factorization`, [A], with the App O register) — `Collins2011` §14.4.8 (A13: check page): rapidity-graduated exchanges lead "very quickly" into "trying to develop a good and deductive QCD version of Regge theory"; "the current versions of genuine proofs are evidently formidable".
* **命题 `prop:front-glauber-bfkl` — Glauber 有效理论：Regge 化、BFKL 与零箱减除** [A].
  - *Statement.* In SCET extended by the Glauber potential operators of `prop:scet-glauber-operators` (Ch15; the leading-power Lagrangian of `RothsteinStewart2016` eq. (5.21), VF-FRO-6, is displayed there and not repeated): (i) the operator basis is fixed by tree-level and one-loop matching and "there are no loop corrections to this form" (RS §5.3); (ii) rapidity renormalization of the one-loop forward amplitude gives gluon Reggeization, and the rapidity RGEs of the leading soft and collinear functions in forward scattering are the BFKL equation (`thm:smallx-bfkl`); (iii) Glauber zero-bin subtractions make hard-scattering soft/collinear loops insensitive to Wilson-line directions; conversely some Glauber exchanges can be absorbed into Wilson lines by choosing their directions — the EFT version of the CSS "deformation into Wilson lines". The one-gluon Feynman rule of the soft operator $\mathcal O_s^{BC}$ is the Lipatov vertex; the operator also governs emissions of two or more soft gluons. Precursor: a Glauber mode must be added to SCET for consistency of spectator interactions (`Bauer:2010cc`, abstract). The spectator–spectator phase $e^{i\phi(b_\perp)}$ and its cancellation iff the $\Delta p_\perp$ integral is unrestricted (RS eqs. (9.1), (9.18)–(9.19), (11.12)–(11.13); `AybatSterman2009` eq. (14); A11) is treated in Ch12 (`prop:dy-abelian-model`, pointer).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子散射。）
  - `\uses{prop:scet-glauber-operators, thm:smallx-bfkl, def:scet-zero-bin}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (agreement with known results; claim (i) argued). RS: "taken alone it is far from a proof of factorization, even in the abelian case"; RS contain no $e^+e^-$-specific Glauber statement; the BPS field redefinition does not remove Glauber couplings (RS §5.1) (A11). Over-claim to avoid: 「Rothstein–Stewart (2016) 证明了 Drell–Yan 因子化」.
* **命题 `prop:front-syz` — 纯 Glauber 梯形图在单标度红外安全观测量中的相消** [A].
  - *Statement.* For pure Glauber ladder graphs (spectator–spectator exchanges, perturbative spectators), all amplitude-level factorization-violating effects cancel at the cross-section level for every infrared-safe single-scale observable (e.g. $E_T$, beam thrust): $\int dz\,d\bar z\sum_{\rm cuts}(\text{ladders})=0$. Corollary: a leading-power violation needs soft radiation off the Glauber rungs (Lipatov vertex), which links factorization violation to the underlying event (`Schwartz:2018obd`, read).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及微扰旁观者。）
  - `\uses{prop:front-glauber-bfkl, prop:scet-glauber-operators}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: conformal variables $(z,\bar z)$ for the spectator transverse kinematics, in which the phase-space integral is inclusive in $z$ for every single-scale observable (scale invariance of the $2\to2$ amplitudes); explicit NLO check (cancellation between the $O(\epsilon^0)$ two-loop and the $\epsilon\times1/\epsilon$ one-loop terms); all orders: the IR divergences of the ladders exponentiate into a phase (their eq. (53) for the singular part), the remainder is integrable.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] for the stated class. Scope (verbatim): "Our argument does not apply to doubly-differential observables, like those studied in [Zeng]." Dispute (E36, Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**; both sides with scope, no verdict): RS §11.1 claim that spectator–spectator Glauber exchanges "spoil the factorization … starting at $O(\alpha_s^4)$" for beam thrust, transverse thrust and $E_T$ when $\Delta p_\perp\sim Q_*$ is constrained with perturbative spectators — contradicted for pure ladders and single-scale observables by SYZ, who state that they "disprove previous claims"; it survives only for multi-differential measurements.
* **猜想 `conj:front-mpi-violation` — 对多重部分子相互作用敏感的观测量：标准因子化的破坏** [C].
  - *Statement.* For MPI-sensitive single-scale observables in hadron–hadron collisions ($E_T$, beam thrust $B^+_{a,b}$, transverse thrust), standard hard–soft–collinear factorization fails at leading power through soft radiation off Glauber rungs, i.e. through multiple-parton-interaction (underlying-event) contributions.
  - `\uses{prop:front-syz}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]. Evidence: `Gaunt2014` (abstract and SYZ's summary): the CSS cancellation fails at the level of two Glauber gluons exchanged between spectators, related to additional soft scatterings — an argument, not a proof, partly superseded by `prop:front-syz`; the SYZ corollary. For *generalized* factorization (decoupled collinear sectors) of DY with jet vetoes/beam thrust a model counterexample exists: decoupling implies no dependence on the longitudinal spins of the hadrons, but spectator–spectator Glauber exchange gives a non-zero leading-power spin asymmetry (QCD with scalar quarks; `Zeng:2015iba`, abstract) — [V] (model), core statement `prop:break-jet-veto` (Ch18, pointer). Over-claim to avoid (S58): 「Gaunt (2014) 证明了强子 $E_T$ 的因子化破坏」 without `Schwartz:2018obd`.
* **命题 `prop:front-heavy-fsi` — 重末态与旁观者之间的软末态相互作用** [A].
  - *Statement.* Soft final-state interactions between high-$p_T$ heavy particles and spectators are power suppressed for single-particle-inclusive cross sections with an unconstrained recoiling partner (e.g. single-top inclusive in $t\bar t$) but give leading-power effects in multi-particle-inclusive cross sections (incl. pair $q_T$ distributions) without hard recoil; non-perturbative effects are suppressed by $\Lambda/p'_T$ (`Mitov:2012gt`, 「据该文摘要」). Related: small-$q_T$ resummation for $t\bar t$ needs a non-trivial soft factor with azimuthal correlations (`Catani:2014qha`, abstract).
  - `\uses{prop:method-fsi}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (abstract level).
* **注 `rem:front-glauber-status` — Glauber 现状与本书分级的后果** (no tag). Summary table (in text): Glauber cancellation in inclusive DY [A] (unchanged; `thm:dy-factorization`); RS Lagrangian [A] (`prop:scet-glauber-operators`); spectator phase [R] for the eikonal computation as stated (Ch12); pure-ladder cancellation [A] (`prop:front-syz`); MPI-sensitive observables [C] (`conj:front-mpi-violation`); generalized factorization with a spin asymmetry [V] (model); strict space-like collinear factorization [V] at one loop (`CataniDeFlorianRodrigo2012`, abstract; `prop:break-spacelike`; they do **not** show a cancellation and say the effect "may invalidate … starting at the N³LO", A20); collinear factorization of non-inclusive $pp$ cross sections [V] from three loops in pure QCD, from two loops beyond QCD (`ForshawSeymourSiodmok2012`; `prop:break-fss`); SCET reproduction of these violations with Glauber operators (`Schwartz:2017nmr`); two-loop soft current in full colour with potentially dangerous terms when the soft gluon becomes collinear to an incoming parton (`Dixon:2019lnw`, erratum 2024); time-like splitting amplitudes to three loops and space-like ones for three-parton amplitudes (`Guan:2024hlf`; cancellation in pure QCD to NNLO, A20). Misconception M21: the violation of strict collinear factorization is an amplitude-level statement about space-like multi-parton limits and does not by itself make PDF factorization fail.
* *Budget of §24.5.* 1.4 pp.

#### §24.6 `sec:front-sll`

* **命题 `prop:front-sll-all-orders` — 非全局 LHC 观测量的因子化与超领头对数的全阶重求和** [A].
  - *Statement.* For non-global hadron-collider observables (gaps between jets, jet vetoes) with veto scale $Q_0\ll Q$, $L=\ln(Q/Q_0)$, the cross section factorizes as $\sigma(Q_0)=\sum_{a_1a_2}\int dx_1dx_2\sum_{m}\langle\mathcal H_m(\{n\},Q,\mu)\otimes\mathcal W_m(\{n\},Q_0,x_1,x_2,\mu)\rangle$ (hard functions × low-energy matrix elements at $Q_0$, matrices in multiplicity and colour space); the one-loop anomalous dimension contains soft and collinear parts with Glauber phases $\propto i\pi$ (`prop:sudakov-coulomb`), whose non-commutation produces super-leading logarithms $\alpha_s^3L^3(\alpha_sL^2)^n$ (i.e. $\alpha_s^4L^5$ at four loops), resummed at LL to all orders for arbitrary $2\to M$ (`Becher:2023mtx`, abstract): alternating series with terms much larger than the sum; sizeable for $pp\to2$ jets, suppressed for $H/Z(+$jet$)$. The full "Glauber series" (arbitrary numbers of phases $(i\pi)^2$) at LL: `Boer:2023jsy`, `Boer:2023ljq`, `Boer:2024hzh` (running coupling), `Boer:2024xzy` (large $N_c$); all partonic channels of $pp\to2$ jets, some $q\bar q\to q\bar q$ interference terms only $1/N_c$ suppressed (`Becher:2024nqc`); massive final states add a Coulomb phase and a new source of SLLs (`Banerjee:2025kkq`) (abstracts). History: `ForshawKyrieleisSeymour2006` (discovery; first SLL coefficient), `Forshaw:2008cq` (colour-basis independence), `Angeles-Martinez:2015rna` (ordering variable), `Forshaw:2021fxs` (coherence violation in almost all soft-sensitive hadron-collider observables, erratum 2024).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子层次的低能矩阵元与硬函数。）
  - `\uses{thm:jets-ngl-factorization, prop:sudakov-coulomb}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] for the $\mathcal H\otimes\mathcal W$ structure and the LL resummation (EFT derivation, one-loop RG). PDF factorization below $Q_0$ is a separate question (`prop:front-restoration`, [P]). Core statement: `prop:jets-sll` (Ch16, [P]; existence of SLLs).
* **命题 `prop:front-sll-closed-form` — 超领头对数的塔与 $q\bar q$ 单态交换的闭式** [A].
  - *Statement.* Iterating the one-loop anomalous dimension of `prop:front-sll-all-orders` gives the tower
    $$\hat\sigma_n^{\rm SLL}=\Big(\frac{\alpha_s}{4\pi}\Big)^{n+3}L^{2n+3}\frac{(-4)^nn!}{(2n+3)!}\sum_{r=0}^n\frac{(2r)!}{4^r(r!)^2}C_{rn}$$
    with explicit colour traces $C_{rn}$ (`Becher:2021zkk` eqs. (14)–(21), read; checked by the authors with ColorMath to eight loops). For $q\bar q$ singlet exchange in forward scattering ($J_2=J_{43}/2=\Delta Y$, the gap rapidity)
    $$\Delta\hat\sigma^{(S)}=\sum_{n\ge0}\hat\sigma_n^{\rm SLL}=-\hat\sigma_B\frac{4C_F}{3\pi}\alpha_s^3L^3\,\Delta Y\,{}_2F_2\Big(1,1;2,\frac52;-w\Big),\qquad w=\frac{N_c\alpha_s}\pi L^2;$$
    the first SLL is $\hat\sigma^{(S)}_1=\hat\sigma_B\frac4{15\pi^2}C_FN_c\alpha_s^4L^5\Delta Y$ (the $n=0$ term is single-logarithmic); at large $w$, $w\,{}_2F_2(1,1;2,\frac52;-w)/\ln w\to\frac32$ ($1.497$ at $w=10^8$): a slow power-like fall-off instead of a Sudakov $e^{-cw}$. SLLs also arise for $2\to1$ processes (from four loops) and $2\to0$ processes such as $q\bar q\to V$ with a central jet veto (from five loops). `% VF-FRO-7`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子截面。）
  - `\uses{prop:front-sll-all-orders}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch: the tower from the iterated anomalous dimension (cited); the closed form is a series identity, full (term by term; OTHERS `s4`, $n\le8$, and `ex:front-sll-first`).
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (inherits `prop:front-sll-all-orders`). Claim N28 (K5, checked transcription; not printed as a result). The first SLL coefficient agrees with `ForshawKyrieleisSeymour2006` (A20: quoted, not recomputed there).
* **例 `ex:front-sll-first` — 由闭式读出第一个超领头对数** (no tag). ${}_2F_2(1,1;2,\frac52;-w)=\sum_k\frac{(-w)^k}{(k+1)(\frac52)_k}=1-\frac w5+\frac{4w^2}{105}-\dots$; the $k=1$ term gives $-\hat\sigma_B\frac{4C_F}{3\pi}\alpha_s^3L^3\Delta Y\cdot(-\frac15\frac{N_c\alpha_s}\pi L^2)=\hat\sigma_B\frac4{15\pi^2}C_FN_c\alpha_s^4L^5\Delta Y$, the first SLL; the $k=0$ term is the single-logarithmic $-\hat\sigma_B\frac{4C_F}{3\pi}\alpha_s^3L^3\Delta Y$. `\uses{prop:front-sll-closed-form}`. 0.15 pp.
* **命题 `prop:front-restoration` — Glauber 胶子引起的"因子化恢复"** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在三圈、领头对数精度上，」 the low-energy matrix elements $\mathcal W_m$ of gap-between-jets cross sections contain a perturbative active–active Glauber contribution, unambiguously defined in pure dimensional regularization, which at LL has exactly the structure needed to turn the double-logarithmic evolution back into single-logarithmic DGLAP evolution (`thm:pdf-dglap`) below the veto scale; it "breaks soft-collinear factorization at the cross-section level but restores PDF factorization" (`Becher:2024kmk`, read); consistency relations from EFT renormalization conditions, verified by an explicit three-loop computation (`Becher:2025igg`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子层次的低能矩阵元。）
  - `\uses{prop:front-sll-all-orders, thm:pdf-dglap}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (three loops, LL structure). Authors: "our analysis does not amount to a proof of PDF factorization, [but] demonstrates that the breaking of collinear factorization does not necessarily translate into a breaking of PDF factorization." The Glauber region involved is missed by automated region finders (`prop:front-hidden-regions`). Over-claim to avoid (S58): 「间隙截面的因子化已被证明破坏」 — the amplitude-level violation is [V]; PDF factorization of gap cross sections is [C] with restoration evidence.
* **命题 `prop:front-jettiness-cvl` — 1-jettiness 中的相干性破坏对数** [V].
  - *Statement.* For one-jettiness $\tau_1=\frac1Q\sum_k\min\{n_a\cdot p_k,n_b\cdot p_k,n_J\cdot p_k\}$ (`Stewart:2010tn`) in colour-singlet + jet production ($qg\to qZ$ studied), coherence-violating logarithms start at $\alpha_s^4\ln^6(1/\tau_1)$ relative to the Born cross section:
    $$\frac{d\sigma_1^{\rm CVL}}{dx_a\,dx_b\,d\mathcal B}\simeq\sum_{a=q,g}\mathcal A_a\Big(\frac{\alpha_s}\pi\Big)^4(-i\pi)^2\frac1{480}\ln^6\frac1{\tau_1},\qquad\mathcal A_q=\frac83N_c^2T_R^4f^q_Af^g_B\sigma^{(0)}_{qg},\quad\mathcal A_g=-\frac{32}3N_c^2T_R^4f^g_Af^q_B\sigma^{(0)}_{gq}$$
    (cumulant in $\tau_1$, differential in the Born variables; PDFs at $\mu_F=\tau_1Q$; fixed coupling); expected as $\alpha_s^nL^{2n-2}$ for $n\ge4$. Hence the cumulant cannot be written in the standard exponentiated form $\sigma(\tau_1)=\sigma\,e^{-\frac{\alpha_s}{2\pi}L^2(2C_F+C_A)}[g_0(\alpha_sL)+\alpha_sg_1(\alpha_sL)+\dots]$ (`Banfi:2025mra` eqs. (1)–(18), read). Mechanism: the Coulomb phases make $[\mathbf T^2_{ad},\mathbf T^2_s]\ne0$ (the colour of the incoming parton $a$ is resolved from that of its collinear emissions — initial-state angular ordering breaks); the extra logarithm comes from a double-logarithmic soft wide-angle phase-space area that never touches the collinear limit, hence no extra collinear poles — consistent with PDF factorization at $\tau_1Q$. `% VF-FRO-8`
  - `\uses{def:jets-algorithms, prop:sudakov-coulomb}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V] for the all-order validity of the standard (three-Wilson-line soft function) factorization and exponentiation of $\tau_1$ in hadron collisions, evidence: explicit LL coherence-violation calculation (approximations: strongly ordered soft-collinear emissions, multiple-uncorrelated-emission approximation of the measurement, dipole-$k_T$ ordering — the $\alpha_s^4L^6$ term is ordering independent, $\alpha_s^4L^5$ is not). Conflict: `Alioli:2023rxx` state that $\tau_1$ in $V+$jet "does not suffer from superleading or nonglobal logarithms" (basis of N³LL resummation and of $N$-jettiness slicing) — contradicted in its super-leading part only; non-global logarithms are not at issue. *Pending (E16, Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**):* until the verify patch, REFS_NOTES_v2 §1.4 ("single group") applies; verify s1 (`verify/claims/batch_E13_s1.md` §5) reports an independent confirmation of the $qg\to q$ result in SCET with Glauber and new momentum modes by Becher, Hager, Neubert, Schwienbacher, arXiv:2603.12383 = PRL 137 (2026) 121902 (key not in `refs_merged_v2.bib`: 「文献待补」), with the statement that "most existing factorization formulas for global LHC observables must be revised" — print after the patch, with the new key. VF-FRO-8 reproduced independently by verify s1 (the $1/480$ and the colour factors).
* *Budget of §24.6.* 1.1 pp. Optional figure `fig:front-sll`.

#### §24.7 `sec:front-nlp`

* Problem statement (text, 0.2 pp): at subleading power in a scale ratio, SCET factorization formulas contain convolutions $\int_0^1dz\,H(z)\langle O(z)\rangle$ of single-scale functions that diverge at the endpoints once the functions are renormalized/expanded in $d=4$; in SCET$_{\rm I}$-type problems the $1/\varepsilon^n$ poles of the convolution cannot be removed by renormalizing the individual functions ("an unexpected failure of dimensional regularization and the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scheme", `Liu:2019oav` abstract); in SCET$_{\rm II}$-type problems additional rapidity regulators are needed. First seen in exclusive $B$ decays; generic at NLP. Core statements: `prop:thr-nlp` (Ch13, [P]), `prop:power-nlp-endpoint` (Ch21, [P]); endpoint divergences are generic and $\overline{\rm MS}$ renormalization of the individual functions is insufficient ([R] by explicit examples, A28 — stated in words, owned by Ch21). Misconception M22.
* **假设 `asm:front-scet-modes` — SCET 的模式完备性与 Glauber 模式的缺席** [A]. Register entry §F1.3.
  - (SM1) The hard, (anti-)hard-collinear, collinear, soft and soft-quark modes listed for the process reproduce all regions that contribute at the stated power (complete mode set; zero-bin subtractions consistent, `def:scet-zero-bin`).
  - (SM2) Glauber modes do not contribute at the stated power: automatic for decays into colour-neutral final states and for $e^+e^-$ annihilation; for partonic coefficient functions of processes with incoming partons the statement concerns the partonic cross section only (the hadronic version then rests on the leading-power factorization theorem of the process, e.g. `thm:dy-factorization`).
  - `\uses{def:scet-modes}`.
* **定义 `def:front-endpoint` — 端点发散与重因子化条件** (no tag).
  - For a convolution $\int_0^1dz\,H(z)\langle O(z)\rangle$ of single-scale functions, an *endpoint divergence* is a divergence at $z\to0$ or $z\to1$ that appears after renormalization or expansion around $d=4$; $[\![f(z)]\!]$ denotes the terms of $f$ that are leading as $z\to0$ (modulo logarithms); *refactorization (endpoint factorization) conditions* are all-order identities between the endpoint asymptotics of different terms of a factorization formula, which allow the terms to be rearranged into separately endpoint-finite ones (e.g. the conditions of `thm:front-hgg`(b)).
  - `\uses{def:scet-modes}`.
* **定理 `thm:front-hgg` — $h\to\gamma\gamma$（$b$ 夸克圈）的重整化端点因子化定理** [A].
  - *Statement.* Let $\lambda=m_b/M_h$; the $b$-quark contribution $\mathcal M_b$ is $O(\lambda)$ (one mass insertion), with soft-quark exchange between the two photon-collinear sectors. (a) (bare theorem, `Liu:2019oav` eq. (34)) $\mathcal M_b=H_1\langle O_1\rangle+\int_0^1dz_1\,H_2(z_1)\langle O_2(z_1)\rangle+(z_1\to z_2)+H_3\langle O_3\rangle$, where $\langle O_3\rangle$ is the convolution of two radiative jet functions $J$ and a soft-quark function $S$ in $\ell_\pm$; with an analytic (rapidity) regulator $\eta$ the convolutions are defined (expand in $\eta$ before $\varepsilon$). (b) (refactorization conditions, proven in $D$ dimensions within SCET, `Liu:2020wbn` §3, the second via the discontinuity of the soft function)
    $$[\![\bar H_2(z)]\!]=-H_3\,J(zM_h^2),\qquad[\![\langle O_2(z)\rangle]\!]=-\frac{g_\perp^{\mu\nu}}2\int_0^\infty\frac{d\ell_+}{\ell_+}J(-\bar n_1\cdot k_1\,\ell_+)\,S(\ell_+\ell_-)\Big|_{z=\ell_-/\bar n_1\cdot k_1};$$
    they imply the cancellation of the rapidity-regulator dependence to all orders. (c) (renormalized theorem, `Liu:2020wbn` eq. (4.1), read; $\bar z=1-z$)
    $$\mathcal M_b=H_1(\mu)\langle O_1(\mu)\rangle+2\int_0^1dz\Big[H_2(z,\mu)\langle O_2(z,\mu)\rangle-\frac{[\![\bar H_2(z,\mu)]\!]}{z}[\![\langle O_2(z,\mu)\rangle]\!]-\frac{[\![\bar H_2(\bar z,\mu)]\!]}{\bar z}[\![\langle O_2(\bar z,\mu)\rangle]\!]\Big]+g_\perp^{\mu\nu}\lim_{\sigma\to-1}H_3(\mu)\int_0^{M_h}\frac{d\ell_-}{\ell_-}\int_0^{\sigma M_h}\frac{d\ell_+}{\ell_+}J(M_h\ell_-,\mu)J(-M_h\ell_+,\mu)S(\ell_+\ell_-,\mu)\Big|_{\rm LP}$$
    holds to all orders; the non-commutativity of endpoint cutoffs and renormalization is absorbed into $H_1(\mu)$; all ingredients obey RGEs (most anomalous dimensions known at two loops); the theorem predicts the three-loop logarithms $O(\alpha_b\alpha_s^2L^k)$, $k=6,5,4,3$, $L=\ln(-M_h^2/m_b^2)$. The writer copies the definitions of $H_i$, $\bar H_2$, $O_i$, $J$, $S$ from the sources (SCET conventions, §F0.3 row 16). `% VF-FRO-9`
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本定理只涉及部分子振幅。）
  - `\uses{asm:front-scet-modes, def:front-endpoint, prop:scet-scet2}`.
  - *Proof plan.* Sketch (0.4 pp): operator basis and matching; the endpoint behaviour of $H_2$ and $\langle O_2\rangle$; the $D$-dimensional conditions (b); rearrangement with subtractions in the $T_2$ term and cutoffs in the $T_3$ term; RG consistency.
  - *Status and gap.* [A]: all-order SCET argument with refactorization conditions proven within SCET; weakest step: the SCET assumptions `asm:front-scet-modes` (colour-neutral final state, no Glauber issue) (A28). Earlier in `Liu:2019oav` the conditions were verified at NLO and conjectured to all orders. The predicted three-loop logarithms agree with a numerical computation and **disagree** with earlier conventional predictions of the subleading-logarithm series (as stated by `Liu:2020wbn`; E34, Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**; no verdict).
  - *Budget.* 0.5 pp. Figure `fig:front-endpoint` (N16).
* **例 `ex:front-hgg-ldl` — 领头双对数与 ${}_2F_2$** (no tag). $\mathcal M_b|_{\rm LDL}=\mathcal M_0\hat L^2\sum_n\frac{n!}{(2n+2)!}\big(-\frac{C_F\alpha_s}{2\pi}\hat L^2\big)^n=\mathcal M_0\frac{\hat L^2}2\,{}_2F_2\big(1,1;\frac32,2;-\frac{C_F\alpha_s}{8\pi}\hat L^2\big)$ (`Kotsky:1997rq`, `Akhoury:2001mz`; three loops `Penin:2014msa`, `Liu:2017vkm`, `Liu:2018czl`; as quoted in `Liu:2019oav` eq. (3)). Identity: $(\frac32)_k=\frac{(2k+1)!}{4^kk!}$ gives $\frac12\frac{k!}{(\frac32)_k(k+1)!}4^{-k}=\frac{k!}{(2k+2)!}$. Match to NLO: $n=0$ gives $\frac{\hat L^2}2$, $n=1$ gives $-\frac{C_F\alpha_s}{48\pi}\hat L^4=\frac{C_F\alpha_s}{4\pi}(-\frac{\hat L^4}{12})$, the exact NLO amplitude (`Liu:2019oav` eq. (1)). Reproduced by `thm:front-hgg` (OTHERS `s4`; N28). `\uses{thm:front-hgg}`. 0.2 pp.
* **命题 `prop:front-ggh` — 轻夸克诱导的 $gg\to h$ 与重因子化减除方案** [A].
  - *Statement.* The same structure holds with external colour for $gg\to h$ via light-quark loops; the refactorization-based subtraction (RBS) scheme yields a factorization theorem free of endpoint divergences; the three-loop logarithms $\alpha_s^3\ln^k$, $k=6,\dots,3$, are predicted and the first three towers $\alpha_s^n\ln^{2n-k}(-M_h^2/m_b^2)$, $k=0,1,2$, resummed (`Liu:2022ajh`, abstract). Precursors in conventional QCD: all-order LL for mass-suppressed amplitudes (`Liu:2017vkm`, `Liu:2018czl`, abstracts). Position-space SCET analyses of individual ingredients (abstracts): analyticity, renormalization and evolution of the soft-quark function (`Bodwin:2021cpx`); renormalization of the radiative jet function (`Bodwin:2021epw`); gauge invariance of radiative jet functions (`Bodwin:2023asf`); "hidden self-energy contributions of collinear functions" (`Bodwin:2025zzi`, 「（预印本）」). A rapidity-divergence-free definition of the $gg\to h$ soft-quark operator and an all-order relation (conformal symmetry) between the $\gamma\gamma\to h$ soft kernel and the $B$-meson LCDA kernel, bootstrapped to two loops (`Beneke:2024cpq`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子振幅。）
  - `\uses{thm:front-hgg, asm:front-scet-modes}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (abstract level; A28).
* **命题 `prop:front-nlp-threshold` — 次领头幂次阈值 Drell–Yan 的 $d$ 维因子化** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在维数正规子移去之前（$d$ 维），」 for $q\bar q\to\gamma^*+X$ near $z=Q^2/\hat s\to1$ there is a general factorization at any subleading power, with NLP collinear functions defined by operator matching and checked against the method of regions to NNLO; "factorization holds only before the dimensional regulator is removed, due to a divergent convolution" when collinear and soft functions are expanded around $d=4$ first — an obstacle to NLP resummation beyond LL (`Beneke:2019oqx`, 「据该文摘要」).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子截面。）
  - `\uses{def:thr-limit, thm:thr-refactorization, def:front-endpoint}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (bare, $d$-dimensional; A28). Refines `prop:thr-nlp` (Ch13, pointer).
* **命题 `prop:front-nlp-ll` — 次领头幂次的领头对数重求和与端点因子化条件** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在领头对数精度上，」 (a) threshold resummation at NLP for DY (`Beneke:2018gvs`) and for $gg\to H$ (`Beneke:2019mua`) (abstracts); (b) off-diagonal DIS at large $x$ from $d$-dimensional refactorization: the $1/\varepsilon$-pole cancellation between regions bootstraps the LL series and reproduces Vogt's Bernoulli-number series for the off-diagonal DGLAP kernel at large $x$; off-diagonal splitting functions and soft-quark Sudakov logarithms are two-scale objects (`Beneke:2020ibj`, abstract); (c) "gluon thrust" (the power-suppressed $q\bar q$ vs gluon-jet channel of thrust): operatorial endpoint factorization conditions — the $r\to0$ limit of the B-type (anti-collinear) term equals the $\omega\to\infty$ limit of the A-type (soft) term to all orders, a "prerequisite for endpoint factorization", checked at $O(\alpha_s)$; with them a factorization formula whose terms are separately endpoint-finite and expressed through renormalized four-dimensional functions, and the first RG resummation of an endpoint-divergent SCET$_{\rm I}$ observable at LL, agreeing with the earlier $d$-dimensional result (`Beneke:2022obx`, read §§1–2, 4); (d) NLP soft function of the DY $qg$ channel, renormalization and RGE (`Beneke:2025ufd`, abstract). Related (abstracts): next-to-eikonal/generalized Low–Burnett–Kroll factorization for NLP threshold logarithms (`Bonocore:2015esa`, `Bonocore:2016awd`; N³LO checks `Bahjat-Abbas:2018hpv`); first subleading-power resummation of an event shape (thrust in $H\to gg$, LL; `Moult:2018jjd`) and radiative functions (`Moult:2019mog`).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子截面与系数函数。）
  - `\uses{asm:front-scet-modes, def:front-endpoint, thm:thr-refactorization}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] at LL only (all-order endpoint conditions argued, verified at one loop in (c)); beyond LL: `conj:front-nlp-beyond-ll`.
* **命题 `prop:front-nlp-dis` — 次领头幂次 DIS 与 PDF 因子化：阈值 PDF 的双标度结构** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在领头对数精度上（预印本），」 position-space SCET factorization of the off-diagonal DIS channel at NLP in $1-x$ (leading twist): the anti-hard-collinear B1-current and soft-collinear (subleading-Lagrangian) contributions have endpoint divergences resolved by endpoint factorization relations; the PDF near threshold becomes a two-scale object at NLP; at leading power the threshold PDFs coincide with the $\xi\to1$ limit of the $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs and $\overline{\rm MS}$ renormalization commutes with the endpoint limit; LL resummation both in $d$-dimensional $\overline{\rm MS}$ and in a refactorization "endpoint scheme", with the all-order relation between the two PDF definitions to double-logarithmic accuracy (`Beneke:2026miy`, 「（预印本）」, read: abstract, conclusion).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子系数函数；强子层次见 `thm:pdf-dis-xspace`。）
  - `\uses{asm:front-scet-modes, def:front-endpoint, def:pdf-quark, thm:pdf-dis-xspace}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] at LL, 「（证明仅见于预印本）」 (A28).
* **猜想 `conj:front-nlp-beyond-ll` — 超出领头对数的次领头幂次端点因子化** [C].
  - *Statement.* For endpoint-divergent cross sections with incoming hadrons (threshold DY and DIS off-diagonal channels, event shapes with power-suppressed channels), NLP factorization with endpoint refactorization holds beyond LL with renormalized four-dimensional functions, and the refactorization conditions follow from a general theorem.
  - `\uses{def:front-endpoint, prop:front-nlp-ll}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C] (open; OTHERS §5.5). Misconception M22 (领头幂次因子化可以直接推广到次领头幂次).
* *Budget of §24.7.* 1.6 pp.

#### §24.8 `sec:front-nlp-tmd`

* Text (0.1 pp): at NLP in $q_T/Q$ three kinds of corrections enter — kinematic (exact kinematics and frame dependence of the LP term), intrinsic/twist-3 (quark–gluon–quark correlators, "TMD twist") and dynamic (new hard functions); all results below are perturbative and assume, explicitly or implicitly, that Glauber exchanges do not spoil factorization at NLP.
* **猜想 `conj:front-glauber-nlp` — 次领头幂次 TMD 过程中 Glauber 交换的相消** [C].
  - *Statement.* In DY and SIDIS at next-to-leading power in $q_T/Q$, Glauber exchanges cancel to the extent needed for the NLP factorization formulas of `prop:front-tmd-nlp-operator` and `prop:front-sidis-nlp` to hold for hadronic cross sections (the analogue of the leading-power cancellation `lem:tmd-glauber`, [A]).
  - `\uses{def:tmd-kin, thm:tmd-factorization}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: assumed everywhere — explicitly by `Ebert:2021jhy` ("working under the assumption that leading power Glauber interactions do not spoil factorization at this order"), implicitly elsewhere; no all-order argument exists at NLP. The forward pointers to `prop:front-tmd-nlp-operator`, `prop:front-sidis-nlp` in the statement are text, not dependencies.
* **命题 `prop:front-tmd-nlp-operator` — TMD 算符展开与运动学幂次修正** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「以下陈述针对 TMD 算符展开本身（只含共线与软自由度）；它在强子截面中的有效性另需\cref{conj:front-glauber-nlp}。到 NLO 与 NLP，」 (a) TMD factorization follows at operator level with operators ordered by TMD twist (separating kinematic and genuine power corrections), process dependence entering through boundary conditions of background fields; the effective operator for the hadronic tensor is constructed to NLP ($\sim q_T/Q$) at NLO for any process with two detected states (`Vladimirov:2021hdn`, abstract); (b) twist-3 TMDs: definition, evolution equations (autonomous at large $N_c$), small-$b$ asymptotics, singularity structure, "physical" twist-3 TMDs (`Rodini:2022wki`, erratum, abstract); (c) each genuine term generates a series of kinematic power corrections with the same non-perturbative content; the series attached to the LP term is summed, restoring electromagnetic-current conservation and frame invariance (violated at fixed power), and gives a factorized form resembling the free-massless-quark hadronic tensor, checked at NLO (`Vladimirov:2023aot`, abstract); SIDIS version `Piloneta:2025jjb`; quasi-TMD version (link to Ch22 `thm:lamet-quasi-tmd`) `BrisCuerpo:2026pma` (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及算符展开；强子层次另需 `asm:intro-bridge` 与上述猜想。）
  - `\uses{thm:tmd-factorization, def:tmd-collins}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (NLO, NLP; abstract level). The pointer to `conj:front-glauber-nlp` in the first sentence is not a dependency (the statement is restricted to the Glauber-free operator expansion).
* **命题 `prop:front-sidis-nlp` — SIDIS 方位角不对称的次领头幂次 SCET 因子化** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在不含 Glauber 模式的 SCET 中（Glauber 相消见\cref{conj:front-glauber-nlp}），」 the power-suppressed hard-scattering effects in SIDIS azimuthal asymmetries factorize completely at NLP; TMDs depending on two longitudinal momentum fractions appear (one only beyond tree level); within the EFT only the leading-power soft function appears and is absorbed into the TMDs; one new hard coefficient enters (`Ebert:2021jhy`, erratum, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及有效理论中的因子化结构。）
  - `\uses{thm:tmd-factorization, def:tmd-soft, def:scet-modes, asm:front-scet-modes}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] within the stated restriction; the hadronic statement needs `conj:front-glauber-nlp` ([C]) (A25). (SM1) of `asm:front-scet-modes` is used; (SM2) is replaced by the restriction.
* **命题 `prop:front-nlp-tmd-oneloop` — 单圈次领头幂次系数函数与扭度 3 TMD 演化** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「在单圈阶，」 (a) all SIDIS structure functions at NLP with one-loop coefficient functions and kernels, and twist-3 TMD FFs with the LO kernel — with "several differences with earlier derivations", traced to the kinematics of quark–gluon interference terms (`Rodini:2023plb`, abstract); (b) kinematic, intrinsic and dynamic NLP pieces for DY and SIDIS, RG consistency at one loop, anomalous-dimension matrices mixing LP and NLP distributions, hard and soft functions different from LP, the rapidity anomalous dimension of the dynamic NLP distributions equal to the LP one (`Gamberg:2022lju`, 「（预印本）」, abstract); (c) DY at NLP in an EFT without explicit soft/collinear modes: power-suppressed operators $T_{(i,j)}$ giving the $O(q_T^2/q^2)$ coefficients of the usual PDFs, factorization for $q^2\gg q_T^2\gg\Lambda^2$, rapidity-divergence cancellation and overlap subtractions at NLP (`Inglis-Whalen:2021bea`, abstract); (d) Wilson-line approach: power corrections to TMD factorization (`Balitsky:2017flc`, `Balitsky:2017gis`) and the $1/Q^2$ corrections to the unpolarized DY tensor at large $N_c$ with electromagnetic gauge invariance (`Balitsky:2024ozy`) (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子系数函数与演化核。）
  - `\uses{prop:front-tmd-nlp-operator}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P] (one loop); the literature is not yet consistent — reported as the papers state it, no verdict (E34, Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**).
* **注 `rem:front-power-rapidity` — 次领头幂次的幂律快度发散** (no tag). 据该文摘要 (`Ebert:2018gsn`): at subleading power rapidity divergences can be power-law and produce derivatives of PDFs; a pure rapidity regulator and an $\overline{\rm MS}$-like scheme preserve the homogeneity of the power expansion; the complete $O(\alpha_s)$ $q_T^2/Q^2$ corrections for colour-singlet production are given. Open (OTHERS §6): NLP TMD factorization beyond one loop and non-perturbatively.
* *Budget of §24.8.* 0.8 pp.

#### §24.9 `sec:front-positivity`

* **命题 `prop:front-msbar-negative` — $\overline{\rm MS}$ 部分子分布可以为负** [V].
  - *Statement.* (a)（单圈 Yukawa 模型，\tagR，$R_{\rm PT}$）In the Yukawa theory $\mathcal L_{\rm int}=-\lambda\bar\Psi_N\psi_q\phi+{\rm h.c.}$ with all particles massive and weak coupling, the one-loop quark-in-"nucleon" $\overline{\rm MS}$ distribution is
    $$f^{\rm renorm}_{q/p}(\xi;\mu)=a_\lambda(\mu)(1-\xi)\Big[\ln\frac{\mu^2}{\Delta(\xi)}-1+\frac{(m_q+\xi m_p)^2}{\Delta(\xi)}\Big]+O(a_\lambda^2),\qquad\Delta(\xi)=\xi m_s^2+(1-\xi)m_q^2-\xi(1-\xi)m_p^2,\quad a_\lambda=\frac{\lambda^2}{16\pi^2},$$
    with $\Delta>0$ for a stable target ($m_p<m_q+m_s$); for every $\xi$ it is negative for small enough $\mu$ and positive for large $\mu$. For $m_q=0.3$, $m_p=1.0$, $m_s=1.5$ GeV: negative for $\xi>0.264$ at $\mu=0.8$ GeV, negative for $\xi>0.567$ at $\mu=1.2$ GeV, positive for all $\xi$ at $\mu=2.5$ GeV; at $\xi\to1$ the sign changes at $\mu_*=m_s\exp\{\frac12[1-(m_q+m_p)^2/m_s^2]\}=1.70$ GeV; at $\xi\to0$ the bracket tends to $\ln(\mu^2/m_q^2)$. (b) Hence 「$\overline{\rm MS}$ 部分子分布在所有标度上非负」 is false as a general theorem. `% VF-FRO-10` (use the exact thresholds 0.264, 0.567; WRITER_SPEC_v2 §6.11 item 7(c))
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题是 Yukawa 模型中的微扰陈述。）
  - `\uses{def:pdf-quark, def:prelim-msbar}`.
  - *Proof.* (a) full (0.3 pp): the DR integral of `CollinsRogersSato2022` eq. (58) with $\overline{\rm MS}$ subtraction gives the bracket and the counterterm $-a_\lambda(1-\xi)S_\varepsilon/\varepsilon$ (their eq. (57)); the $k_T$-cut-off version (eq. (59)), manifestly positive, agrees up to $O(m^2/\mu^2)$ (OTHERS `s2`; v2 check Q6 for the sign changes and $\mu_*$). Mechanism (CRS §VIII B): the $\overline{\rm MS}$ distribution equals the cut-off one minus $a_\lambda(1-\xi)\int dk_T^2/k_T^2$ over $[\mu^2,k^2_{\rm cut}]$; at low $\mu$ the $1/k_T^2$ asymptote over-subtracts the true small-$k_T$ behaviour.
  - *Status and gap.* [V], evidence: explicit perturbative counterexample (model). Dispute (E35, Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**; both sides, no verdict): `CandidoForteHekhorn2020` (abstract) argue that NLO $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs are positive "in the perturbative regime" (positive factorization scheme, then a scheme change small compared with LO); `CollinsRogersSato2022` (read): the argument fails at "bare PDFs are positive" — in "track B" (collinear divergences of massless partonic quantities regulated with $n>4$ and convoluted with bare hadronic PDFs) the UV-divergent contributions are positive for $n<4$ but "can and often do become negative after analytic continuation to $n>4$"; in "track A" (renormalized operator PDFs, partonic calculations only for hard coefficients) nothing in the definitions or the factorization theorems excludes negative $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs at low and moderate scales; `Candido:2023ujx` (read §§3–4): PDFs "always turn positive at high enough scale" and "turn negative at a low enough scale", that scale being non-perturbative. A physical example: $\overline{\rm MS}$ heavy-quark PDFs are negative just below the matching threshold (CRS §I). Over-claim to avoid: 「$\overline{\rm MS}$ PDF 是正的」. Core: `conj:pdf-positivity` (Ch8, [C]) cites this proposition in its status paragraph (§F5). Misconception M2.
  - *Budget.* 0.5 pp. Figure `fig:front-yukawa-pdf` (N17; tested starter code in FIGURES_INDEX_v2).
* **例 `ex:front-yukawa` — Yukawa 模型分布的端点行为与符号变化** (no tag). $\xi\to0$: $\Delta\to m_q^2$, $(m_q+\xi m_p)^2/\Delta\to1$, bracket $\to\ln(\mu^2/m_q^2)$ — negative iff $\mu<m_q$. $\xi\to1$: $\Delta\to m_s^2$, bracket $\to\ln(\mu^2/m_s^2)-1+(m_q+m_p)^2/m_s^2$, zero at $\mu_*^2=m_s^2e^{1-(m_q+m_p)^2/m_s^2}$; with the masses above $\mu_*=1.5\,e^{\frac12(1-1.69/2.25)}=1.70$ GeV. `\uses{prop:front-msbar-negative}`. 0.15 pp.
* **命题 `prop:front-positivity-large-scale` — 足够大标度上的正定性** [P].
  - *Statement.* Assume (PS1) leading-power collinear factorization for a set of positive observables that defines a "physical" scheme (e.g. DIS structure functions, `thm:pdf-dis-xspace`); (PS2) negligible higher twist; (PS3) perturbativity of the scheme transformation at NLO (`rem:pdf-schemes`). Then $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs are positive above a scale which, thanks to the Sudakov sign of the diagonal $O(\alpha_s)$ term ($\overline{\rm MS}$ over-subtracts collinear singularities, which in the diagonal channel have the Sudakov sign), is $x$-independent at large $x$; `Candido:2023ujx` estimate it conservatively as $Q^2\gtrsim5$ GeV$^2$ (from $\frac{\alpha_s}{2\pi}C_{gg}(x)<1$ at $x\sim0.3$, $\alpha_s\lesssim0.2$). CRS agree qualitatively ("once the $\overline{\rm MS}$ scale is large enough compared with intrinsic transverse momentum scales, the pdfs indeed become positive").
  - *Non-perturbative line.* 非微扰地位：\cref{asm:intro-wightman}、\cref{asm:intro-composite}、\cref{asm:intro-bridge}（MA0、MA1+MA2）。
  - `\uses{thm:pdf-dis-xspace, rem:pdf-schemes}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: an NLO argument under (PS1)–(PS3); higher orders and power corrections are not controlled; the threshold scale is non-perturbative.
* **注 `rem:front-positivity-tracks` — 对本书与拟合的后果** (no tag). (1) Positivity of $\overline{\rm MS}$ PDFs and Soffer-type bounds are never stated as theorems (`prop:coll-soffer`: only preservation by LO evolution); (2) positivity constraints in fits at low input scales are a theoretical bias (CRS); (3) a fixed-order factorized cross section can be negative without contradiction (CRS App. A); (4) positive schemes ($k_T$ cut-off, physical schemes) exist — explicitly at one loop in the model, by general arguments otherwise (in words, no tag).
* *Budget of §24.9.* 1.0 pp.

#### §24.10 `sec:front-brst`

* **注 `rem:front-k-gluon` — 为什么需要 BRST：K 胶子与非定域缺口** (no tag). In Feynman gauge the leading regions of DIS involve arbitrarily many longitudinally polarized ("K") gluons attached to the hard part; the diagrammatic non-abelian Ward-identity proof (`Labastida:1984gy`; `Collins2011` §11.9) works only in the strictly collinear limit, and the remainder is non-zero at leading power (pure K-gluon terms contribute to the gluon density through the gluons' transverse momenta; explicit calculation `Collins:2008sg`) — a non-abelian effect needed to build the gauge-invariant gluon PDF. Collins' BRST route (`Collins2011` §11.3.2, pp. 407–412, A13: check pages): the off-shell Green function $T^{\mu\nu}_{\rm off\text{-}shell}(q)=\frac1{4\pi}\int d^4z\,e^{iq\cdot z}\langle0|T\,{\rm fields}\,j^\mu(z/2)j^\nu(-z/2)|0\rangle$ (his (11.11)); leading regions give $\sum_iC_i\otimes\langle0|T\,{\rm fields}\,O_i|0\rangle$ (11.12) with a priori "arbitrarily complicated multilocal operators" $O_i$ on the light ray; BRST invariance of Green functions (11.13) and of the currents gives $\delta_{\rm BRST}\sum_iC_i\otimes O_i=0$ (11.17); for **local** operators BRST-invariant = gauge-invariant ⊕ (A) BRST variations ⊕ (B) equation-of-motion operators (`Joglekar:1975nu`; Joglekar 1977 and Nakanishi–Ojima 1990 as cited by Collins, 「文献待补」; as a theorem on local BRST cohomology `Henneaux:1993jn`, `Barnich:2000zw`; Ward identities and background-field gauge `Kluberg-Stern:1974iel`, `Kluberg-Stern:1974nmx`, `Kluberg-Stern:1975ebk`; first explicit gauge-variant counterterms `DixonTaylor1974`) — the core statement is `prop:appJ-local-operators` (App J, [R, R_PT]); classes A and B vanish in physical matrix elements. The gap, verbatim (p. 410): "Unfortunately, the published proofs that BRST-invariant operators are either gauge invariant or are in one of classes A or B apply as written to local operators. It is natural that the result also applies to the non-local operators we use in factorization. But, as far as I know, no proof has been given. For the purposes of the discussion, I will assume the result is true, and leave the proof (or refutation) to future research." Beyond one loop off-shell calculations need the A, B operators (`Hamberg:1991qt`, `Collins:1994ee`); all-loop structure of the gauge-variant counterterms (`Falcioni:2022fdm`, explicit basis to four loops; `Gehrmann:2023ksf`, three-loop singlet anomalous dimensions; abstracts). Light-cone gauge $A^+=0$ removes the K gluons (Collins: "given the known problems with $A^+=0$ gauge, it would be nice to have a proof that does not rely on the gauge"). Even granting gauge invariance, the path of the Wilson line is fixed by the remaining requirements (straight light-like for collinear PDFs; process-dependent staples for TMDs).
* **命题 `prop:front-brst-bilocal` — 双局域 PDF 算符 BRST 分类的矩约化** [R, R_PT] (planning tag; N04 pending).
  - *Statement.* （$R_{\mathrm{PT}}$：在重整化微扰论中对红外正规化的部分子态严格。）Let $O_q(w^-)=\bar\psi(w)\gamma^+W[w,0]\psi(0)$, $w=(0^+,w^-,\mathbf 0_T)$, and let $O_g(w^-)$ be the analogous gluon operator (`def:pdf-lightray`). In physical (on-shell, BRST-invariant) matrix elements, to every order of renormalized perturbation theory, $O^R=Z_{OO}\otimes O_{(0)}$ with
    $$\int_0^1dz\,z^{N-1}Z_{OO}(z)=Z^{(N)}_{\rm twist\text{-}2}\quad\text{for all }N,$$
    the admixtures of BRST-exact (class A) and equation-of-motion (class B) operators drop out, and $Z_{OO}$ is uniquely fixed by its moments (compact support; for the $C$-even/$C$-odd combinations $q\pm\bar q$ and for the gluon only even or odd $N$ occur, and uniqueness follows from `thm:appK-muntz`). Hence the triangular mixing (`Collins2011` eq. (11.20); VF-FRO-15)
    $$\begin{pmatrix}O\\A\\B\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}Z_{OO}&Z_{OA}&Z_{OB}\\0&Z_{AA}&Z_{AB}\\0&0&Z_{BB}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}O_{(0)}\\A_{(0)}\\B_{(0)}\end{pmatrix}$$
    restricted to physical matrix elements holds for the bilocal PDF operators. `% VF-FRO-15`
  - *Inputs in this book* (they replace the hypotheses of the frontier report, whose numbering is never printed): local classification for $\bar\psi\gamma^+(iD^+)^{N-1}\psi$ and $F^{+\mu}(iD^+)^{N-2}F^+{}_\mu$ — all indices "+", hence symmetric and traceless, exactly the twist-2 operators (`prop:appJ-local-operators`); support of the forward matrix elements in $[-1,1]$ order by order (`thm:pdf-support-pt`); the bare non-local operator is the generating function of the bare local ones (`prop:appL-bare`; exact for compact support, since the Fourier transform of a compactly supported distribution is entire with Taylor coefficients = moments) and the renormalized moment relation (`prop:appL-renormalized`).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及算符与部分子态。）
  - `\uses{def:pdf-lightray, prop:appJ-local-operators, thm:pdf-support-pt, prop:appL-bare, prop:appL-renormalized, thm:appK-muntz}`.
  - *Proof.* Full (0.4 pp): (1) moments of the bare bilocal operator = bare local twist-2 operators (`prop:appL-bare`); (2) local classification ⇒ in physical matrix elements only $Z^{(N)}_{\rm twist\text{-}2}$ survives (`prop:appJ-local-operators`); (3) compact support ⇒ the matrix element of the bilocal operator is determined by its moments (`thm:pdf-support-pt`); (4) define $Z_{OO}$ through its moments and prove uniqueness (`thm:appK-muntz`); (5) the renormalized moment relation closes the argument (`prop:appL-renormalized`). One-loop instance verified graph by graph (`research/c_pdf/DERIVATION.md` §5.4).
  - *Status and pending.* The frontier report grades this [A] (its support hypothesis is only [A] there); in this book all inputs are [R] or mathematics, so the planning tag is [R, R_PT]. Claim N04 (K1) **[PENDING-VERIFY]**: if verify does not confirm the proof — in particular if the App L proof of `prop:appL-renormalized` itself presupposes the classification for the non-local operator (circularity check) — the tag falls back to [A] and the statement is reported. No novelty word before the patch. What it does **not** cover: the classification step *inside* the factorization proof (multilocal operators with fields at different light-cone positions before gauge invariance is imposed; `conj:front-brst-multilocal`); TMD operators (staples, rapidity divergences); off-shell renormalization of the light-ray operators themselves (not needed for physical predictions).
* **猜想 `conj:front-brst-multilocal` — 多局域光线算符的 BRST 分类** [C].
  - *Statement.* Every BRST-invariant multilocal operator of ghost number zero, supported on a light-like line, of the type that appears in the leading-region decomposition of the off-shell Green function (`Collins2011` (11.12)), is the sum of a gauge-invariant operator, a BRST variation (class A) and an operator that vanishes by the equations of motion (class B).
  - `\uses{def:prelim-brst, prop:appJ-local-operators}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [C]: assumed by Collins (quote in `rem:front-k-gluon`); wording of the literature status only as 「据我们截至 2026 年 10 月的文献检索（INSPIRE 与网络检索），尚未见非定域（多局域）BRST 分类的证明」 (A06 **[PENDING-VERIFY]**). Natural tool: localization by an auxiliary field (the Wilson line as a field), as in the renormalizability proofs for space-like Wilson-line operators (`Ji:2017oey`, `Ishikawa:2017faj`, `Green:2017xeu`; Ch22 `prop:lattice-renormalizability`, `asm:lamet-aux-renorm`, pointers). Over-claim to avoid: 「Collins (2011) 证明了非定域算符的 BRST 分类」. Coordination: App J `rem:appJ-nonlocal` ([A] in v1) should point here and to `prop:front-brst-bilocal` (§F5).
* *Budget of §24.10.* 1.2 pp.

#### §24.11 `sec:front-smallx`

* Framing (text, 0.1 pp): small-$x$ "factorization" is a different expansion from the collinear one — the Regge limit $s\gg|t|$ (DIS: $x\to0$ at fixed $Q^2$), organized by rapidity factorization of Wilson lines (shockwave picture): projectile impact factors ⊗ target Wilson-line correlators evolved in rapidity (BK/JIMWLK). Its results are all-order statements within the eikonal (Regge) expansion at fixed logarithmic accuracy, not leading-power theorems with power-counting control; the grades below are relative to that framework. The LO BK equation (VF-FRO-12 = VF-MISC-4) is stated in Ch17 (`thm:smallx-bk`), not here.
* **命题 `prop:front-nlo-bk` — NLO BK/JIMWLK 与三圈 BK 核** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在 eikonal（Regge）展开的次领头对数精度上，」 (a) the NLO evolution of colour dipoles contains quadratic and cubic non-linearities; the NLO kernel is the sum of a running-coupling part ($\propto\beta_0$), a conformal part ($1\to3$ dipole transitions) and a non-conformal double-logarithmic term; its forward linearization agrees with NLO BFKL up to a term $\propto\alpha_s^2\zeta(3)\times$(the original dipole), attributed by the authors to different longitudinal cutoffs (`Balitsky:2007feb`, abstract, §VIII checked; quark part and running coupling `Balitsky:2006wa`, `Kovchegov:2006vj`); (b) NLO JIMWLK (`Balitsky:2013fea`, `Kovner:2013ona`; conformal symmetry `Kovner:2014xia`; "unabridged" `Kovner:2014lca`; abstracts); (c) the three-loop BK kernel in planar $\mathcal N=4$ SYM via the equivalence with non-global logarithms, with a Lorentz-covariant IR/collinear subtraction method and checks against the Pomeron trajectory and the twist-2 spectrum (`Caron-Huot:2016tzz`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题是关于 Wilson 线算符的演化方程。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bk, thm:smallx-bfkl}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] within the eikonal framework; the $\zeta(3)$ difference is reported as the paper states it (E34, Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**).
* **命题 `prop:front-bk-collinear` — NLO BK 的不稳定性与共线重求和** [P].
  - *Statement.* 「对现实的初始条件，」 the NLO BK solution is unstable (`Lappi:2015fma`); kinematic constraints and collinear resummation cure it (`Beuf:2014uia`, `Iancu:2015vea`) (abstracts).
  - `\uses{prop:front-nlo-bk}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [P]: the resummation prescriptions differ beyond the controlled logarithms; a scheme-independent collinear improvement is open (`rem:front-open`).
* **命题 `prop:front-regge` — 高能极限中的 Wilson 线方法、胶子 Regge 化与 Regge 极限的红外奇异部分** [A].
  - *Statement.* (a) The eikonal approximation with rapidity-dependent collections of Wilson lines (Balitsky–JIMWLK) reproduces Reggeization and BFKL; from four loops BFKL dynamics is incompatible with the dipole formula for IR divergences; in the strict planar limit the theory can be formulated with a finite, process-dependent number of Wilson lines without reference to a reggeized gluon, checked exactly in $\mathcal N=4$ (`Caron-Huot:2013fea`, abstract, §4 checked). (b) The IR-singular part of $2\to2$ amplitudes in the high-energy limit is given in closed form to all orders at NLL (two-Reggeon contribution), predicting the Regge limit of the soft anomalous dimension at any loop order (`Caron-Huot:2017zfo`, abstract).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （本命题只涉及部分子振幅。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bfkl, thm:sudakov-ir-poles}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A].
* **命题 `prop:front-regge-pole` — 简单 Regge 极点因子化在 NNLL 失效** [V].
  - *Statement.* Using IR factorization, the simplest Regge-pole factorization of $2\to2$ amplitudes fails at NNLL in $\ln(s/|t|)$: a two-loop non-logarithmic double pole and non-factorizing single-logarithmic singular terms at three loops; the hard functions vanish in $d=4$ to all orders up to NLL (`DelDuca:2013ara`, `DelDuca:2014cya`, 「据该文摘要」).
  - `\uses{def:smallx-regge, thm:sudakov-ir-poles}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [V] for Regge-pole factorization beyond NLL, evidence: explicit calculation based on IR factorization ([A], `thm:sudakov-ir-poles`); the corrected multi-Reggeon picture is [A] (`prop:front-regge`(b)).
* **命题 `prop:front-smallx-tmd` — 小 $x$ 的 TMD 与 Sudakov 对数** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在色玻璃凝聚框架的领头阶与领头对数精度上，」 (a) effective $k_t$-factorization for dijets in dilute–dense collisions; the Weizsäcker–Williams and dipole gluon distributions are the universal small-$x$ TMDs, with process-dependent gauge links at small $x$ (`Dominguez:2011wm`); (b) Sudakov double logarithms combined with small-$x$ resummation in the saturation formalism (`Mueller:2012uf`) (abstracts). (c)（部分结果，\tagP）rapidity evolution of gluon TMDs from low to moderate $x$ (`Balitsky:2015qba`); gauge-invariant TMD factorization at small $x$, rapidity-only factorization, Sudakov–BFKL interpolation (`Balitsky:2020jzt`, `Balitsky:2021fer`, `Balitsky:2023hmh`, `Balitsky:2026lop`) (abstracts).
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （高能展开框架内的陈述；靶的非微扰信息作为初始条件输入。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bk}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (LO/LL) for (a), (b); (c) [P].
* **命题 `prop:front-smallx-helicity` — 小 $x$ 螺旋度演化：截距与 BER 的分歧** [A].
  - *Statement.* 「在双对数近似与大 $N_c$（或大 $N_c\&N_f$）下（据该文摘要），」 helicity evolution at small $x$ (double logarithms $\alpha_s\ln^2(1/x)$) follows from polarized dipole amplitudes, i.e. sub-eikonal Wilson-line operators (`Kovchegov:2015pbl`, erratum; `Kovchegov:2016weo`), in the revised form of `Cougoulic:2022gbk`; its exact large-$N_c$ solution gives $\Delta\Sigma\sim\Delta G\sim g_1\sim(1/x)^{\alpha_h}$ with
    $$\alpha_h\simeq3.66074\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_sN_c}{2\pi}}\quad(\texttt{Borden:2023ugd}),\qquad\alpha_h^{\rm BER}\simeq3.66394\sqrt{\frac{\alpha_sN_c}{2\pi}}\quad(\text{infrared evolution equations, }\texttt{Bartels:1996wc});$$
    the resummed $\Delta\gamma_{GG}(\omega)$ agrees with the fixed-order anomalous dimensions through three loops and differs from the Bartels–Ermolaev–Ryskin result (`Bartels:1995iu`, `Bartels:1996wc`) from four loops on; large $N_c\&N_f$: `Adamiak:2023okq`, `Borden:2024bxa` (quark↔gluon transition operators), `Borden:2025ehe` (analytic solution; a slight disagreement with BER and with Blümlein–Vogt `Blumlein:1996hb` persists). `% VF-FRO-17` (in the book the formula is typeset with the keys as `\cite`, not `\texttt`)
  - *Non-perturbative line.* （高能展开框架内的陈述。）
  - `\uses{thm:smallx-bk, def:pdf-polarized}`.
  - *Status and gap.* [A] (double-logarithmic approximation, large $N_c$ or large $N_c\&N_f$); the disagreement with BER is an open problem, printed without a verdict (E34(iv), Q3, **[PENDING-VERIFY]**). Related (abstracts): gluon orbital angular momentum at small $x$ (`Hatta:2016aoc`), small-$x$ evolution of the gluon GPD $E_g$ (`Hatta:2022bxn`) — [A]/[P] in words.
* *Budget of §24.11.* 1.2 pp.

#### §24.12 `sec:front-dps`

* Text (0.1 pp): two hard scatterings in one $pp$ collision (e.g. double Drell–Yan); formally power suppressed for integrated cross sections but leading for transverse-momentum-differential ones ($q_{T,i}\ll Q_i$) and enhanced for many final states at small $x$. Framework: colour-correlated DPDs, power counting, elements of an all-order proof, links to GPDs and the lattice (`DiehlOstermeierSchafer2012`, erratum); colour correlations, interference, evolution, the $1\to2$ splitting singularity ($\propto1/y^2$ at small separation $y$) and the double-counting problem (`Manohar:2012jr`, `Manohar:2012pe`) (abstracts).
